# 中华人民共和国民法典

**分类**：法律  
**法律部门**：民法典  
**公布日期**：2020-05-28  
**生效日期**：2021-01-01

> （2020年5月28日第十三届全国人民代表大会第三次会议通过）

---

## 第一编　总则

### 第一章　基本规定

<a id="art-1"></a>第一条　为了保护民事主体的合法权益，调整民事关系，维护社会和经济秩序，适应中国特色社会主义发展要求，弘扬社会主义核心价值观，根据宪法，制定本法。

**Article 1** This Law is enacted in accordance with the Constitution for the purposes of protecting the lawful rights and interests of civil subjects, regulating civil relations, maintaining social and economic order, meeting the requirements for the development of socialism with Chinese characteristics, and promoting the core socialist values.

<a id="art-2"></a>第二条　民法调整平等主体的自然人、法人和非法人组织之间的人身关系和财产关系。

**Article 2** The civil law regulates personal and property relations among natural persons, legal persons, and unincorporated organizations that are equal subjects.

<a id="art-3"></a>第三条　民事主体的人身权利、财产权利以及其他合法权益受法律保护，任何组织或者个人不得侵犯。

**Article 3** The personal rights, property rights, and other lawful rights and interests of civil subjects shall be protected by law, and no organization or individual shall infringe upon them.

<a id="art-4"></a>第四条　民事主体在民事活动中的法律地位一律平等。

**Article 4** All civil subjects have equal legal status in civil activities.

<a id="art-5"></a>第五条　民事主体从事民事活动，应当遵循自愿原则，按照自己的意思设立、变更、终止民事法律关系。

**Article 5** In engaging in civil activities, civil subjects shall follow the principle of voluntariness, establishing, modifying, or terminating civil legal relations according to their own will.

<a id="art-6"></a>第六条　民事主体从事民事活动，应当遵循公平原则，合理确定各方的权利和义务。

**Article 6** In engaging in civil activities, civil subjects shall follow the principle of fairness, reasonably determining the rights and obligations of each party.

<a id="art-7"></a>第七条　民事主体从事民事活动，应当遵循诚信原则，秉持诚实，恪守承诺。

**Article 7** In engaging in civil activities, civil subjects shall follow the principle of good faith, acting honestly and honoring their commitments.

<a id="art-8"></a>第八条　民事主体从事民事活动，不得违反法律，不得违背公序良俗。

**Article 8** In engaging in civil activities, civil subjects shall not violate the law or contravene public order and good customs.

<a id="art-9"></a>第九条　民事主体从事民事活动，应当有利于节约资源、保护生态环境。

**Article 9** In engaging in civil activities, civil subjects shall act in a manner conducive to conserving resources and protecting the ecological environment.

<a id="art-10"></a>第十条　处理民事纠纷，应当依照法律；法律没有规定的，可以适用习惯，但是不得违背公序良俗。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 10** In handling civil disputes, the law shall be applied. Where no provision exists in the law, customs may be applied, provided that they do not contravene public order and good customs.

<a id="art-11"></a>第十一条　其他法律对民事关系有特别规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 11** Where other laws contain special provisions on civil relations, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-12"></a>第十二条　中华人民共和国领域内的民事活动，适用中华人民共和国法律。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 12** Civil activities within the territory of the People's Republic of China shall be governed by the laws of the People's Republic of China. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

### 第二章　自然人

#### 第一节　民事权利能力和民事行为能力

<a id="art-13"></a>第十三条　自然人从出生时起到死亡时止，具有民事权利能力，依法享有民事权利，承担民事义务。

**Article 13** A natural person shall have capacity for civil rights from the time of birth until death, and shall, in accordance with the law, enjoy civil rights and assume civil obligations.

<a id="art-14"></a>第十四条　自然人的民事权利能力一律平等。

**Article 14** All natural persons have equal capacity for civil rights.

<a id="art-15"></a>第十五条　自然人的出生时间和死亡时间，以出生证明、死亡证明记载的时间为准；没有出生证明、死亡证明的，以户籍登记或者其他有效身份登记记载的时间为准。有其他证据足以推翻以上记载时间的，以该证据证明的时间为准。

**Article 15** The time of birth and time of death of a natural person shall be determined based on the time recorded on the birth certificate or death certificate. In the absence of such certificates, the time recorded on the household registration or other valid identity registration shall prevail. Where other evidence is sufficient to overturn the recorded time, the time proved by such evidence shall prevail.

<a id="art-16"></a>第十六条　涉及遗产继承、接受赠与等胎儿利益保护的，胎儿视为具有民事权利能力。但是，胎儿娩出时为死体的，其民事权利能力自始不存在。

**Article 16** In matters involving the protection of the interests of a fetus, such as inheritance of estate or acceptance of gifts, the fetus shall be deemed to have capacity for civil rights. However, if the fetus is stillborn, its capacity for civil rights shall be deemed never to have existed.

<a id="art-17"></a>第十七条　十八周岁以上的自然人为成年人。不满十八周岁的自然人为未成年人。

**Article 17** A natural person who has attained the age of 18 is an adult. A natural person under the age of 18 is a minor.

<a id="art-18"></a>第十八条　成年人为完全民事行为能力人，可以独立实施民事法律行为。  

**Article 18** An adult is a person with full capacity for civil conduct and may independently perform civil legal acts.
A minor who has attained the age of 16 and whose main source of livelihood is his or her own labor income shall be deemed a person with full capacity for civil conduct.
十六周岁以上的未成年人，以自己的劳动收入为主要生活来源的，视为完全民事行为能力人。

<a id="art-19"></a>第十九条　八周岁以上的未成年人为限制民事行为能力人，实施民事法律行为由其法定代理人代理或者经其法定代理人同意、追认；但是，可以独立实施纯获利益的民事法律行为或者与其年龄、智力相适应的民事法律行为。

**Article 19** A minor who has attained the age of 8 is a person with limited capacity for civil conduct, and the performance of civil legal acts shall be conducted by his or her legal representative or be subject to the consent or ratification of the legal representative. However, such a minor may independently perform civil legal acts that are purely beneficial or that are appropriate to his or her age and intelligence.

<a id="art-20"></a>第二十条　不满八周岁的未成年人为无民事行为能力人，由其法定代理人代理实施民事法律行为。

**Article 20** A minor under the age of 8 is a person with no capacity for civil conduct, and the performance of civil legal acts shall be conducted by his or her legal representative.

<a id="art-21"></a>第二十一条　不能辨认自己行为的成年人为无民事行为能力人，由其法定代理人代理实施民事法律行为。  

**Article 21** An adult who is unable to discern his or her own conduct is a person with no capacity for civil conduct, and his or her legal representative shall act for him or her in performing civil juristic acts. If a minor aged eight or above is unable to discern his or her own conduct, the provisions of the preceding paragraph shall apply.
八周岁以上的未成年人不能辨认自己行为的，适用前款规定。

<a id="art-22"></a>第二十二条　不能完全辨认自己行为的成年人为限制民事行为能力人，实施民事法律行为由其法定代理人代理或者经其法定代理人同意、追认；但是，可以独立实施纯获利益的民事法律行为或者与其智力、精神健康状况相适应的民事法律行为。

**Article 22** An adult who is unable to fully discern his or her own conduct is a person with limited capacity for civil conduct, and his or her legal representative shall act for him or her in performing civil juristic acts or consent to or ratify such acts; provided that he or she may independently perform civil juristic acts that are purely for his or her benefit or that are appropriate to his or her intellectual or mental health condition.

<a id="art-23"></a>第二十三条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人的监护人是其法定代理人。

**Article 23** The guardian of a person with no capacity for civil conduct or a person with limited capacity for civil conduct is his or her legal representative.

<a id="art-24"></a>第二十四条　不能辨认或者不能完全辨认自己行为的成年人，其利害关系人或者有关组织，可以向人民法院申请认定该成年人为无民事行为能力人或者限制民事行为能力人。  

**Article 24** An interested party or relevant organization may apply to the People's Court for determining an adult who is unable to discern or fully discern his or her own conduct as a person with no capacity for civil conduct or a person with limited capacity for civil conduct. Where a person has been determined by the People's Court as a person with no capacity for civil conduct or a person with limited capacity for civil conduct, upon application by the person himself or herself, an interested party, or a relevant organization, the People's Court may, based on the recovery of his or her intellectual or mental health, determine the adult to be restored to a person with limited capacity for civil conduct or a person with full capacity for civil conduct. The relevant organizations specified in this Article include: residents committees, villagers committees, schools, medical institutions, women's federations, disabled persons' federations, lawfully established elderly organizations, civil affairs departments, etc.
被人民法院认定为无民事行为能力人或者限制民事行为能力人的，经本人、利害关系人或者有关组织申请，人民法院可以根据其智力、精神健康恢复的状况，认定该成年人恢复为限制民事行为能力人或者完全民事行为能力人。  
本条规定的有关组织包括：居民委员会、村民委员会、学校、医疗机构、妇女联合会、残疾人联合会、依法设立的老年人组织、民政部门等。

<a id="art-25"></a>第二十五条　自然人以户籍登记或者其他有效身份登记记载的居所为住所；经常居所与住所不一致的，经常居所视为住所。

**Article 25** A natural person shall take the residence recorded in the domicile registration or other valid identity registration as his or her domicile; if the habitual residence is inconsistent with the domicile, the habitual residence shall be deemed the domicile.

#### 第二节　监护

<a id="art-26"></a>第二十六条　父母对未成年子女负有抚养、教育和保护的义务。  

**Article 26** Parents have the duty to raise, educate, and protect their minor children. Adult children have the duty to support, assist, and protect their parents.
成年子女对父母负有赡养、扶助和保护的义务。

<a id="art-27"></a>第二十七条　父母是未成年子女的监护人。  

**Article 27** Parents are the guardians of their minor children. Where the parents of a minor are deceased or lack the capacity to serve as guardians, the following persons who have the capacity to serve as guardians shall act as guardians in the following order: (1) paternal grandparents and maternal grandparents; (2) elder brothers and sisters; and (3) other individuals or organizations willing to serve as guardians, subject to the consent of the residents committee, villagers committee, or civil affairs department in the place of the minor's domicile.
未成年人的父母已经死亡或者没有监护能力的，由下列有监护能力的人按顺序担任监护人：  
（一）祖父母、外祖父母；  
（二）兄、姐；  
（三）其他愿意担任监护人的个人或者组织，但是须经未成年人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门同意。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-83" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第83条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》侵权责任编的解释（一）.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》侵权责任编的解释（一）》第7条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第7条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第8条引用">[4]</a></sup>

<a id="art-28"></a>第二十八条　无民事行为能力或者限制民事行为能力的成年人，由下列有监护能力的人按顺序担任监护人：  

**Article 28** For an adult with no capacity for civil conduct or with limited capacity for civil conduct, the following persons who have the capacity to serve as guardians shall act as guardians in the following order: (1) spouse; (2) parents and children; (3) other close relatives; and (4) other individuals or organizations willing to serve as guardians, subject to the consent of the residents committee, villagers committee, or civil affairs department in the place of the ward's domicile.
（一）配偶；  
（二）父母、子女；  
（三）其他近亲属；  
（四）其他愿意担任监护人的个人或者组织，但是须经被监护人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门同意。

<a id="art-29"></a>第二十九条　被监护人的父母担任监护人的，可以通过遗嘱指定监护人。

**Article 29** Where the parents of a ward serve as guardians, they may appoint a guardian by will.

<a id="art-30"></a>第三十条　依法具有监护资格的人之间可以协议确定监护人。协议确定监护人应当尊重被监护人的真实意愿。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第8条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 30** Persons legally qualified to serve as guardians may determine the guardian by agreement. The determination of a guardian by agreement shall respect the genuine will of the ward.

<a id="art-31"></a>第三十一条　对监护人的确定有争议的，由被监护人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门指定监护人，有关当事人对指定不服的，可以向人民法院申请指定监护人；有关当事人也可以直接向人民法院申请指定监护人。  

**Article 31** Where there is a dispute over the determination of a guardian, the residents committee, villagers committee, or civil affairs department in the place of the ward's domicile shall designate a guardian. If an interested party disagrees with the designation, it may apply to the People's Court for the designation of a guardian; the interested party may also directly apply to the People's Court for the designation of a guardian. The residents committee, villagers committee, civil affairs department, or the People's Court shall respect the genuine will of the ward and designate a guardian from among persons legally qualified to serve as guardians in accordance with the principle of best interests of the ward. Before the designation of a guardian in accordance with the provisions of the first paragraph of this Article, where the personal rights, property rights, and other lawful rights and interests of the ward are left unprotected, the residents committee, villagers committee, relevant organization prescribed by law, or civil affairs department in the place of the ward's domicile shall serve as the provisional guardian. Once a guardian is designated, no unauthorized change shall be made; an unauthorized change shall not relieve the designated guardian of his or her responsibility.
居民委员会、村民委员会、民政部门或者人民法院应当尊重被监护人的真实意愿，按照最有利于被监护人的原则在依法具有监护资格的人中指定监护人。  
依据本条第一款规定指定监护人前，被监护人的人身权利、财产权利以及其他合法权益处于无人保护状态的，由被监护人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会、法律规定的有关组织或者民政部门担任临时监护人。  
监护人被指定后，不得擅自变更；擅自变更的，不免除被指定的监护人的责任。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-349" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第349条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-32"></a>第三十二条　没有依法具有监护资格的人的，监护人由民政部门担任，也可以由具备履行监护职责条件的被监护人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会担任。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-83" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第83条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 32** Where there is no person legally qualified to serve as a guardian, the guardian shall be the civil affairs department, or alternatively, the residents committee or villagers committee in the place of the ward's domicile, provided that the committee meets the conditions for fulfilling the responsibilities of guardianship.

<a id="art-33"></a>第三十三条　具有完全民事行为能力的成年人，可以与其近亲属、其他愿意担任监护人的个人或者组织事先协商，以书面形式确定自己的监护人，在自己丧失或者部分丧失民事行为能力时，由该监护人履行监护职责。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-11" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第11条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 33** An adult with full capacity for civil conduct may, through prior consultation with his or her close relatives or other individuals or organizations willing to serve as guardians, determine his or her own guardian in writing, and when the adult loses or partially loses capacity for civil conduct, the guardian shall fulfill the responsibilities of guardianship.

<a id="art-34"></a>第三十四条　监护人的职责是代理被监护人实施民事法律行为，保护被监护人的人身权利、财产权利以及其他合法权益等。  

**Article 34** The duties of a guardian are to act for the ward in performing civil juristic acts and to protect the personal rights, property rights, and other lawful rights and interests of the ward. Rights arising from the guardian's fulfillment of guardianship duties according to law shall be protected by law. A guardian who fails to fulfill the duties of guardianship or infringes upon the lawful rights and interests of the ward shall bear legal liability. Where, due to an emergency such as the occurrence of a sudden incident, the guardian is temporarily unable to fulfill the duties of guardianship, and the ward's life is left unattended, the residents committee, villagers committee, or civil affairs department in the place of the ward's domicile shall arrange necessary temporary living care measures for the ward.
监护人依法履行监护职责产生的权利，受法律保护。  
监护人不履行监护职责或者侵害被监护人合法权益的，应当承担法律责任。  
因发生突发事件等紧急情况，监护人暂时无法履行监护职责，被监护人的生活处于无人照料状态的，被监护人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门应当为被监护人安排必要的临时生活照料措施。

<a id="art-35"></a>第三十五条　监护人应当按照最有利于被监护人的原则履行监护职责。监护人除为维护被监护人利益外，不得处分被监护人的财产。  

**Article 35** A guardian shall fulfill the duties of guardianship in accordance with the principle of best interests of the ward. Except for the purpose of protecting the interests of the ward, the guardian shall not dispose of the property of the ward. When fulfilling the duties of guardianship, the guardian of a minor shall, in making decisions relating to the interests of the ward, respect the genuine will of the ward based on the ward's age and intellectual capacity. When fulfilling the duties of guardianship, the guardian of an adult shall, to the greatest extent possible, respect the genuine will of the ward, guarantee and assist the ward in performing civil juristic acts appropriate to his or her intellectual or mental health condition. The guardian shall not interfere in matters that the ward is capable of handling independently.
未成年人的监护人履行监护职责，在作出与被监护人利益有关的决定时，应当根据被监护人的年龄和智力状况，尊重被监护人的真实意愿。  
成年人的监护人履行监护职责，应当最大程度地尊重被监护人的真实意愿，保障并协助被监护人实施与其智力、精神健康状况相适应的民事法律行为。对被监护人有能力独立处理的事务，监护人不得干涉。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-15" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第15条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-36"></a>第三十六条　监护人有下列情形之一的，人民法院根据有关个人或者组织的申请，撤销其监护人资格，安排必要的临时监护措施，并按照最有利于被监护人的原则依法指定监护人：  

**Article 36** Where a guardian falls under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall, upon application by a relevant individual or organization, disqualify the guardian, arrange necessary provisional guardianship measures, and designate a guardian according to law in accordance with the principle of best interests of the ward: (1) committing acts that seriously harm the physical or mental health of the ward; (2) neglecting the duties of guardianship, or being unable to fulfill the duties of guardianship and refusing to delegate part or all of the duties of guardianship to another person, thereby leaving the ward in distress or danger; or (3) committing other acts that seriously infringe upon the lawful rights and interests of the ward. The relevant individuals and organizations specified in this Article include: other persons legally qualified to serve as guardians, residents committees, villagers committees, schools, medical institutions, women's federations, disabled persons' federations, organizations for the protection of minors, lawfully established elderly organizations, civil affairs departments, etc. Where an individual or an organization other than the civil affairs department specified in the preceding paragraph fails to apply to the People's Court in a timely manner for disqualification of a guardian, the civil affairs department shall apply to the People's Court.
（一）实施严重损害被监护人身心健康的行为；  
（二）怠于履行监护职责，或者无法履行监护职责且拒绝将监护职责部分或者全部委托给他人，导致被监护人处于危困状态；  
（三）实施严重侵害被监护人合法权益的其他行为。  
本条规定的有关个人、组织包括：其他依法具有监护资格的人，居民委员会、村民委员会、学校、医疗机构、妇女联合会、残疾人联合会、未成年人保护组织、依法设立的老年人组织、民政部门等。  
前款规定的个人和民政部门以外的组织未及时向人民法院申请撤销监护人资格的，民政部门应当向人民法院申请。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-62" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第62条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-11" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第11条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-37"></a>第三十七条　依法负担被监护人抚养费、赡养费、扶养费的父母、子女、配偶等，被人民法院撤销监护人资格后，应当继续履行负担的义务。

**Article 37** Parents, children, spouses, or others who are legally obligated to pay child support, alimony, or maintenance to the ward shall continue to fulfill such obligations after being disqualified as guardians by the People's Court.

<a id="art-38"></a>第三十八条　被监护人的父母或者子女被人民法院撤销监护人资格后，除对被监护人实施故意犯罪的外，确有悔改表现的，经其申请，人民法院可以在尊重被监护人真实意愿的前提下，视情况恢复其监护人资格，人民法院指定的监护人与被监护人的监护关系同时终止。

**Article 38** Where a ward's parent or child has been disqualified as a guardian by the People's Court, if he or she shows genuine repentance (except in cases where he or she commits an intentional crime against the ward), upon his or her application, the People's Court may, subject to respecting the genuine will of the ward and depending on the circumstances, restore his or her qualification as a guardian, and the guardianship relationship between the guardian designated by the People's Court and the ward shall terminate simultaneously.

<a id="art-39"></a>第三十九条　有下列情形之一的，监护关系终止：  

**Article 39** The guardianship relationship shall terminate under any of the following circumstances: (1) the ward acquires or regains full capacity for civil conduct; (2) the guardian loses the capacity for guardianship; (3) the ward or the guardian dies; or (4) other circumstances under which the People's Court determines that the guardianship relationship shall terminate. After the termination of the guardianship relationship, if the ward still needs guardianship, another guardian shall be determined according to law.
（一）被监护人取得或者恢复完全民事行为能力；  
（二）监护人丧失监护能力；  
（三）被监护人或者监护人死亡；  
（四）人民法院认定监护关系终止的其他情形。  
监护关系终止后，被监护人仍然需要监护的，应当依法另行确定监护人。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-12" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第12条引用">[1]</a></sup>

#### 第三节　宣告失踪和宣告死亡

<a id="art-40"></a>第四十条　自然人下落不明满二年的，利害关系人可以向人民法院申请宣告该自然人为失踪人。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-14" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第14条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 40** Where a natural person has been missing for two full years, an interested party may apply to the People's Court for a declaration of the natural person as a missing person.

<a id="art-41"></a>第四十一条　自然人下落不明的时间自其失去音讯之日起计算。战争期间下落不明的，下落不明的时间自战争结束之日或者有关机关确定的下落不明之日起计算。

**Article 41** The period of disappearance of a natural person shall be calculated from the date on which he or she is last heard from. If a person disappears during a war, the period of disappearance shall be calculated from the date on which the war ends or from the date determined by the relevant authority as the date of disappearance.

<a id="art-42"></a>第四十二条　失踪人的财产由其配偶、成年子女、父母或者其他愿意担任财产代管人的人代管。  

**Article 42** The property of a missing person shall be managed by his or her spouse, adult children, parents, or any other person willing to serve as the property custodian. If a dispute arises over the custodianship, if none of the persons specified in the preceding paragraph exist, or if the persons specified in the preceding paragraph lack the capacity to serve as custodian, the property shall be managed by a person designated by the People's Court.
代管有争议，没有前款规定的人，或者前款规定的人无代管能力的，由人民法院指定的人代管。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-341" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第341条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-43"></a>第四十三条　财产代管人应当妥善管理失踪人的财产，维护其财产权益。  

**Article 43** The property custodian shall properly manage the missing person's property and protect his or her property rights and interests. Any taxes, debts, or other expenses owed by the missing person shall be paid by the property custodian from the missing person's property. If the property custodian causes damage to the missing person's property through intentional conduct or gross negligence, he or she shall bear compensation liability.
失踪人所欠税款、债务和应付的其他费用，由财产代管人从失踪人的财产中支付。  
财产代管人因故意或者重大过失造成失踪人财产损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

<a id="art-44"></a>第四十四条　财产代管人不履行代管职责、侵害失踪人财产权益或者丧失代管能力的，失踪人的利害关系人可以向人民法院申请变更财产代管人。  

**Article 44** If the property custodian fails to perform his or her custodial duties, infringes upon the property rights and interests of the missing person, or loses the capacity to serve as custodian, any interested party of the missing person may apply to the People's Court for the replacement of the property custodian. If the property custodian has justifiable reasons, he or she may apply to the People's Court for replacement. If the People's Court replaces the property custodian, the new property custodian shall have the right to request the original property custodian to promptly transfer the relevant property and report on the status of the property management.
财产代管人有正当理由的，可以向人民法院申请变更财产代管人。  
人民法院变更财产代管人的，变更后的财产代管人有权请求原财产代管人及时移交有关财产并报告财产代管情况。

<a id="art-45"></a>第四十五条　失踪人重新出现，经本人或者利害关系人申请，人民法院应当撤销失踪宣告。  

**Article 45** If the missing person reappears, the People's Court shall revoke the declaration of missing person upon application by the person himself or herself or by any interested party. Upon reappearance, the missing person shall have the right to request the property custodian to promptly transfer the relevant property and report on the status of the property management.
失踪人重新出现，有权请求财产代管人及时移交有关财产并报告财产代管情况。

<a id="art-46"></a>第四十六条　自然人有下列情形之一的，利害关系人可以向人民法院申请宣告该自然人死亡：  

**Article 46** An interested party may apply to the People's Court for declaring a natural person deceased under any of the following circumstances: (1) the natural person has been missing for four years; (2) the natural person has been missing for two years due to an accident. If a natural person is missing due to an accident and it is proven by the relevant authority that he or she is unlikely to survive, the application for declaration of death shall not be subject to the two-year time limit.
（一）下落不明满四年；  
（二）因意外事件，下落不明满二年。  
因意外事件下落不明，经有关机关证明该自然人不可能生存的，申请宣告死亡不受二年时间的限制。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-16" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第16条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第17条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-47"></a>第四十七条　对同一自然人，有的利害关系人申请宣告死亡，有的利害关系人申请宣告失踪，符合本法规定的宣告死亡条件的，人民法院应当宣告死亡。

**Article 47** With respect to the same natural person, if some interested parties apply for a declaration of death and others apply for a declaration of disappearance, and the conditions for a declaration of death as provided for in this Law are met, the People's Court shall declare the death.

<a id="art-48"></a>第四十八条　被宣告死亡的人，人民法院宣告死亡的判决作出之日视为其死亡的日期；因意外事件下落不明宣告死亡的，意外事件发生之日视为其死亡的日期。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》继承编的解释（一）》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 48** For a person declared dead, the date on which the People's Court renders the judgment declaring death shall be deemed the date of his or her death; if the declaration of death is made due to disappearance caused by an accident, the date on which the accident occurred shall be deemed the date of his or her death.

<a id="art-49"></a>第四十九条　自然人被宣告死亡但是并未死亡的，不影响该自然人在被宣告死亡期间实施的民事法律行为的效力。

**Article 49** If a natural person is declared dead but is not actually deceased, the validity of the civil juristic acts performed by that natural person during the period of being declared dead shall not be affected.

<a id="art-50"></a>第五十条　被宣告死亡的人重新出现，经本人或者利害关系人申请，人民法院应当撤销死亡宣告。

**Article 50** If a person declared dead reappears, the People's Court shall revoke the declaration of death upon application by the person himself or herself or by any interested party.

<a id="art-51"></a>第五十一条　被宣告死亡的人的婚姻关系，自死亡宣告之日起消除。死亡宣告被撤销的，婚姻关系自撤销死亡宣告之日起自行恢复。但是，其配偶再婚或者向婚姻登记机关书面声明不愿意恢复的除外。

**Article 51** The marital relationship of a person declared dead shall terminate from the date of the declaration of death. If the declaration of death is revoked, the marital relationship shall automatically resume from the date of revocation. However, this shall not apply if the spouse has remarried or has made a written declaration to the marriage registration authority expressing unwillingness to resume the marital relationship.

<a id="art-52"></a>第五十二条　被宣告死亡的人在被宣告死亡期间，其子女被他人依法收养的，在死亡宣告被撤销后，不得以未经本人同意为由主张收养行为无效。

**Article 52** If, during the period in which a person is declared dead, his or her child is adopted by another person in accordance with law, the person shall not, after the revocation of the declaration of death, claim that the adoption is invalid on the ground that his or her consent was not obtained.

<a id="art-53"></a>第五十三条　被撤销死亡宣告的人有权请求依照本法第六编取得其财产的民事主体返还财产；无法返还的，应当给予适当补偿。  

**Article 53** A person whose declaration of death has been revoked shall have the right to request any civil subject who has obtained his or her property in accordance with Part VI of this Law to return the property; if the property cannot be returned, appropriate compensation shall be made. If an interested party conceals the true facts, causing another person to be declared dead and thereby obtaining that person's property, the interested party shall, in addition to returning the property, also bear compensation liability for the losses caused thereby.
利害关系人隐瞒真实情况，致使他人被宣告死亡而取得其财产的，除应当返还财产外，还应当对由此造成的损失承担赔偿责任。

#### 第四节　个体工商户和农村承包经营户

<a id="art-54"></a>第五十四条　自然人从事工商业经营，经依法登记，为个体工商户。个体工商户可以起字号。

**Article 54** A natural person engaged in industrial or commercial operations and registered in accordance with law shall be deemed an individual industrial and commercial household. An individual industrial and commercial household may adopt a trade name.

<a id="art-55"></a>第五十五条　农村集体经济组织的成员，依法取得农村土地承包经营权，从事家庭承包经营的，为农村承包经营户。

**Article 55** A member of a rural collective economic organization who, in accordance with law, obtains the right to operate rural land under a household contract and engages in household contractual operations, shall be deemed a rural contractual operation household.

<a id="art-56"></a>第五十六条　个体工商户的债务，个人经营的，以个人财产承担；家庭经营的，以家庭财产承担；无法区分的，以家庭财产承担。  

**Article 56** The debts of an individual industrial and commercial household shall be borne by the personal property of the individual operator if the business is operated individually; by the property of the household if the business is operated by the household; and by the property of the household if the distinction cannot be made. The debts of a rural contractual operation household shall be borne by the property of the household engaged in the contractual operation of rural land; if the business is actually operated by some members of the household, the debts shall be borne by the property of those members.
农村承包经营户的债务，以从事农村土地承包经营的农户财产承担；事实上由农户部分成员经营的，以该部分成员的财产承担。

### 第三章　法人

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-57"></a>第五十七条　法人是具有民事权利能力和民事行为能力，依法独立享有民事权利和承担民事义务的组织。

**Article 57** A legal person is an organization that has the capacity for civil rights and capacity for civil conduct, and independently enjoys civil rights and assumes civil obligations in accordance with law.

<a id="art-58"></a>第五十八条　法人应当依法成立。  

**Article 58** A legal person shall be established in accordance with law. A legal person shall have its own name, organizational structure, domicile, property, or funds. The specific conditions and procedures for the establishment of a legal person shall be governed by the provisions of laws and administrative regulations. If a law or administrative regulation requires approval by the relevant authority for the establishment of a legal person, such approval shall be obtained in accordance with those provisions.
法人应当有自己的名称、组织机构、住所、财产或者经费。法人成立的具体条件和程序，依照法律、行政法规的规定。  
设立法人，法律、行政法规规定须经有关机关批准的，依照其规定。

<a id="art-59"></a>第五十九条　法人的民事权利能力和民事行为能力，从法人成立时产生，到法人终止时消灭。

**Article 59** The capacity for civil rights and capacity for civil conduct of a legal person shall arise upon its establishment and shall cease upon its termination.

<a id="art-60"></a>第六十条　法人以其全部财产独立承担民事责任。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../宪法与国家机构/司法解释/最高人民法院关于审理军队、武警部队、政法机关移交、撤销企业和与党政机关脱钩企业相关纠纷案件若干问题的规定.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理军队、武警部队、政法机关移交、撤销企业和与党政机关脱钩企业相关纠纷案件若干问题的规定》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 60** A legal person shall independently assume civil liability with all of its property.

<a id="art-61"></a>第六十一条　依照法律或者法人章程的规定，代表法人从事民事活动的负责人，为法人的法定代表人。  

**Article 61** In accordance with the provisions of the law or the articles of association of a legal person, the person in charge who represents the legal person in engaging in civil activities shall be the legal representative of the legal person.
The legal consequences of civil activities conducted by the legal representative in the name of the legal person shall be borne by the legal person.
Any restriction imposed by the articles of association of the legal person or its governing body on the representative authority of the legal representative shall not be set up against a bona fide counterparty.
法定代表人以法人名义从事的民事活动，其法律后果由法人承受。  
法人章程或者法人权力机构对法定代表人代表权的限制，不得对抗善意相对人。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-62"></a>第六十二条　法定代表人因执行职务造成他人损害的，由法人承担民事责任。  

**Article 62** Where the legal representative causes harm to another person in the course of performing his or her duties, the legal person shall bear civil liability.
After the legal person bears civil liability, it may, in accordance with the provisions of the law or its articles of association, seek recourse against the legal representative who is at fault.
法人承担民事责任后，依照法律或者法人章程的规定，可以向有过错的法定代表人追偿。

<a id="art-63"></a>第六十三条　法人以其主要办事机构所在地为住所。依法需要办理法人登记的，应当将主要办事机构所在地登记为住所。

**Article 63** The domicile of a legal person shall be the place where its principal office is located. Where registration of the legal person is required by law, the place of its principal office shall be registered as its domicile.

<a id="art-64"></a>第六十四条　法人存续期间登记事项发生变化的，应当依法向登记机关申请变更登记。

**Article 64** If any registered item of a legal person changes during its existence, it shall apply to the registration authority for modification of the registration in accordance with the law.

<a id="art-65"></a>第六十五条　法人的实际情况与登记的事项不一致的，不得对抗善意相对人。

**Article 65** If the actual situation of a legal person is inconsistent with the registered items, the inconsistency shall not be set up against a bona fide counterparty.

<a id="art-66"></a>第六十六条　登记机关应当依法及时公示法人登记的有关信息。

**Article 66** The registration authority shall, in accordance with the law, promptly publicize relevant information on the registration of legal persons.

<a id="art-67"></a>第六十七条　法人合并的，其权利和义务由合并后的法人享有和承担。  

**Article 67** Where a legal person is merged, the rights and obligations of the former legal person shall be enjoyed and assumed by the surviving legal person.
Where a legal person is divided, the rights and obligations of the former legal person shall be enjoyed and assumed by the resulting legal persons, who shall have joint and several claims and bear joint and several debts, unless otherwise agreed by the creditors and debtors.
法人分立的，其权利和义务由分立后的法人享有连带债权，承担连带债务，但是债权人和债务人另有约定的除外。

<a id="art-68"></a>第六十八条　有下列原因之一并依法完成清算、注销登记的，法人终止：  

**Article 68** A legal person shall terminate upon occurrence of any of the following reasons and upon completion of liquidation and deregistration in accordance with the law:
(1) The legal person is dissolved;
(2) The legal person is declared bankrupt;
(3) Other reasons provided by the law.
Where the termination of a legal person requires approval from relevant authorities under any law or administrative regulation, such provisions shall prevail.
（一）法人解散；  
（二）法人被宣告破产；  
（三）法律规定的其他原因。  
法人终止，法律、行政法规规定须经有关机关批准的，依照其规定。

<a id="art-69"></a>第六十九条　有下列情形之一的，法人解散：  

**Article 69** A legal person shall be dissolved under any of the following circumstances:
(1) The term of existence specified in its articles of association expires or another cause of dissolution specified in its articles of association arises;
(2) Its governing body adopts a resolution to dissolve it;
(3) It needs to be dissolved due to merger or division;
(4) Its business license or registration certificate is lawfully revoked, it is ordered to be closed down, or it is abolished; or
(5) Other circumstances provided by the law.
（一）法人章程规定的存续期间届满或者法人章程规定的其他解散事由出现；  
（二）法人的权力机构决议解散；  
（三）因法人合并或者分立需要解散；  
（四）法人依法被吊销营业执照、登记证书，被责令关闭或者被撤销；  
（五）法律规定的其他情形。

<a id="art-70"></a>第七十条　法人解散的，除合并或者分立的情形外，清算义务人应当及时组成清算组进行清算。  

**Article 70** Where a legal person is dissolved, the persons obligated to liquidate shall, except in the case of merger or division, promptly form a liquidation committee to carry out the liquidation.
The members of the executive body or decision-making body, such as directors and council members, of the legal person shall be the persons obligated to liquidate, unless otherwise provided by any law or administrative regulation.
If the persons obligated to liquidate fail to timely perform their obligation of liquidation and cause damage, they shall bear civil liability. The competent authority or interested parties may apply to the People's Court for appointing relevant persons to form a liquidation committee for carrying out the liquidation.
法人的董事、理事等执行机构或者决策机构的成员为清算义务人。法律、行政法规另有规定的，依照其规定。  
清算义务人未及时履行清算义务，造成损害的，应当承担民事责任；主管机关或者利害关系人可以申请人民法院指定有关人员组成清算组进行清算。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/公司与破产/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（二）.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（二）》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/公司与破产/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（二）.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（二）》第7条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-71"></a>第七十一条　法人的清算程序和清算组职权，依照有关法律的规定；没有规定的，参照适用公司法律的有关规定。

**Article 71** The liquidation procedures of a legal person and the powers and functions of the liquidation committee shall be governed by the provisions of relevant laws; where there are no such provisions, the relevant provisions of the company law shall apply mutatis mutandis.

<a id="art-72"></a>第七十二条　清算期间法人存续，但是不得从事与清算无关的活动。  

**Article 72** During the period of liquidation, the legal person shall continue to exist but shall not engage in any activities unrelated to the liquidation.
The remaining property of the legal person after liquidation shall be disposed of in accordance with the provisions of its articles of association or a resolution of its governing body, unless otherwise provided by the law.
When the liquidation is completed and the deregistration of the legal person is accomplished, the legal person shall terminate. Where registration of the legal person is not required by law, the legal person shall terminate upon the completion of the liquidation.
法人清算后的剩余财产，按照法人章程的规定或者法人权力机构的决议处理。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。  
清算结束并完成法人注销登记时，法人终止；依法不需要办理法人登记的，清算结束时，法人终止。

<a id="art-73"></a>第七十三条　法人被宣告破产的，依法进行破产清算并完成法人注销登记时，法人终止。

**Article 73** Where a legal person is declared bankrupt, the legal person shall terminate when the bankruptcy liquidation is carried out in accordance with the law and the deregistration of the legal person is completed.

<a id="art-74"></a>第七十四条　法人可以依法设立分支机构。法律、行政法规规定分支机构应当登记的，依照其规定。  

**Article 74** A legal person may establish branches in accordance with the law. Where any law or administrative regulation provides that a branch shall be registered, such provisions shall prevail.
Where a branch engages in civil activities in its own name, the legal person shall bear the civil liability arising therefrom. Alternatively, the liability may first be borne with the property managed by such branch, and if such property is insufficient, the legal person shall bear the remaining liability.
分支机构以自己的名义从事民事活动，产生的民事责任由法人承担；也可以先以该分支机构管理的财产承担，不足以承担的，由法人承担。

<a id="art-75"></a>第七十五条　设立人为设立法人从事的民事活动，其法律后果由法人承受；法人未成立的，其法律后果由设立人承受，设立人为二人以上的，享有连带债权，承担连带债务。  

**Article 75** The legal consequences of civil activities conducted by the founder(s) for the purpose of establishing a legal person shall be borne by the legal person. If the legal person is not formed, the legal consequences shall be borne by the founder(s); where there are two or more founders, they shall have joint and several claims and bear joint and several debts.
Where civil activities are conducted by the founder(s) in their own name for the purpose of establishing the legal person, giving rise to civil liability, a third party shall have the right to elect to require either the legal person or the founder(s) to bear the liability.
设立人为设立法人以自己的名义从事民事活动产生的民事责任，第三人有权选择请求法人或者设立人承担。

#### 第二节　营利法人

<a id="art-76"></a>第七十六条　以取得利润并分配给股东等出资人为目的成立的法人，为营利法人。  

**Article 76** A legal person established for the purpose of obtaining profits and distributing them to its shareholders or other contributors shall be a for-profit legal person.
For-profit legal persons include limited liability companies, companies limited by shares, and other enterprise legal persons, among others.
营利法人包括有限责任公司、股份有限公司和其他企业法人等。

<a id="art-77"></a>第七十七条　营利法人经依法登记成立。

**Article 77** A for-profit legal person shall be formed upon registration in accordance with the law.

<a id="art-78"></a>第七十八条　依法设立的营利法人，由登记机关发给营利法人营业执照。营业执照签发日期为营利法人的成立日期。

**Article 78** A for-profit legal person established in accordance with the law shall be issued a business license for the for-profit legal person by the registration authority. The date of issuance of the business license shall be the date of formation of the for-profit legal person.

<a id="art-79"></a>第七十九条　设立营利法人应当依法制定法人章程。

**Article 79** The articles of association of a for-profit legal person shall be formulated in accordance with the law when it is established.

<a id="art-80"></a>第八十条　营利法人应当设权力机构。  

**Article 80** A for-profit legal person shall have a governing body.
The governing body shall exercise the powers and functions to amend the articles of association of the legal person, elect or replace members of the executive body and the supervisory body, and perform other functions and powers specified in the articles of association of the legal person.
权力机构行使修改法人章程，选举或者更换执行机构、监督机构成员，以及法人章程规定的其他职权。

<a id="art-81"></a>第八十一条　营利法人应当设执行机构。  

**Article 81** A for-profit legal person shall establish an executive body.
The executive body shall exercise the power to convene meetings of the power organ, decide on the business plan and investment plan, decide on the setup of the internal management structure, and other functions and powers provided for by the legal person's articles of association.
Where the executive body is a board of directors or an executive director, the chairman of the board, executive director, or manager shall serve as the legal representative under the legal person's articles of association; where no board of directors or executive director is established, the principal responsible person prescribed by the legal person's articles of association shall serve as its executive body and legal representative.
执行机构行使召集权力机构会议，决定法人的经营计划和投资方案，决定法人内部管理机构的设置，以及法人章程规定的其他职权。  
执行机构为董事会或者执行董事的，董事长、执行董事或者经理按照法人章程的规定担任法定代表人；未设董事会或者执行董事的，法人章程规定的主要负责人为其执行机构和法定代表人。

<a id="art-82"></a>第八十二条　营利法人设监事会或者监事等监督机构的，监督机构依法行使检查法人财务，监督执行机构成员、高级管理人员执行法人职务的行为，以及法人章程规定的其他职权。

**Article 82** Where a for-profit legal person establishes a board of supervisors or a supervisor as its supervisory body, the supervisory body shall, in accordance with the law, exercise the power to inspect the legal person's financial affairs, supervise the performance of duties by members of the executive body and senior officers, and other functions and powers provided for by the legal person's articles of association.

<a id="art-83"></a>第八十三条　营利法人的出资人不得滥用出资人权利损害法人或者其他出资人的利益；滥用出资人权利造成法人或者其他出资人损失的，应当依法承担民事责任。  

**Article 83** The contributors of a for-profit legal person shall not abuse their rights as contributors to harm the interests of the legal person or other contributors; where damage is caused to the legal person or other contributors by such abuse, they shall bear civil liability in accordance with the law.
The contributors of a for-profit legal person shall not abuse the legal person's independent status and the contributors' limited liability to harm the interests of the legal person's creditors; where they abuse the legal person's independent status and the contributors' limited liability to evade debts and seriously harm the interests of the legal person's creditors, they shall bear joint and several liability for the legal person's debts.
营利法人的出资人不得滥用法人独立地位和出资人有限责任损害法人债权人的利益；滥用法人独立地位和出资人有限责任，逃避债务，严重损害法人债权人的利益的，应当对法人债务承担连带责任。

<a id="art-84"></a>第八十四条　营利法人的控股出资人、实际控制人、董事、监事、高级管理人员不得利用其关联关系损害法人的利益；利用关联关系造成法人损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/公司与破产/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（五）.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（五）》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 84** The controlling contributor, actual controller, directors, supervisors, and senior officers of a for-profit legal person shall not use their affiliated relationships to harm the interests of the legal person; where damage is caused to the legal person by using such affiliated relationships, they shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-85"></a>第八十五条　营利法人的权力机构、执行机构作出决议的会议召集程序、表决方式违反法律、行政法规、法人章程，或者决议内容违反法人章程的，营利法人的出资人可以请求人民法院撤销该决议。但是，营利法人依据该决议与善意相对人形成的民事法律关系不受影响。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/公司与破产/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（五）.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（五）》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/公司与破产/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（四）.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（四）》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/公司与破产/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（四）.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国公司法》若干问题的规定（四）》第4条引用">[3]</a></sup>

**Article 85** Where the convening procedure or voting method of a meeting at which a resolution is adopted by a power organ or executive body of a for-profit legal person violates any law, administrative regulation, or the legal person's articles of association, or where the content of the resolution violates the articles of association, the contributors of the for-profit legal person may request the People's Court to revoke the resolution. However, the civil legal relationship formed between the for-profit legal person and a bona fide counterparty based on such a resolution shall not be affected.

<a id="art-86"></a>第八十六条　营利法人从事经营活动，应当遵守商业道德，维护交易安全，接受政府和社会的监督，承担社会责任。

**Article 86** A for-profit legal person, when engaging in business activities, shall comply with business ethics, maintain transaction security, accept supervision from the government and society, and assume social responsibility.

#### 第三节　非营利法人

<a id="art-87"></a>第八十七条　为公益目的或者其他非营利目的成立，不向出资人、设立人或者会员分配所取得利润的法人，为非营利法人。  

**Article 87** A legal person established for a public interest purpose or another non-profit purpose, which does not distribute profits obtained to its contributors, founders, or members, is a non-profit legal person.
Non-profit legal persons include public institutions, social organizations, foundations, social service organizations, and the like.
非营利法人包括事业单位、社会团体、基金会、社会服务机构等。

<a id="art-88"></a>第八十八条　具备法人条件，为适应经济社会发展需要，提供公益服务设立的事业单位，经依法登记成立，取得事业单位法人资格；依法不需要办理法人登记的，从成立之日起，具有事业单位法人资格。

**Article 88** A public institution established to meet the needs of economic and social development and to provide public services, which meets the conditions for a legal person and is legally registered, acquires the qualification of a public institution legal person; where legal person registration is not required by law, it has the qualification of a public institution legal person from the date of its establishment.

<a id="art-89"></a>第八十九条　事业单位法人设理事会的，除法律另有规定外，理事会为其决策机构。事业单位法人的法定代表人依照法律、行政法规或者法人章程的规定产生。

**Article 89** Where the legal person of a public institution has a council, the council shall be its decision-making body, unless otherwise provided by law. The legal representative of the legal person of a public institution shall be determined in accordance with any law, administrative regulation, or the legal person's articles of association.

<a id="art-90"></a>第九十条　具备法人条件，基于会员共同意愿，为公益目的或者会员共同利益等非营利目的设立的社会团体，经依法登记成立，取得社会团体法人资格；依法不需要办理法人登记的，从成立之日起，具有社会团体法人资格。

**Article 90** A social organization established based on the common will of its members for a public interest purpose or a non-profit purpose such as the common interest of its members, which meets the conditions for a legal person and is legally registered, acquires the qualification of a social organization legal person; where legal person registration is not required by law, it has the qualification of a social organization legal person from the date of its establishment.

<a id="art-91"></a>第九十一条　设立社会团体法人应当依法制定法人章程。  

**Article 91** To establish a social organization legal person, the legal person's articles of association shall be formulated in accordance with the law.
A social organization legal person shall establish a power organ such as a general meeting of members or a meeting of representatives of members.
A social organization legal person shall establish an executive body such as a council. The principal officer, such as the chairman of the council or the president, shall serve as the legal representative under the provisions of the legal person's articles of association.
社会团体法人应当设会员大会或者会员代表大会等权力机构。  
社会团体法人应当设理事会等执行机构。理事长或者会长等负责人按照法人章程的规定担任法定代表人。

<a id="art-92"></a>第九十二条　具备法人条件，为公益目的以捐助财产设立的基金会、社会服务机构等，经依法登记成立，取得捐助法人资格。  

**Article 92** A foundation, social service organization, or the like established by donated property for a public interest purpose, which meets the conditions for a legal person and is legally registered, acquires the qualification of a endowed legal person.
A religious activity site established in accordance with the law, which meets the conditions for a legal person, may apply for legal person registration and acquire the qualification of a endowed legal person. Where there are provisions of any law or administrative regulation on religious activity sites, those provisions shall prevail.
依法设立的宗教活动场所，具备法人条件的，可以申请法人登记，取得捐助法人资格。法律、行政法规对宗教活动场所有规定的，依照其规定。

<a id="art-93"></a>第九十三条　设立捐助法人应当依法制定法人章程。  

**Article 93** To establish a endowed legal person, the legal person's articles of association shall be formulated in accordance with the law.
A endowed legal person shall establish a decision-making body such as a council or a democratic management organization, and shall establish an executive body. The principal officer, such as the chairman of the council, shall serve as the legal representative under the provisions of the legal person's articles of association.
A endowed legal person shall establish a supervisory body such as a board of supervisors.
捐助法人应当设理事会、民主管理组织等决策机构，并设执行机构。理事长等负责人按照法人章程的规定担任法定代表人。  
捐助法人应当设监事会等监督机构。

<a id="art-94"></a>第九十四条　捐助人有权向捐助法人查询捐助财产的使用、管理情况，并提出意见和建议，捐助法人应当及时、如实答复。  

**Article 94** Donors shall have the right to inquire about the use and management of donated property from the endowed legal person and to make comments and suggestions, and the endowed legal person shall respond promptly and truthfully.
Where the procedure for making a decision by the decision-making body, executive body, or legal representative of an endowed legal person violates any law, administrative regulation, or the legal person's articles of association, or where the content of the decision violates the legal person's articles of association, interested parties such as the donors or the competent authority may request the People's Court to revoke the decision. However, the civil legal relationship formed between the endowed legal person and a bona fide counterparty based on such a decision shall not be affected.
捐助法人的决策机构、执行机构或者法定代表人作出决定的程序违反法律、行政法规、法人章程，或者决定内容违反法人章程的，捐助人等利害关系人或者主管机关可以请求人民法院撤销该决定。但是，捐助法人依据该决定与善意相对人形成的民事法律关系不受影响。

<a id="art-95"></a>第九十五条　为公益目的成立的非营利法人终止时，不得向出资人、设立人或者会员分配剩余财产。剩余财产应当按照法人章程的规定或者权力机构的决议用于公益目的；无法按照法人章程的规定或者权力机构的决议处理的，由主管机关主持转给宗旨相同或者相近的法人，并向社会公告。

**Article 95** When a non-profit legal person established for a public interest purpose is terminated, no residual property shall be distributed to its contributors, founders, or members. The residual property shall be used for a public interest purpose in accordance with the provisions of the legal person's articles of association or the resolution of its power organ; where it cannot be handled in accordance with the articles of association or the resolution of the power organ, the competent authority shall oversee its transfer to another legal person with the same or a similar purpose and make a public announcement.

#### 第四节　特别法人

<a id="art-96"></a>第九十六条　本节规定的机关法人、农村集体经济组织法人、城镇农村的合作经济组织法人、基层群众性自治组织法人，为特别法人。

**Article 96** The organ legal persons, rural collective economic organization legal persons, urban and rural cooperative economic organization legal persons, and grassroots self-governing mass organization legal persons provided for in this Section are special legal persons.

<a id="art-97"></a>第九十七条　有独立经费的机关和承担行政职能的法定机构从成立之日起，具有机关法人资格，可以从事为履行职能所需要的民事活动。

**Article 97** An organ with independent funds and any statutory body undertaking administrative functions shall, from the date of its establishment, have the qualification of an organ legal person and may engage in civil activities necessary for performing its functions.

<a id="art-98"></a>第九十八条　机关法人被撤销的，法人终止，其民事权利和义务由继任的机关法人享有和承担；没有继任的机关法人的，由作出撤销决定的机关法人享有和承担。

**Article 98** Where an organ legal person is abolished, the legal person is terminated, and its civil rights and obligations shall be enjoyed and borne by the succeeding organ legal person; where there is no succeeding organ legal person, they shall be enjoyed and borne by the organ legal person that made the decision to abolish it.

<a id="art-99"></a>第九十九条　农村集体经济组织依法取得法人资格。  

**Article 99** A rural collective economic organization shall acquire the qualification of a legal person in accordance with the law.
Where there are provisions of any law or administrative regulation on rural collective economic organizations, those provisions shall prevail.
法律、行政法规对农村集体经济组织有规定的，依照其规定。

<a id="art-100"></a>第一百条　城镇农村的合作经济组织依法取得法人资格。  

**Article 100** An urban or rural cooperative economic organization shall acquire the qualification of a legal person in accordance with the law.
Where there are provisions of any law or administrative regulation on urban or rural cooperative economic organizations, those provisions shall prevail.
法律、行政法规对城镇农村的合作经济组织有规定的，依照其规定。

<a id="art-101"></a>第一百零一条　居民委员会、村民委员会具有基层群众性自治组织法人资格，可以从事为履行职能所需要的民事活动。  
未设立村集体经济组织的，村民委员会可以依法代行村集体经济组织的职能。

### 第四章　非法人组织

<a id="art-102"></a>第一百零二条　非法人组织是不具有法人资格，但是能够依法以自己的名义从事民事活动的组织。  
非法人组织包括个人独资企业、合伙企业、不具有法人资格的专业服务机构等。

<a id="art-103"></a>第一百零三条　非法人组织应当依照法律的规定登记。  
设立非法人组织，法律、行政法规规定须经有关机关批准的，依照其规定。

<a id="art-104"></a>第一百零四条　非法人组织的财产不足以清偿债务的，其出资人或者设立人承担无限责任。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

<a id="art-105"></a>第一百零五条　非法人组织可以确定一人或者数人代表该组织从事民事活动。

<a id="art-106"></a>第一百零六条　有下列情形之一的，非法人组织解散：  
（一）章程规定的存续期间届满或者章程规定的其他解散事由出现；  
（二）出资人或者设立人决定解散；  
（三）法律规定的其他情形。

<a id="art-107"></a>第一百零七条　非法人组织解散的，应当依法进行清算。

<a id="art-108"></a>第一百零八条　非法人组织除适用本章规定外，参照适用本编第三章第一节的有关规定。

### 第五章　民事权利

<a id="art-109"></a>第一百零九条　自然人的人身自由、人格尊严受法律保护。

<a id="art-110"></a>第一百一十条　自然人享有生命权、身体权、健康权、姓名权、肖像权、名誉权、荣誉权、隐私权、婚姻自主权等权利。  

**Article 110** A natural person shall enjoy the rights to life, bodily integrity, health, name, portrait, reputation, honor, privacy, and marital autonomy, among others.
A legal person or an unincorporated organization shall enjoy the rights to name, reputation, and honor.
法人、非法人组织享有名称权、名誉权和荣誉权。

<a id="art-111"></a>第一百一十一条　自然人的个人信息受法律保护。任何组织或者个人需要获取他人个人信息的，应当依法取得并确保信息安全，不得非法收集、使用、加工、传输他人个人信息，不得非法买卖、提供或者公开他人个人信息。

**Article 111** The personal information of a natural person shall be protected by law. Any organization or individual that needs to obtain another person's personal information shall acquire it according to law and ensure its security, and shall not illegally collect, use, process, or transmit another person's personal information, nor illegally trade in, provide, or disclose another person's personal information.

<a id="art-112"></a>第一百一十二条　自然人因婚姻家庭关系等产生的人身权利受法律保护。

**Article 112** The personal rights of a natural person arising from marital or family relations, among others, shall be protected by law.

<a id="art-113"></a>第一百一十三条　民事主体的财产权利受法律平等保护。

**Article 113** The property rights of civil subjects shall be equally protected by law.

<a id="art-114"></a>第一百一十四条　民事主体依法享有物权。  

**Article 114** A civil subject shall enjoy real rights according to law.
A real right is the right of the right holder to directly control and exclusively enjoy a specific property according to law, including ownership, usufruct, and security interests.
物权是权利人依法对特定的物享有直接支配和排他的权利，包括所有权、用益物权和担保物权。

<a id="art-115"></a>第一百一十五条　物包括不动产和动产。法律规定权利作为物权客体的，依照其规定。

**Article 115** Property includes immovable property and movable property. Where a law provides that a right is the object of a real right, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-116"></a>第一百一十六条　物权的种类和内容，由法律规定。

**Article 116** The types and content of real rights shall be provided for by law.

<a id="art-117"></a>第一百一十七条　为了公共利益的需要，依照法律规定的权限和程序征收、征用不动产或者动产的，应当给予公平、合理的补偿。

**Article 117** Where immovable or movable property is expropriated or requisitioned for the needs of the public interest, in accordance with the authority and procedures provided for by law, fair and reasonable compensation shall be given.

<a id="art-118"></a>第一百一十八条　民事主体依法享有债权。  

**Article 118** A civil subject shall enjoy creditor's rights according to law.
A creditor's right is the right of a right holder to request a specific obligor to perform or refrain from performing a certain act, arising from contracts, tortious acts, negotiorum gestio, unjust enrichment, or other provisions of law.
债权是因合同、侵权行为、无因管理、不当得利以及法律的其他规定，权利人请求特定义务人为或者不为一定行为的权利。

<a id="art-119"></a>第一百一十九条　依法成立的合同，对当事人具有法律约束力。

**Article 119** A contract lawfully formed shall be legally binding on the parties.

<a id="art-120"></a>第一百二十条　民事权益受到侵害的，被侵权人有权请求侵权人承担侵权责任。

**Article 120** Where a party's civil rights or interests are infringed upon, the infringed party shall be entitled to request the tortfeasor to bear tort liability.

<a id="art-121"></a>第一百二十一条　没有法定的或者约定的义务，为避免他人利益受损失而进行管理的人，有权请求受益人偿还由此支出的必要费用。

**Article 121** : A person who, without a statutory or contractual obligation, manages affairs to prevent loss of another's interests shall have the right to claim from the beneficiary reimbursement of the necessary expenses incurred thereby.

<a id="art-122"></a>第一百二十二条　因他人没有法律根据，取得不当利益，受损失的人有权请求其返还不当利益。

**Article 122** : Where a person obtains an unjust benefit without legal basis, the person who suffers the loss shall have the right to claim restitution of the unjust benefit.

<a id="art-123"></a>第一百二十三条　民事主体依法享有知识产权。  

**Article 123** : Civil subjects shall enjoy intellectual property rights in accordance with law.
Intellectual property rights are the exclusive rights enjoyed by the right holder in accordance with law in respect of the following subject matters:
(1) works;
(2) inventions, utility models, and designs;
(3) trademarks;
(4) geographical indications;
(5) trade secrets;
(6) integrated circuit layout designs;
(7) new plant varieties;
(8) other subject matters as provided by law.
知识产权是权利人依法就下列客体享有的专有的权利：  
（一）作品；  
（二）发明、实用新型、外观设计；  
（三）商标；  
（四）地理标志；  
（五）商业秘密；  
（六）集成电路布图设计；  
（七）植物新品种；  
（八）法律规定的其他客体。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院关于内地与香港特别行政区法院相互认可和执行民商事案件判决的安排.md#art-5" title="被《最高人民法院关于内地与香港特别行政区法院相互认可和执行民商事案件判决的安排》第5条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-124"></a>第一百二十四条　自然人依法享有继承权。  

**Article 124** : Natural persons shall enjoy the right of succession in accordance with law.
The lawful private property of a natural person may be inherited in accordance with law.
自然人合法的私有财产，可以依法继承。

<a id="art-125"></a>第一百二十五条　民事主体依法享有股权和其他投资性权利。

**Article 125** : Civil subjects shall enjoy equity rights and other investment rights in accordance with law.

<a id="art-126"></a>第一百二十六条　民事主体享有法律规定的其他民事权利和利益。

**Article 126** : Civil subjects shall enjoy other civil rights and interests as provided by law.

<a id="art-127"></a>第一百二十七条　法律对数据、网络虚拟财产的保护有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 127** : Where laws provide for the protection of data and network virtual property, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-128"></a>第一百二十八条　法律对未成年人、老年人、残疾人、妇女、消费者等的民事权利保护有特别规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 128** : Where laws have special provisions on the protection of the civil rights of minors, the elderly, the disabled, women, consumers, and the like, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-129"></a>第一百二十九条　民事权利可以依据民事法律行为、事实行为、法律规定的事件或者法律规定的其他方式取得。

**Article 129** : Civil rights may be acquired based on a civil juristic act, a factual act, an event provided by law, or other means provided by law.

<a id="art-130"></a>第一百三十条　民事主体按照自己的意愿依法行使民事权利，不受干涉。

**Article 130** : Civil subjects shall exercise their civil rights in accordance with their own will and pursuant to law, and shall not be subject to interference.

<a id="art-131"></a>第一百三十一条　民事主体行使权利时，应当履行法律规定的和当事人约定的义务。

**Article 131** : When exercising rights, civil subjects shall perform the obligations provided by law and agreed upon by the parties.

<a id="art-132"></a>第一百三十二条　民事主体不得滥用民事权利损害国家利益、社会公共利益或者他人合法权益。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 132** : Civil subjects shall not abuse civil rights to harm the national interest, the public interest, or the lawful rights and interests of others.

### 第六章　民事法律行为

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-133"></a>第一百三十三条　民事法律行为是民事主体通过意思表示设立、变更、终止民事法律关系的行为。

**Article 133** : A civil juristic act is an act of a civil subject to establish, modify, or terminate a civil legal relationship through a declaration of will.

<a id="art-134"></a>第一百三十四条　民事法律行为可以基于双方或者多方的意思表示一致成立，也可以基于单方的意思表示成立。  

**Article 134** : A civil juristic act may be formed based on the consensus of declarations of will by two or more parties, or may be formed based on a unilateral declaration of will.
Where a legal person or an unincorporated organization makes a resolution in accordance with the methods of deliberation and voting procedures provided by law or its articles of association, such act of resolution shall be formed.
法人、非法人组织依照法律或者章程规定的议事方式和表决程序作出决议的，该决议行为成立。

<a id="art-135"></a>第一百三十五条　民事法律行为可以采用书面形式、口头形式或者其他形式；法律、行政法规规定或者当事人约定采用特定形式的，应当采用特定形式。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-18" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第18条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 135** : A civil juristic act may be in written form, oral form, or other forms; where a law or administrative regulation provides, or the parties agree, that a specific form shall be adopted, such specific form shall be adopted.

<a id="art-136"></a>第一百三十六条　民事法律行为自成立时生效，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。  

**Article 136** : A civil juristic act shall take effect upon its formation, unless otherwise provided by law or agreed by the parties.
The actor shall not alter or rescind the civil juristic act without legal basis or without the consent of the other party.
行为人非依法律规定或者未经对方同意，不得擅自变更或者解除民事法律行为。

#### 第二节　意思表示

<a id="art-137"></a>第一百三十七条　以对话方式作出的意思表示，相对人知道其内容时生效。  

**Article 137** : A declaration of will made orally shall take effect when the counterparty becomes aware of its content.
A declaration of will made in a non-oral manner shall take effect when it reaches the counterparty. Where a declaration of will is made in the form of data messages in a non-oral manner, if the counterparty designates a specific system for receiving data messages, such data message shall take effect when it enters that specific system; if no specific system is designated, it shall take effect when the counterparty knows or should know that the data message has entered its system. If the parties have otherwise agreed on the time of effectiveness of a declaration of will made in the form of data messages, such agreement shall prevail.
以非对话方式作出的意思表示，到达相对人时生效。以非对话方式作出的采用数据电文形式的意思表示，相对人指定特定系统接收数据电文的，该数据电文进入该特定系统时生效；未指定特定系统的，相对人知道或者应当知道该数据电文进入其系统时生效。当事人对采用数据电文形式的意思表示的生效时间另有约定的，按照其约定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-29" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第29条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-138"></a>第一百三十八条　无相对人的意思表示，表示完成时生效。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 138** : A declaration of will made without a counterparty shall take effect upon its completion. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-139"></a>第一百三十九条　以公告方式作出的意思表示，公告发布时生效。

**Article 139** : A declaration of will made by way of announcement shall take effect upon the issuance of the announcement.

<a id="art-140"></a>第一百四十条　行为人可以明示或者默示作出意思表示。  

**Article 140** : An actor may make a declaration of will expressly or by implication.
Silence may only be regarded as a declaration of will where it is provided by law, agreed by the parties, or consistent with the usage of trade between the parties.
沉默只有在有法律规定、当事人约定或者符合当事人之间的交易习惯时，才可以视为意思表示。

<a id="art-141"></a>第一百四十一条　行为人可以撤回意思表示。撤回意思表示的通知应当在意思表示到达相对人前或者与意思表示同时到达相对人。

**Article 141** An actor may withdraw an declaration of intention. The notice of withdrawal of the declaration of intention shall arrive at the counterparty before or at the same time as the declaration of intention arrives at the counterparty.

<a id="art-142"></a>第一百四十二条　有相对人的意思表示的解释，应当按照所使用的词句，结合相关条款、行为的性质和目的、习惯以及诚信原则，确定意思表示的含义。  

**Article 142** For the interpretation of a declaration of intention made to a counterparty, the meaning of the declaration of intention shall be determined in accordance with the words and sentences used, with reference to the relevant terms, the nature and purpose of the act, usage, and the principle of good faith.
For the interpretation of a declaration of intention made without a counterparty, the true intention of the actor shall be determined not strictly in accordance with the words and sentences used, but with reference to the relevant terms, the nature and purpose of the act, usage, and the principle of good faith.
无相对人的意思表示的解释，不能完全拘泥于所使用的词句，而应当结合相关条款、行为的性质和目的、习惯以及诚信原则，确定行为人的真实意思。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

#### 第三节　民事法律行为的效力

<a id="art-143"></a>第一百四十三条　具备下列条件的民事法律行为有效：  

**Article 143** A civil juristic act that meets the following conditions is valid:
(1) the actor has the corresponding capacity for civil conduct;
(2) the declaration of intention is genuine; and
(3) it does not violate the mandatory provisions of any law or administrative regulation, nor contravenes public order and good customs.
（一）行为人具有相应的民事行为能力；  
（二）意思表示真实；  
（三）不违反法律、行政法规的强制性规定，不违背公序良俗。

<a id="art-144"></a>第一百四十四条　无民事行为能力人实施的民事法律行为无效。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理民间借贷案件适用法律若干问题的规定.md#art-11" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理民间借贷案件适用法律若干问题的规定》第11条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理民间借贷案件适用法律若干问题的规定.md#art-12" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理民间借贷案件适用法律若干问题的规定》第12条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 144** A civil juristic act performed by a person without capacity for civil conduct is void.

<a id="art-145"></a>第一百四十五条　限制民事行为能力人实施的纯获利益的民事法律行为或者与其年龄、智力、精神健康状况相适应的民事法律行为有效；实施的其他民事法律行为经法定代理人同意或者追认后有效。  

**Article 145** A civil juristic act performed by a person with limited capacity for civil conduct that is purely beneficial to him or is appropriate to his age, intelligence, or mental health condition is valid; other civil juristic acts performed by such a person are valid only upon consent or ratification by his statutory agent.
The counterparty may urge the statutory agent to ratify the act within thirty days from the date of receipt of the notice. If the statutory agent fails to respond, it shall be deemed as refusal to ratify. Before the civil juristic act is ratified, a bona fide counterparty has the right to revoke the act. Revocation shall be made by notice.
相对人可以催告法定代理人自收到通知之日起三十日内予以追认。法定代理人未作表示的，视为拒绝追认。民事法律行为被追认前，善意相对人有撤销的权利。撤销应当以通知的方式作出。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-29" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第29条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-146"></a>第一百四十六条　行为人与相对人以虚假的意思表示实施的民事法律行为无效。  

**Article 146** A civil juristic act performed by an actor and a counterparty based on a false declaration of intention is void.
The validity of a civil juristic act concealed under a false declaration of intention shall be dealt with in accordance with the relevant provisions of the law.
以虚假的意思表示隐藏的民事法律行为的效力，依照有关法律规定处理。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理民间借贷案件适用法律若干问题的规定.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理民间借贷案件适用法律若干问题的规定》第10条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-68" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第68条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-147"></a>第一百四十七条　基于重大误解实施的民事法律行为，行为人有权请求人民法院或者仲裁机构予以撤销。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第19条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 147** An actor who performs a civil juristic act based on a material misunderstanding has the right to request a People's Court or an arbitration institution to revoke the act.

<a id="art-148"></a>第一百四十八条　一方以欺诈手段，使对方在违背真实意思的情况下实施的民事法律行为，受欺诈方有权请求人民法院或者仲裁机构予以撤销。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-21" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第21条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 148** Where a party, by fraudulent means, induces the other party to perform a civil juristic act against its true intention, the defrauded party has the right to request a People's Court or an arbitration institution to revoke the act.

<a id="art-149"></a>第一百四十九条　第三人实施欺诈行为，使一方在违背真实意思的情况下实施的民事法律行为，对方知道或者应当知道该欺诈行为的，受欺诈方有权请求人民法院或者仲裁机构予以撤销。

**Article 149** Where a third party commits a fraudulent act, causing one party to perform a civil juristic act against its true intention, and the other party knows or should have known of the fraud, the defrauded party has the right to request a People's Court or an arbitration institution to revoke the act.

<a id="art-150"></a>第一百五十条　一方或者第三人以胁迫手段，使对方在违背真实意思的情况下实施的民事法律行为，受胁迫方有权请求人民法院或者仲裁机构予以撤销。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-22" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第22条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 150** Where a party or a third party, by means of duress, causes the other party to perform a civil juristic act against its true intention, the coerced party has the right to request a People's Court or an arbitration institution to revoke the act.

<a id="art-151"></a>第一百五十一条　一方利用对方处于危困状态、缺乏判断能力等情形，致使民事法律行为成立时显失公平的，受损害方有权请求人民法院或者仲裁机构予以撤销。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-11" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第11条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 151** Where a party takes advantage of the other party's distress, lack of judgment ability, or other such circumstances, resulting in a manifestly unfair civil juristic act at the time of its formation, the aggrieved party has the right to request a People's Court or an arbitration institution to revoke the act.

<a id="art-152"></a>第一百五十二条　有下列情形之一的，撤销权消灭：  

**Article 152** The right to revoke is extinguished under any of the following circumstances:
(1) The party fails to exercise the right to revoke within one year from the date it knows or should have known of the grounds for revocation, or, in the case of a material misunderstanding, within ninety days from the date it knows or should have known of the grounds for revocation;
(2) The party, being subjected to duress, fails to exercise the right to revoke within one year from the date the duress ceases; or
(3) The party, after becoming aware of the grounds for revocation, expressly waives the right to revoke or indicates such waiver through its conduct.
If the party fails to exercise the right to revoke within five years from the date the civil juristic act occurs, the right to revoke is extinguished.
（一）当事人自知道或者应当知道撤销事由之日起一年内、重大误解的当事人自知道或者应当知道撤销事由之日起九十日内没有行使撤销权；  
（二）当事人受胁迫，自胁迫行为终止之日起一年内没有行使撤销权；  
（三）当事人知道撤销事由后明确表示或者以自己的行为表明放弃撤销权。  
当事人自民事法律行为发生之日起五年内没有行使撤销权的，撤销权消灭。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第19条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-153"></a>第一百五十三条　违反法律、行政法规的强制性规定的民事法律行为无效。但是，该强制性规定不导致该民事法律行为无效的除外。  

**Article 153** A civil juristic act that violates the mandatory provisions of any law or administrative regulation is void, unless such mandatory provision does not render the civil juristic act void.
A civil juristic act that contravenes public order and good customs is void.
违背公序良俗的民事法律行为无效。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于大型企业与中小企业约定以第三方支付款项为付款前提条款效力问题的批复.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于大型企业与中小企业约定以第三方支付款项为付款前提条款效力问题的批复》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理垄断民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-48" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理垄断民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第48条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）》第1条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理森林资源民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-13" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理森林资源民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第13条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-14" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第14条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-16" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第16条引用">[6]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第17条引用">[7]</a></sup>

<a id="art-154"></a>第一百五十四条　行为人与相对人恶意串通，损害他人合法权益的民事法律行为无效。

**Article 154** A civil juristic act performed by an actor and a counterparty through malicious collusion to harm the lawful rights and interests of another person is void.

<a id="art-155"></a>第一百五十五条　无效的或者被撤销的民事法律行为自始没有法律约束力。

**Article 155** A void or revoked civil juristic act has no legal binding force from the very beginning.

<a id="art-156"></a>第一百五十六条　民事法律行为部分无效，不影响其他部分效力的，其他部分仍然有效。

**Article 156** If part of a civil juristic act is void without affecting the validity of the other parts, the other parts remain valid.

<a id="art-157"></a>第一百五十七条　民事法律行为无效、被撤销或者确定不发生效力后，行为人因该行为取得的财产，应当予以返还；不能返还或者没有必要返还的，应当折价补偿。有过错的一方应当赔偿对方由此所受到的损失；各方都有过错的，应当各自承担相应的责任。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理城镇房屋租赁合同纠纷案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理城镇房屋租赁合同纠纷案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理预付式消费民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-15" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理预付式消费民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第15条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-12" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第12条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-14" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第14条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-20" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第20条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-21" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第21条引用">[6]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第7条引用">[7]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释.md#art-23" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》总则编若干问题的解释》第23条引用">[8]</a></sup>

**Article 157** After a civil juristic act is void, revoked, or confirmed to be of no effect, any property obtained by an actor as a result of the act shall be returned; if return is impossible or unnecessary, compensation shall be made at an appraised price. The party at fault shall compensate the other party for the losses incurred as a result; if all parties are at fault, each shall bear its respective liability. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

#### 第四节　民事法律行为的附条件和附期限

<a id="art-158"></a>第一百五十八条　民事法律行为可以附条件，但是根据其性质不得附条件的除外。附生效条件的民事法律行为，自条件成就时生效。附解除条件的民事法律行为，自条件成就时失效。

**Article 158** A civil juristic act may be subject to a condition, unless it cannot be subject to a condition by its nature. A civil juristic act subject to a condition precedent becomes effective upon fulfillment of the condition. A civil juristic act subject to a condition subsequent becomes void upon fulfillment of the condition.

<a id="art-159"></a>第一百五十九条　附条件的民事法律行为，当事人为自己的利益不正当地阻止条件成就的，视为条件已经成就；不正当地促成条件成就的，视为条件不成就。

**Article 159** Where, in a conditional civil juristic act, a party improperly prevents the fulfillment of a condition for its own benefit, the condition shall be deemed to have been fulfilled; where a party improperly causes the fulfillment of a condition for its own benefit, the condition shall be deemed not to have been fulfilled.

<a id="art-160"></a>第一百六十条　民事法律行为可以附期限，但是根据其性质不得附期限的除外。附生效期限的民事法律行为，自期限届至时生效。附终止期限的民事法律行为，自期限届满时失效。

**Article 160** A civil juristic act may be subject to a time period, unless it cannot be subject to a time period by its nature. A civil juristic act subject to a time period for taking effect becomes effective upon the arrival of the time period. A civil juristic act subject to a time period for termination becomes void upon the expiry of the time period.

### 第七章　代理

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-161"></a>第一百六十一条　民事主体可以通过代理人实施民事法律行为。  

**Article 161** Civil subjects may perform civil juristic acts through agents.
A civil juristic act which shall be performed by the principal in person pursuant to legal provisions, the agreement between the parties, or the nature of the civil juristic act may not be performed through an agent.
依照法律规定、当事人约定或者民事法律行为的性质，应当由本人亲自实施的民事法律行为，不得代理。

<a id="art-162"></a>第一百六十二条　代理人在代理权限内，以被代理人名义实施的民事法律行为，对被代理人发生效力。

**Article 162** A civil juristic act performed by an agent within the scope of the authority delegated and in the name of the principal shall have effects on the principal.

<a id="art-163"></a>第一百六十三条　代理包括委托代理和法定代理。  

**Article 163** Agency includes agency by agreement and agency by operation of law.
An agent ad litem shall exercise the power of agency in accordance with the authorization of the principal. A statutory agent shall exercise the power of agency in accordance with the provisions of law.
委托代理人按照被代理人的委托行使代理权。法定代理人依照法律的规定行使代理权。

<a id="art-164"></a>第一百六十四条　代理人不履行或者不完全履行职责，造成被代理人损害的，应当承担民事责任。  

**Article 164** Where an agent fails to perform or does not fully perform its duties, thereby causing damage to the principal, it shall bear civil liability.
Where the agent and the other party maliciously collude, damaging the lawful rights and interests of the principal, the agent and the other party shall be jointly and severally liable.
代理人和相对人恶意串通，损害被代理人合法权益的，代理人和相对人应当承担连带责任。

#### 第二节　委托代理

<a id="art-165"></a>第一百六十五条　委托代理授权采用书面形式的，授权委托书应当载明代理人的姓名或者名称、代理事项、权限和期限，并由被代理人签名或者盖章。

**Article 165** Where the authorization for agency by agreement is made in written form, the power of attorney shall state the name or title of the agent, the matters entrusted, the scope of authority and the term of agency, and shall be signed or sealed by the principal.

<a id="art-166"></a>第一百六十六条　数人为同一代理事项的代理人的，应当共同行使代理权，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 166** Where several persons act as agents for the same agency matter, they shall jointly exercise the power of agency, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-167"></a>第一百六十七条　代理人知道或者应当知道代理事项违法仍然实施代理行为，或者被代理人知道或者应当知道代理人的代理行为违法未作反对表示的，被代理人和代理人应当承担连带责任。

**Article 167** Where an agent, knowing or should have known that the agency matter is illegal, still performs the act of agency, or the principal, knowing or should have known that the agent’s act of agency is illegal, expresses no objection, the principal and the agent shall be jointly and severally liable.

<a id="art-168"></a>第一百六十八条　代理人不得以被代理人的名义与自己实施民事法律行为，但是被代理人同意或者追认的除外。  

**Article 168** An agent may not perform a civil juristic act in the name of the principal with himself, unless the principal consents or subsequently ratifies it.
An agent may not perform a civil juristic act in the name of the principal with another person whom the agent is also acting as an agent for at the same time, unless both principals consent or subsequently ratify it.
代理人不得以被代理人的名义与自己同时代理的其他人实施民事法律行为，但是被代理的双方同意或者追认的除外。
