**Article 338** Where the contracted land is expropriated, the person holding the right to land contractual management shall have the right to obtain corresponding compensation in accordance with Article 243 of this Law.

<a id="art-339"></a>第三百三十九条　土地承包经营权人可以自主决定依法采取出租、入股或者其他方式向他人流转土地经营权。

**Article 339** The person holding the right to land contractual management may independently decide to transfer the land management right to another person by means of lease, shareholding, or other means in accordance with the law.

<a id="art-340"></a>第三百四十条　土地经营权人有权在合同约定的期限内占有农村土地，自主开展农业生产经营并取得收益。

**Article 340** The person holding the land management right shall have the right to possess the rural land within the term agreed upon in the contract, independently carry out agricultural production and operation, and obtain proceeds therefrom.

<a id="art-341"></a>第三百四十一条　流转期限为五年以上的土地经营权，自流转合同生效时设立。当事人可以向登记机构申请土地经营权登记；未经登记，不得对抗善意第三人。

**Article 341** The right to operate land with a transfer term of five years or more shall be established upon the entry into effect of the transfer contract. A party may apply to the registration authority for the registration of the right to operate land; without registration, such right may not be set up against a bona fide third party.

<a id="art-342"></a>第三百四十二条　通过招标、拍卖、公开协商等方式承包农村土地，经依法登记取得权属证书的，可以依法采取出租、入股、抵押或者其他方式流转土地经营权。

**Article 342** Where rural land is contracted by means of bidding, auction, open negotiation, or the like, and a title certificate is obtained upon registration according to law, the right to operate land may be transferred through leasing, equity contribution, mortgage, or other means according to law.

<a id="art-343"></a>第三百四十三条　国家所有的农用地实行承包经营的，参照适用本编的有关规定。

**Article 343** Where contract operation is implemented for agricultural land owned by the State, the relevant provisions of this Part shall apply mutatis mutandis.

### 第十二章　建设用地使用权

<a id="art-344"></a>第三百四十四条　建设用地使用权人依法对国家所有的土地享有占有、使用和收益的权利，有权利用该土地建造建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施。

**Article 344** A person with the right to use construction land shall, according to law, have the right to possess, use, and benefit from the State-owned land, and have the right to use such land to construct buildings, structures, and auxiliary facilities thereof.

<a id="art-345"></a>第三百四十五条　建设用地使用权可以在土地的地表、地上或者地下分别设立。

**Article 345** The right to use construction land may be established on the surface, above the ground, or underground respectively.

<a id="art-346"></a>第三百四十六条　设立建设用地使用权，应当符合节约资源、保护生态环境的要求，遵守法律、行政法规关于土地用途的规定，不得损害已经设立的用益物权。

**Article 346** The establishment of the right to use construction land shall comply with the requirements of resource conservation and ecological and environmental protection, adhere to the provisions of laws and administrative regulations on land use purposes, and shall not impair any established usufruct.

<a id="art-347"></a>第三百四十七条　设立建设用地使用权，可以采取出让或者划拨等方式。  

**Article 347** The right to use construction land may be established by means of transfer or allocation, among others.
For commercial land such as for industry, commerce, tourism, recreation, and commodity housing, as well as where there are two or more prospective land users for the same piece of land, the transfer shall be conducted by means of public bidding, auction, or other public competitive bidding methods.
The establishment of the right to use construction land by means of allocation shall be strictly restricted.
工业、商业、旅游、娱乐和商品住宅等经营性用地以及同一土地有两个以上意向用地者的，应当采取招标、拍卖等公开竞价的方式出让。  
严格限制以划拨方式设立建设用地使用权。

<a id="art-348"></a>第三百四十八条　通过招标、拍卖、协议等出让方式设立建设用地使用权的，当事人应当采用书面形式订立建设用地使用权出让合同。  

**Article 348** Where the right to use construction land is established by means of transfer through bidding, auction, agreement, or the like, the parties shall conclude a contract on the transfer of the right to use construction land in writing.
A contract on the transfer of the right to use construction land generally includes the following clauses:
(1) the names and domiciles of the parties;
(2) the boundaries, area, etc. of the land;
(3) the space occupied by buildings, structures, and auxiliary facilities thereof;
(4) the purposes and planning conditions of the land;
(5) the term of the right to use construction land;
(6) the transfer fee and other expenses and the method of payment;
(7) the methods for dispute resolution.
建设用地使用权出让合同一般包括下列条款：  
（一）当事人的名称和住所；  
（二）土地界址、面积等；  
（三）建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施占用的空间；  
（四）土地用途、规划条件；  
（五）建设用地使用权期限；  
（六）出让金等费用及其支付方式；  
（七）解决争议的方法。

<a id="art-349"></a>第三百四十九条　设立建设用地使用权的，应当向登记机构申请建设用地使用权登记。建设用地使用权自登记时设立。登记机构应当向建设用地使用权人发放权属证书。

**Article 349** To establish the right to use construction land, an application for registration of the right to use construction land shall be made to the registration authority. The right to use construction land shall be established upon registration. The registration authority shall issue a title certificate to the person with the right to use construction land.

<a id="art-350"></a>第三百五十条　建设用地使用权人应当合理利用土地，不得改变土地用途；需要改变土地用途的，应当依法经有关行政主管部门批准。

**Article 350** The person with the right to use construction land shall reasonably utilize the land and shall not change the land use purpose; where it is necessary to change the land use purpose, approval from the relevant administrative department shall be obtained according to law.

<a id="art-351"></a>第三百五十一条　建设用地使用权人应当依照法律规定以及合同约定支付出让金等费用。

**Article 351** The person with the right to use construction land shall pay the transfer fee and other expenses in accordance with the provisions of laws and the contract.

<a id="art-352"></a>第三百五十二条　建设用地使用权人建造的建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施的所有权属于建设用地使用权人，但是有相反证据证明的除外。

**Article 352** The ownership of buildings, structures, and auxiliary facilities thereof constructed by the person with the right to use construction land shall vest in the person with the right to use construction land, unless there is evidence to the contrary.

<a id="art-353"></a>第三百五十三条　建设用地使用权人有权将建设用地使用权转让、互换、出资、赠与或者抵押，但是法律另有规定的除外。

**Article 353** The person with the right to use construction land has the right to transfer, exchange, contribute as capital, donate, or mortgage the right to use construction land, unless otherwise provided by law.

<a id="art-354"></a>第三百五十四条　建设用地使用权转让、互换、出资、赠与或者抵押的，当事人应当采用书面形式订立相应的合同。使用期限由当事人约定，但是不得超过建设用地使用权的剩余期限。

**Article 354** Where the right to use construction land is transferred, exchanged, contributed as capital, donated, or mortgaged, the parties shall conclude the corresponding contract in writing. The term of use shall be agreed upon by the parties, but shall not exceed the remaining term of the right to use construction land.

<a id="art-355"></a>第三百五十五条　建设用地使用权转让、互换、出资或者赠与的，应当向登记机构申请变更登记。

**Article 355** Where the right to use construction land is transferred, exchanged, contributed as capital, or donated, an application for modification registration shall be made to the registration authority.

<a id="art-356"></a>第三百五十六条　建设用地使用权转让、互换、出资或者赠与的，附着于该土地上的建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施一并处分。

**Article 356** Where the right to use construction land is transferred, exchanged, contributed as capital, or donated, the buildings, structures, and auxiliary facilities thereof attached to such land shall be disposed of together.

<a id="art-357"></a>第三百五十七条　建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施转让、互换、出资或者赠与的，该建筑物、构筑物及其附属设施占用范围内的建设用地使用权一并处分。

**Article 357** Where buildings, structures, and auxiliary facilities thereof are transferred, exchanged, contributed as capital, or donated, the right to use construction land within the area occupied by such buildings, structures, and auxiliary facilities shall be disposed of together.

<a id="art-358"></a>第三百五十八条　建设用地使用权期限届满前，因公共利益需要提前收回该土地的，应当依据[本法第二百四十三条](#art-243)的规定对该土地上的房屋以及其他不动产给予补偿，并退还相应的出让金。

**Article 358** Where the land is to be recovered before the expiry of the term of the right to use construction land due to the needs of public interest, compensation shall be given for the houses and other immovable property on such land, and the corresponding transfer fee shall be refunded, in accordance with the provisions of Article 243 of this Code.

<a id="art-359"></a>第三百五十九条　住宅建设用地使用权期限届满的，自动续期。续期费用的缴纳或者减免，依照法律、行政法规的规定办理。  

**Article 359** Upon the expiry of the term of the right to use residential construction land, the term shall be automatically renewed. The payment or reduction or exemption of the renewal fee shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations.
The renewal of the term of the right to use non-residential construction land after its expiry shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of laws. The ownership of the houses and other immovable property on such land shall, if there is an agreement, be determined according to the agreement; if there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, it shall be handled in accordance with the provisions of laws and administrative regulations.
非住宅建设用地使用权期限届满后的续期，依照法律规定办理。该土地上的房屋以及其他不动产的归属，有约定的，按照约定；没有约定或者约定不明确的，依照法律、行政法规的规定办理。

<a id="art-360"></a>第三百六十条　建设用地使用权消灭的，出让人应当及时办理注销登记。登记机构应当收回权属证书。

**Article 360** Where the right to use construction land is extinguished, the transferor shall promptly complete the cancellation registration. The registration authority shall retrieve the title certificate.

<a id="art-361"></a>第三百六十一条　集体所有的土地作为建设用地的，应当依照土地管理的法律规定办理。

**Article 361** Where collectively-owned land is used as construction land, the provisions of the laws and regulations on land administration shall apply.

### 第十三章　宅基地使用权

<a id="art-362"></a>第三百六十二条　宅基地使用权人依法对集体所有的土地享有占有和使用的权利，有权依法利用该土地建造住宅及其附属设施。

**Article 362** A holder of the right to use a homestead shall, in accordance with the law, enjoy the right to possess and use the collectively-owned land, and shall have the right to use such land for building a residence and its auxiliary facilities in accordance with the law.

<a id="art-363"></a>第三百六十三条　宅基地使用权的取得、行使和转让，适用土地管理的法律和国家有关规定。

**Article 363** The acquisition, exercise, and transfer of the right to use a homestead shall be governed by the laws on land administration and the relevant provisions of the State.

<a id="art-364"></a>第三百六十四条　宅基地因自然灾害等原因灭失的，宅基地使用权消灭。对失去宅基地的村民，应当依法重新分配宅基地。

**Article 364** Where a homestead is lost due to natural disasters or other reasons, the right to use the homestead shall extinguish. For villagers who have lost their homesteads, a new homestead shall be allocated to them in accordance with the law.

<a id="art-365"></a>第三百六十五条　已经登记的宅基地使用权转让或者消灭的，应当及时办理变更登记或者注销登记。

**Article 365** Where a registered right to use a homestead is transferred or extinguished, the registration for modification or cancellation shall be handled in a timely manner.

### 第十四章　居住权

<a id="art-366"></a>第三百六十六条　居住权人有权按照合同约定，对他人的住宅享有占有、使用的用益物权，以满足生活居住的需要。

**Article 366** A holder of the right of habitation shall, in accordance with the provisions of the contract, have the right to possess and use another person's residence as a usufruct to satisfy the needs of living and residing.

<a id="art-367"></a>第三百六十七条　设立居住权，当事人应当采用书面形式订立居住权合同。  

**Article 367** To establish a right of habitation, the parties shall conclude a contract on the right of habitation in writing.
A contract on the right of habitation generally includes the following clauses:
(1) the name or title and domicile of the parties;
(2) the location of the residence;
(3) the conditions and requirements for habitation;
(4) the term of the right of habitation; and
(5) the method for dispute resolution.
居住权合同一般包括下列条款：  
（一）当事人的姓名或者名称和住所；  
（二）住宅的位置；  
（三）居住的条件和要求；  
（四）居住权期限；  
（五）解决争议的方法。

<a id="art-368"></a>第三百六十八条　居住权无偿设立，但是当事人另有约定的除外。设立居住权的，应当向登记机构申请居住权登记。居住权自登记时设立。

**Article 368** The right of habitation shall be established gratuitously, unless otherwise agreed by the parties. To establish a right of habitation, the parties shall apply to the registration authority for the registration of the right of habitation. The right of habitation shall be established upon registration.

<a id="art-369"></a>第三百六十九条　居住权不得转让、继承。设立居住权的住宅不得出租，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 369** The right of habitation shall not be transferred or inherited. The residence on which a right of habitation is established shall not be leased, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-370"></a>第三百七十条　居住权期限届满或者居住权人死亡的，居住权消灭。居住权消灭的，应当及时办理注销登记。

**Article 370** The right of habitation shall extinguish upon the expiration of its term or upon the death of the holder of the right of habitation. Where the right of habitation extinguishes, the registration for cancellation shall be handled in a timely manner.

<a id="art-371"></a>第三百七十一条　以遗嘱方式设立居住权的，参照适用本章的有关规定。

**Article 371** Where a right of habitation is established by testament, the relevant provisions of this Chapter shall apply mutatis mutandis.

### 第十五章　地役权

<a id="art-372"></a>第三百七十二条　地役权人有权按照合同约定，利用他人的不动产，以提高自己的不动产的效益。  

**Article 372** A holder of an easement shall, in accordance with the provisions of the contract, have the right to use another person's immovable property for improving the efficiency of his own immovable property.
The immovable property of another person mentioned in the preceding paragraph is the servient land, and his own immovable property is the dominant land.
前款所称他人的不动产为供役地，自己的不动产为需役地。

<a id="art-373"></a>第三百七十三条　设立地役权，当事人应当采用书面形式订立地役权合同。  

**Article 373** To establish an easement, the parties shall conclude a contract on the easement in writing.
A contract on an easement generally includes the following clauses:
(1) the name or title and domicile of the parties;
(2) the location of the servient land and the dominant land;
(3) the purpose and method of use;
(4) the term of the easement;
(5) the fees and method of payment thereof; and
(6) the method for dispute resolution.
地役权合同一般包括下列条款：  
（一）当事人的姓名或者名称和住所；  
（二）供役地和需役地的位置；  
（三）利用目的和方法；  
（四）地役权期限；  
（五）费用及其支付方式；  
（六）解决争议的方法。

<a id="art-374"></a>第三百七十四条　地役权自地役权合同生效时设立。当事人要求登记的，可以向登记机构申请地役权登记；未经登记，不得对抗善意第三人。

**Article 374** An easement shall be established upon the entry into effect of the contract on the easement. Where a party requests registration, an application may be made to the registration authority for registration of the easement; without registration, the easement shall not be set up against a bona fide third party.

<a id="art-375"></a>第三百七十五条　供役地权利人应当按照合同约定，允许地役权人利用其不动产，不得妨害地役权人行使权利。

**Article 375** The holder of the right in the servient land shall, in accordance with the provisions of the contract, allow the holder of the easement to use his immovable property and shall not obstruct the holder of the easement from exercising his right.

<a id="art-376"></a>第三百七十六条　地役权人应当按照合同约定的利用目的和方法利用供役地，尽量减少对供役地权利人物权的限制。

**Article 376** The holder of an easement shall use the servient land in accordance with the purpose and method of use agreed upon in the contract and shall minimize the restrictions on the real rights of the holder of the right in the servient land.

<a id="art-377"></a>第三百七十七条　地役权期限由当事人约定；但是，不得超过土地承包经营权、建设用地使用权等用益物权的剩余期限。

**Article 377** The term of an easement shall be agreed upon by the parties; provided, however, that it shall not exceed the remaining term of usufructs such as the right to land contractual management and the right to use construction land.

<a id="art-378"></a>第三百七十八条　土地所有权人享有地役权或者负担地役权的，设立土地承包经营权、宅基地使用权等用益物权时，该用益物权人继续享有或者负担已经设立的地役权。

**Article 378** Where the owner of the land enjoys an easement or is burdened with an easement, when establishing usufructs such as the right to land contractual management or the right to use a homestead, such usufructuary shall continue to enjoy or be burdened with the easement already established.

<a id="art-379"></a>第三百七十九条　土地上已经设立土地承包经营权、建设用地使用权、宅基地使用权等用益物权的，未经用益物权人同意，土地所有权人不得设立地役权。

**Article 379** Where usufructs such as the right to land contractual management, the right to use construction land, or the right to use a homestead have already been established on the land, the owner of the land shall not establish an easement without the consent of the usufructuary.

<a id="art-380"></a>第三百八十条　地役权不得单独转让。土地承包经营权、建设用地使用权等转让的，地役权一并转让，但是合同另有约定的除外。

**Article 380** An easement shall not be transferred independently. Where the right to land contractual management, the right to use construction land, or the like is transferred, the easement shall be transferred together therewith, unless otherwise agreed in the contract.

<a id="art-381"></a>第三百八十一条　地役权不得单独抵押。土地经营权、建设用地使用权等抵押的，在实现抵押权时，地役权一并转让。

**Article 381** An easement shall not be separately mortgaged. Where the land management right, the right to use construction land, or the like is mortgaged, the easement shall be transferred at the time of realizing the mortgage.

<a id="art-382"></a>第三百八十二条　需役地以及需役地上的土地承包经营权、建设用地使用权等部分转让时，转让部分涉及地役权的，受让人同时享有地役权。

**Article 382** Where the dominant tenement and the land contract management right, the right to use construction land, or the like on the dominant tenement are partially transferred and the transferred part involves an easement, the transferee shall simultaneously enjoy the easement.

<a id="art-383"></a>第三百八十三条　供役地以及供役地上的土地承包经营权、建设用地使用权等部分转让时，转让部分涉及地役权的，地役权对受让人具有法律约束力。

**Article 383** Where the servient tenement and the land contract management right, the right to use construction land, or the like on the servient tenement are partially transferred and the transferred part involves an easement, the easement shall be legally binding on the transferee.

<a id="art-384"></a>第三百八十四条　地役权人有下列情形之一的，供役地权利人有权解除地役权合同，地役权消灭：  

**Article 384** In any of the following circumstances, the owner of the servient tenement shall have the right to rescind the easement contract, and the easement shall be extinguished:
(1) The easement is abused in violation of laws or contractual stipulations;
(2) Where the servient tenement is used for compensation, the fee is not paid after two demands within a reasonable period following the expiration of the agreed payment term.
（一）违反法律规定或者合同约定，滥用地役权；  
（二）有偿利用供役地，约定的付款期限届满后在合理期限内经两次催告未支付费用。

<a id="art-385"></a>第三百八十五条　已经登记的地役权变更、转让或者消灭的，应当及时办理变更登记或者注销登记。  

**Article 385** Where a registered easement is modified, transferred, or extinguished, the modification registration or cancellation registration shall be promptly handled.

Part 4 Security Interests
第四分编　担保物权

### 第十六章　一般规定

<a id="art-386"></a>第三百八十六条　担保物权人在债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现担保物权的情形，依法享有就担保财产优先受偿的权利，但是法律另有规定的除外。

**Article 386** Where the debtor fails to perform an obligation due or the circumstances agreed upon by the parties for realizing the security interest occur, the security interest holder shall have the right to be satisfied with priority from the secured property according to law, unless the law provides otherwise.

<a id="art-387"></a>第三百八十七条　债权人在借贷、买卖等民事活动中，为保障实现其债权，需要担保的，可以依照本法和其他法律的规定设立担保物权。  

**Article 387** In civil activities such as lending or purchase, where a creditor needs security to secure the realization of his or her claim, a security interest may be created in accordance with this Law and other laws.
Where a third person provides security for the debtor to the creditor, the third person may require the debtor to provide a counter-security. The provisions of this Law and other laws shall apply to a counter-security.
第三人为债务人向债权人提供担保的，可以要求债务人提供反担保。反担保适用本法和其他法律的规定。

<a id="art-388"></a>第三百八十八条　设立担保物权，应当依照本法和其他法律的规定订立担保合同。担保合同包括抵押合同、质押合同和其他具有担保功能的合同。担保合同是主债权债务合同的从合同。主债权债务合同无效的，担保合同无效，但是法律另有规定的除外。  

**Article 388** To create a security interest, a security contract shall be concluded in accordance with this Law and other laws. Security contracts include mortgage contracts, pledge contracts, and other contracts with a security function. A security contract is an ancillary contract to the principal claim-debt contract. Where the principal claim-debt contract is void, the security contract shall be void, unless the law provides otherwise.
Where a security contract is confirmed to be void, the debtor, the security provider, and the creditor shall, if at fault, bear the corresponding civil liability according to their respective faults.
担保合同被确认无效后，债务人、担保人、债权人有过错的，应当根据其过错各自承担相应的民事责任。

<a id="art-389"></a>第三百八十九条　担保物权的担保范围包括主债权及其利息、违约金、损害赔偿金、保管担保财产和实现担保物权的费用。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。

**Article 389** The scope of security of a security interest shall include the principal claim and its interest, liquidated damages, damages for loss, costs of storing the secured property, and expenses for realizing the security interest. Where the parties have otherwise agreed, such agreement shall prevail.

<a id="art-390"></a>第三百九十条　担保期间，担保财产毁损、灭失或者被征收等，担保物权人可以就获得的保险金、赔偿金或者补偿金等优先受偿。被担保债权的履行期限未届满的，也可以提存该保险金、赔偿金或者补偿金等。

**Article 390** During the period of security, if the secured property is damaged, destroyed, expropriated, or the like, the security interest holder may be satisfied with priority from the insurance proceeds, damages, compensation, or the like obtained. If the performance period of the secured claim has not yet expired, the insurance proceeds, damages, compensation, or the like may also be deposited.

<a id="art-391"></a>第三百九十一条　第三人提供担保，未经其书面同意，债权人允许债务人转移全部或者部分债务的，担保人不再承担相应的担保责任。

**Article 391** Where a third person provides security, if the creditor permits the debtor to transfer all or part of the debt without the third person's written consent, the security provider shall no longer bear the corresponding security liability.

<a id="art-392"></a>第三百九十二条　被担保的债权既有物的担保又有人的担保的，债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现担保物权的情形，债权人应当按照约定实现债权；没有约定或者约定不明确，债务人自己提供物的担保的，债权人应当先就该物的担保实现债权；第三人提供物的担保的，债权人可以就物的担保实现债权，也可以请求保证人承担保证责任。提供担保的第三人承担担保责任后，有权向债务人追偿。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释.md#art-363" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民事诉讼法》的解释》第363条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 392** Where a secured claim is secured by both a security interest in property and a personal suretyship, and the debtor fails to perform an obligation due or the circumstances agreed upon by the parties for realizing the security interest occur, the creditor shall realize the claim in accordance with the agreement. If there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, and the debtor provides the security interest in property himself or itself, the creditor shall first realize the claim by enforcing the security interest in the property; if a third person provides the security interest in property, the creditor may realize the claim by enforcing the security interest in the property or may demand that the surety assume the suretyship liability. After assuming the security liability, the third person who provided the security shall have the right of recourse against the debtor.

<a id="art-393"></a>第三百九十三条　有下列情形之一的，担保物权消灭：  

**Article 393** A security interest shall be extinguished in any of the following circumstances:
(1) The principal claim is extinguished;
(2) The security interest is realized;
(3) The creditor waives the security interest;
(4) Other circumstances provided by law for the extinction of a security interest.
（一）主债权消灭；  
（二）担保物权实现；  
（三）债权人放弃担保物权；  
（四）法律规定担保物权消灭的其他情形。

### 第十七章　抵押权

#### 第一节　一般抵押权

<a id="art-394"></a>第三百九十四条　为担保债务的履行，债务人或者第三人不转移财产的占有，将该财产抵押给债权人的，债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形，债权人有权就该财产优先受偿。  

**Article 394** To secure the performance of a debt, where the debtor or a third person, without transferring the possession of the property, mortgages the property to the creditor, and the debtor fails to perform an obligation due or the circumstances agreed upon by the parties for realizing the mortgage occur, the creditor shall have the right to be satisfied with priority from such property.
The debtor or third person referred to in the preceding paragraph shall be the mortgagor, the creditor shall be the mortgagee, and the property provided as security shall be the mortgaged property.
前款规定的债务人或者第三人为抵押人，债权人为抵押权人，提供担保的财产为抵押财产。

<a id="art-395"></a>第三百九十五条　债务人或者第三人有权处分的下列财产可以抵押：  

**Article 395** The following property over which the debtor or a third person has the right to dispose of may be mortgaged:
(1) Buildings and other things attached to the land;
(2) The right to use construction land;
(3) The right to use sea areas;
(4) Production equipment, raw materials, semi-finished products, and finished products;
(5) Buildings, ships, and aircraft under construction;
(6) Means of transport;
(7) Other property not prohibited by laws or administrative regulations from being mortgaged.
A mortgagor may mortgage all property listed in the preceding paragraph together.
（一）建筑物和其他土地附着物；  
（二）建设用地使用权；  
（三）海域使用权；  
（四）生产设备、原材料、半成品、产品；  
（五）正在建造的建筑物、船舶、航空器；  
（六）交通运输工具；  
（七）法律、行政法规未禁止抵押的其他财产。  
抵押人可以将前款所列财产一并抵押。

<a id="art-396"></a>第三百九十六条　企业、个体工商户、农业生产经营者可以将现有的以及将有的生产设备、原材料、半成品、产品抵押，债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形，债权人有权就抵押财产确定时的动产优先受偿。

**Article 396** An enterprise, individual industrial or commercial household, or agricultural producer or operator may mortgage its or his existing and after-acquired production equipment, raw materials, semi-finished products, and finished products. Where the debtor fails to perform an obligation due or the circumstances agreed upon by the parties for realizing the mortgage occur, the creditor shall have the right to be satisfied with priority from the movables as identified at the time the mortgaged property is determined.

<a id="art-397"></a>第三百九十七条　以建筑物抵押的，该建筑物占用范围内的建设用地使用权一并抵押。以建设用地使用权抵押的，该土地上的建筑物一并抵押。  

**Article 397** Where a building is mortgaged, the right to use construction land within the area occupied by the building shall be mortgaged together with it. Where the right to use construction land is mortgaged, the buildings on the land shall be mortgaged together with it.
Where the mortgagor fails to mortgage the property together in accordance with the preceding paragraph, the unmortgaged property shall be deemed to be mortgaged together.
抵押人未依据前款规定一并抵押的，未抵押的财产视为一并抵押。

<a id="art-398"></a>第三百九十八条　乡镇、村企业的建设用地使用权不得单独抵押。以乡镇、村企业的厂房等建筑物抵押的，其占用范围内的建设用地使用权一并抵押。

**Article 398** The right to use construction land owned by a township or village enterprise shall not be separately mortgaged. Where a factory building or other building of a township or village enterprise is mortgaged, the right to use construction land within the area occupied by such building shall be mortgaged together with it.

<a id="art-399"></a>第三百九十九条　下列财产不得抵押：  

**Article 399** The following property shall not be mortgaged:
(1) Land ownership;
(2) The right to use collective-owned land, such as residential land, reserved land, and hilly land, unless the law provides that it may be mortgaged;
(3) Educational facilities, medical and health facilities, and other public welfare facilities owned by non-profit legal persons established for public welfare purposes, such as schools, kindergartens, and medical institutions;
(4) Property whose ownership or right to use is unclear or disputed;
(5) Property that has been sealed, seized, or placed under supervision according to law;
(6) Other property that is prohibited by provisions of laws or administrative regulations from being mortgaged.
（一）土地所有权；  
（二）宅基地、自留地、自留山等集体所有土地的使用权，但是法律规定可以抵押的除外；  
（三）学校、幼儿园、医疗机构等为公益目的成立的非营利法人的教育设施、医疗卫生设施和其他公益设施；  
（四）所有权、使用权不明或者有争议的财产；  
（五）依法被查封、扣押、监管的财产；  
（六）法律、行政法规规定不得抵押的其他财产。

<a id="art-400"></a>第四百条　设立抵押权，当事人应当采用书面形式订立抵押合同。  

**Article 400** To create a mortgage, the parties shall conclude a mortgage contract in writing.
A mortgage contract generally includes the following clauses:
(1) The type and amount of the secured claim;
(2) The time limit for the debtor to perform the debt;
(3) The name, quantity, and other information of the mortgaged property;
(4) The scope of the security.
抵押合同一般包括下列条款：  
（一）被担保债权的种类和数额；  
（二）债务人履行债务的期限；  
（三）抵押财产的名称、数量等情况；  
（四）担保的范围。

<a id="art-401"></a>第四百零一条　抵押权人在债务履行期限届满前，与抵押人约定债务人不履行到期债务时抵押财产归债权人所有的，只能依法就抵押财产优先受偿。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》时间效力的若干规定.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》时间效力的若干规定》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-402"></a>第四百零二条　以[本法第三百九十五条](#art-395)第一款第一项至第三项规定的财产或者第五项规定的正在建造的建筑物抵押的，应当办理抵押登记。抵押权自登记时设立。

<a id="art-403"></a>第四百零三条　以动产抵押的，抵押权自抵押合同生效时设立；未经登记，不得对抗善意第三人。

<a id="art-404"></a>第四百零四条　以动产抵押的，不得对抗正常经营活动中已经支付合理价款并取得抵押财产的买受人。

<a id="art-405"></a>第四百零五条　抵押权设立前，抵押财产已经出租并转移占有的，原租赁关系不受该抵押权的影响。

<a id="art-406"></a>第四百零六条　抵押期间，抵押人可以转让抵押财产。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。抵押财产转让的，抵押权不受影响。  
抵押人转让抵押财产的，应当及时通知抵押权人。抵押权人能够证明抵押财产转让可能损害抵押权的，可以请求抵押人将转让所得的价款向抵押权人提前清偿债务或者提存。转让的价款超过债权数额的部分归抵押人所有，不足部分由债务人清偿。

<a id="art-407"></a>第四百零七条　抵押权不得与债权分离而单独转让或者作为其他债权的担保。债权转让的，担保该债权的抵押权一并转让，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。

<a id="art-408"></a>第四百零八条　抵押人的行为足以使抵押财产价值减少的，抵押权人有权请求抵押人停止其行为；抵押财产价值减少的，抵押权人有权请求恢复抵押财产的价值，或者提供与减少的价值相应的担保。抵押人不恢复抵押财产的价值，也不提供担保的，抵押权人有权请求债务人提前清偿债务。

<a id="art-409"></a>第四百零九条　抵押权人可以放弃抵押权或者抵押权的顺位。抵押权人与抵押人可以协议变更抵押权顺位以及被担保的债权数额等内容。但是，抵押权的变更未经其他抵押权人书面同意的，不得对其他抵押权人产生不利影响。  
债务人以自己的财产设定抵押，抵押权人放弃该抵押权、抵押权顺位或者变更抵押权的，其他担保人在抵押权人丧失优先受偿权益的范围内免除担保责任，但是其他担保人承诺仍然提供担保的除外。

<a id="art-410"></a>第四百一十条　债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形，抵押权人可以与抵押人协议以抵押财产折价或者以拍卖、变卖该抵押财产所得的价款优先受偿。协议损害其他债权人利益的，其他债权人可以请求人民法院撤销该协议。  

**Article 410** If the debtor fails to perform its due obligation or any circumstance agreed upon by the parties for the enforcement of the mortgage right occurs, the mortgagee may agree with the mortgagor on converting the mortgaged property into money or use the proceeds from the auction or sale of the mortgaged property to be compensated preferentially. If such an agreement prejudices the interests of other creditors, such other creditors may request the People's Court to revoke the agreement.
If the mortgagee and the mortgagor fail to reach an agreement on the method of enforcing the mortgage right, the mortgagee may request the People's Court to auction or sell the mortgaged property.
Where the mortgaged property is converted into money or sold, reference shall be made to the market price.
抵押权人与抵押人未就抵押权实现方式达成协议的，抵押权人可以请求人民法院拍卖、变卖抵押财产。  
抵押财产折价或者变卖的，应当参照市场价格。

<a id="art-411"></a>第四百一十一条　依据[本法第三百九十六条](#art-396)规定设定抵押的，抵押财产自下列情形之一发生时确定：  

**Article 411** Where a mortgage is created in accordance with Article 396 of this Code, the mortgaged property shall be determined upon the occurrence of any of the following circumstances:
(1) The time limit for the performance of the obligation expires and the claim is not satisfied;
(2) The mortgagor is declared bankrupt or dissolved;
(3) The circumstance agreed upon by the parties for the enforcement of the mortgage right occurs; or
(4) Any other circumstance that materially affects the satisfaction of the claim.
（一）债务履行期限届满，债权未实现；  
（二）抵押人被宣告破产或者解散；  
（三）当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形；  
（四）严重影响债权实现的其他情形。

<a id="art-412"></a>第四百一十二条　债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形，致使抵押财产被人民法院依法扣押的，自扣押之日起，抵押权人有权收取该抵押财产的天然孳息或者法定孳息，但是抵押权人未通知应当清偿法定孳息义务人的除外。  

**Article 412** If, due to the debtor's failure to perform its due obligation or the occurrence of a circumstance agreed upon by the parties for the enforcement of the mortgage right, the mortgaged property is seized by the People's Court according to law, from the date of the seizure, the mortgagee shall have the right to collect the natural fruits or legal fruits of the mortgaged property, unless the mortgagee has not notified the person obliged to pay the legal fruits.
The fruits specified in the preceding paragraph shall first be applied to cover the expenses of collecting such fruits.
前款规定的孳息应当先充抵收取孳息的费用。

<a id="art-413"></a>第四百一十三条　抵押财产折价或者拍卖、变卖后，其价款超过债权数额的部分归抵押人所有，不足部分由债务人清偿。

**Article 413** After the mortgaged property has been converted into money, auctioned, or sold, the portion of the proceeds exceeding the amount of the claim shall belong to the mortgagor, and the shortfall shall be satisfied by the debtor.

<a id="art-414"></a>第四百一十四条　同一财产向两个以上债权人抵押的，拍卖、变卖抵押财产所得的价款依照下列规定清偿：  

**Article 414** Where the same property is mortgaged to two or more creditors, the proceeds from the auction or sale of the mortgaged property shall be applied to satisfy the claims in accordance with the following provisions:
(1) Where the mortgage rights have been registered, the order of satisfaction shall be determined by the chronological order of registration;
(2) Registered mortgage rights shall be satisfied before unregistered ones; and
(3) Unregistered mortgage rights shall be satisfied in proportion to the amounts of their respective claims.
For other security interests that may be registered, the order of satisfaction shall be determined by reference to the provisions of the preceding paragraph.
（一）抵押权已经登记的，按照登记的时间先后确定清偿顺序；  
（二）抵押权已经登记的先于未登记的受偿；  
（三）抵押权未登记的，按照债权比例清偿。  
其他可以登记的担保物权，清偿顺序参照适用前款规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/公司与破产/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国企业破产法》若干问题的规定（三）.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国企业破产法》若干问题的规定（三）》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-415"></a>第四百一十五条　同一财产既设立抵押权又设立质权的，拍卖、变卖该财产所得的价款按照登记、交付的时间先后确定清偿顺序。

**Article 415** Where both a mortgage right and a pledge right are created on the same property, the order of satisfaction from the proceeds of the auction or sale of such property shall be determined by the chronological order of the registration or delivery.

<a id="art-416"></a>第四百一十六条　动产抵押担保的主债权是抵押物的价款，标的物交付后十日内办理抵押登记的，该抵押权人优先于抵押物买受人的其他担保物权人受偿，但是留置权人除外。

**Article 416** Where the principal claim secured by a chattel mortgage is the purchase price of the mortgaged property, and the mortgage is registered within ten days after the delivery of the subject matter, the mortgagee shall be entitled to satisfaction in priority over other security interest holders of the buyer of the mortgaged property, except for lien holders.

<a id="art-417"></a>第四百一十七条　建设用地使用权抵押后，该土地上新增的建筑物不属于抵押财产。该建设用地使用权实现抵押权时，应当将该土地上新增的建筑物与建设用地使用权一并处分。但是，新增建筑物所得的价款，抵押权人无权优先受偿。

**Article 417** After the creation of a mortgage on the right to use construction land, newly constructed buildings on that land shall not be part of the mortgaged property. Where the mortgage right over the said right to use construction land is enforced, the newly constructed buildings on that land shall be disposed of together with the right to use construction land. However, the mortgagee shall have no right to be compensated preferentially from the proceeds attributable to the newly constructed buildings.

<a id="art-418"></a>第四百一十八条　以集体所有土地的使用权依法抵押的，实现抵押权后，未经法定程序，不得改变土地所有权的性质和土地用途。

**Article 418** Where the right to use collectively owned land is mortgaged according to law, after the enforcement of the mortgage right, the nature of the land ownership and the purpose of the land shall not be changed without the statutory procedure.

<a id="art-419"></a>第四百一十九条　抵押权人应当在主债权诉讼时效期间行使抵押权；未行使的，人民法院不予保护。

**Article 419** A mortgagee shall exercise its mortgage right within the period of prescription for the principal claim; if it fails to do so, the People's Court shall not provide protection.

#### 第二节　最高额抵押权

<a id="art-420"></a>第四百二十条　为担保债务的履行，债务人或者第三人对一定期间内将要连续发生的债权提供担保财产的，债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现抵押权的情形，抵押权人有权在最高债权额限度内就该担保财产优先受偿。  

**Article 420** To secure the performance of debts, if a debtor or a third party provides a security property for claims that will arise continuously within a certain period, and the debtor fails to perform its due obligation or any circumstance agreed upon by the parties for the enforcement of the mortgage right occurs, the mortgagee shall have the right to be compensated preferentially from such security property up to the maximum amount of the claim.
Claims existing before the creation of a maximum amount mortgage may, with the consent of the parties, be included within the scope of the claims covered by the maximum amount mortgage.
最高额抵押权设立前已经存在的债权，经当事人同意，可以转入最高额抵押担保的债权范围。

<a id="art-421"></a>第四百二十一条　最高额抵押担保的债权确定前，部分债权转让的，最高额抵押权不得转让，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 421** If, before the claims secured by a maximum mortgage are determined, part of the claims are assigned, the maximum mortgage right shall not be assigned, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-422"></a>第四百二十二条　最高额抵押担保的债权确定前，抵押权人与抵押人可以通过协议变更债权确定的期间、债权范围以及最高债权额。但是，变更的内容不得对其他抵押权人产生不利影响。

**Article 422** Before the claims secured by a maximum mortgage are determined, the mortgagee and the mortgagor may, by agreement, modify the period for determining the claims, the scope of the claims, and the maximum amount of the claims. However, the modification shall not adversely affect other mortgagees.

<a id="art-423"></a>第四百二十三条　有下列情形之一的，抵押权人的债权确定：  

**Article 423** The claims of the mortgagee shall be determined under any of the following circumstances:
(1) the agreed period for determining the claims expires;
(2) if no period for determining the claims is agreed or the agreement is unclear, the mortgagee or the mortgagor requests the determination of the claims two years after the establishment of the maximum mortgage right;
(3) it is impossible for new claims to arise;
(4) the mortgagee knows or should know that the mortgaged property has been sealed up or distrained;
(5) the debtor or the mortgagor is declared bankrupt or dissolved; or
(6) other circumstances under which the claims are determined as prescribed by law.
（一）约定的债权确定期间届满；  
（二）没有约定债权确定期间或者约定不明确，抵押权人或者抵押人自最高额抵押权设立之日起满二年后请求确定债权；  
（三）新的债权不可能发生；  
（四）抵押权人知道或者应当知道抵押财产被查封、扣押；  
（五）债务人、抵押人被宣告破产或者解散；  
（六）法律规定债权确定的其他情形。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-30" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第30条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-424"></a>第四百二十四条　最高额抵押权除适用本节规定外，适用本章第一节的有关规定。

**Article 424** In addition to the provisions of this Section, the provisions of Section 1 of this Chapter shall apply to maximum mortgage rights.

### 第十八章　质权

#### 第一节　动产质权

<a id="art-425"></a>第四百二十五条　为担保债务的履行，债务人或者第三人将其动产出质给债权人占有的，债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现质权的情形，债权人有权就该动产优先受偿。  

**Article 425** To secure the performance of an obligation, if the debtor or a third party pledges his or its chattel to the creditor for possession, and the debtor fails to perform the due obligation or the circumstances agreed upon by the parties for the enforcement of the pledge right occur, the creditor shall have the right to be paid with priority out of the proceeds from that chattel.
The debtor or third party as specified in the preceding paragraph is the pledgor, the creditor is the pledgee, and the chattel delivered is the pledged property.
前款规定的债务人或者第三人为出质人，债权人为质权人，交付的动产为质押财产。

<a id="art-426"></a>第四百二十六条　法律、行政法规禁止转让的动产不得出质。

**Article 426** Chattels the transfer of which is prohibited by laws or administrative regulations shall not be pledged.

<a id="art-427"></a>第四百二十七条　设立质权，当事人应当采用书面形式订立质押合同。  

**Article 427** To create a pledge right, the parties shall enter into a pledge contract in writing.
A pledge contract generally includes the following clauses:
(1) the type and amount of the secured claim;
(2) the time limit for the debtor to perform the obligation;
(3) the name, quantity, and other information of the pledged property;
(4) the scope of the security; and
(5) the time and method of delivery of the pledged property.
质押合同一般包括下列条款：  
（一）被担保债权的种类和数额；  
（二）债务人履行债务的期限；  
（三）质押财产的名称、数量等情况；  
（四）担保的范围；  
（五）质押财产交付的时间、方式。

<a id="art-428"></a>第四百二十八条　质权人在债务履行期限届满前，与出质人约定债务人不履行到期债务时质押财产归债权人所有的，只能依法就质押财产优先受偿。

**Article 428** If, before the time limit for the performance of the obligation expires, the pledgee agrees with the pledgor that the pledged property shall vest in the creditor if the debtor fails to perform the due obligation, the pledgee may only, according to law, seek priority payment out of the proceeds from the pledged property.

<a id="art-429"></a>第四百二十九条　质权自出质人交付质押财产时设立。

**Article 429** A pledge right is created upon delivery of the pledged property by the pledgor.

<a id="art-430"></a>第四百三十条　质权人有权收取质押财产的孳息，但是合同另有约定的除外。  

**Article 430** The pledgee shall have the right to collect the fruits of the pledged property, unless the contract provides otherwise.
The fruits as specified in the preceding paragraph shall first be used to offset the expenses for collecting the fruits.
前款规定的孳息应当先充抵收取孳息的费用。

<a id="art-431"></a>第四百三十一条　质权人在质权存续期间，未经出质人同意，擅自使用、处分质押财产，造成出质人损害的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 431** If, during the existence of the pledge right, the pledgee uses or disposes of the pledged property without the consent of the pledgor, causing damage to the pledgor, the pledgee shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-432"></a>第四百三十二条　质权人负有妥善保管质押财产的义务；因保管不善致使质押财产毁损、灭失的，应当承担赔偿责任。  

**Article 432** The pledgee is under the obligation to properly keep the pledged property; if the pledged property is damaged or lost due to improper keeping, the pledgee shall bear compensation liability.
If the pledgee's act may cause the pledged property to be damaged or lost, the pledgor may request the pledgee to deposit the pledged property, or request the advance discharge of the obligation and the return of the pledged property.
质权人的行为可能使质押财产毁损、灭失的，出质人可以请求质权人将质押财产提存，或者请求提前清偿债务并返还质押财产。

<a id="art-433"></a>第四百三十三条　因不可归责于质权人的事由可能使质押财产毁损或者价值明显减少，足以危害质权人权利的，质权人有权请求出质人提供相应的担保；出质人不提供的，质权人可以拍卖、变卖质押财产，并与出质人协议将拍卖、变卖所得的价款提前清偿债务或者提存。

**Article 433** If, due to a cause not attributable to the pledgee, the pledged property is likely to be damaged or its value is likely to decrease obviously, thus sufficiently endangering the pledgee's rights, the pledgee shall have the right to request the pledgor to provide corresponding security; if the pledgor fails to provide such security, the pledgee may auction or sell off the pledged property, and agree with the pledgor to use the proceeds from the auction or sale to advance discharge the obligation or to deposit the proceeds.

<a id="art-434"></a>第四百三十四条　质权人在质权存续期间，未经出质人同意转质，造成质押财产毁损、灭失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 434** If, during the existence of the pledge right, the pledgee repledges the pledged property without the consent of the pledgor, causing damage or loss to the pledged property, the pledgee shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-435"></a>第四百三十五条　质权人可以放弃质权。债务人以自己的财产出质，质权人放弃该质权的，其他担保人在质权人丧失优先受偿权益的范围内免除担保责任，但是其他担保人承诺仍然提供担保的除外。

**Article 435** The pledgee may abandon the pledge right. If the debtor pledges his own property and the pledgee abandons the pledge right, other guarantors shall be released from the guaranty liability within the scope of the pledgee's loss of the right to priority payment, unless the other guarantors commit to still provide the guaranty.

<a id="art-436"></a>第四百三十六条　债务人履行债务或者出质人提前清偿所担保的债权的，质权人应当返还质押财产。  

**Article 436** If the debtor performs the obligation or the pledgor advances the discharge of the secured claim, the pledgee shall return the pledged property.
If the debtor fails to perform the due obligation or the circumstances agreed upon by the parties for the enforcement of the pledge right occur, the pledgee may agree with the pledgor to convert the pledged property into money, or seek priority payment out of the proceeds from the auction or sale of the pledged property.
If the pledged property is converted into money or sold, it shall be valued by reference to the market price.
债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的实现质权的情形，质权人可以与出质人协议以质押财产折价，也可以就拍卖、变卖质押财产所得的价款优先受偿。  
质押财产折价或者变卖的，应当参照市场价格。

<a id="art-437"></a>第四百三十七条　出质人可以请求质权人在债务履行期限届满后及时行使质权；质权人不行使的，出质人可以请求人民法院拍卖、变卖质押财产。  

**Article 437** The pledgor may request the pledgee to enforce the pledge right promptly after the time limit for the performance of the obligation expires; if the pledgee fails to do so, the pledgor may request the People's Court to auction or sell off the pledged property.
If the pledgor requests the pledgee to enforce the pledge right promptly, and the pledgee's failure to diligently enforce the right causes damage to the pledgor, the pledgee shall bear compensation liability.
出质人请求质权人及时行使质权，因质权人怠于行使权利造成出质人损害的，由质权人承担赔偿责任。

<a id="art-438"></a>第四百三十八条　质押财产折价或者拍卖、变卖后，其价款超过债权数额的部分归出质人所有，不足部分由债务人清偿。

**Article 438** After the pledged property is converted into money, auctioned, or sold, the portion of the proceeds exceeding the amount of the claim shall belong to the pledgor and the shortfall shall be paid by the debtor.

<a id="art-439"></a>第四百三十九条　出质人与质权人可以协议设立最高额质权。  

**Article 439** The pledgor and the pledgee may agree to create a maximum pledge right.
In addition to the relevant provisions of this Section, the relevant provisions of Section 2 of Chapter 17 of this Book shall apply mutatis mutandis to maximum pledge rights.
最高额质权除适用本节有关规定外，参照适用本编第十七章第二节的有关规定。

#### 第二节　权利质权

<a id="art-440"></a>第四百四十条　债务人或者第三人有权处分的下列权利可以出质：  

**Article 440** The following rights that the debtor or a third party has the right to dispose of may be pledged:
(1) drafts, promissory notes, and checks;
(2) bonds and certificates of deposit;
(3) warehouse receipts and bills of lading;
(4) transferable fund units and equities;
(5) property rights in intellectual property such as transferable rights to exclusively use a registered trademark, patent rights, and copyrights;
(6) existing and future accounts receivable; and
(7) other property rights that may be pledged as prescribed by laws or administrative regulations.
（一）汇票、本票、支票；  
（二）债券、存款单；  
（三）仓单、提单；  
（四）可以转让的基金份额、股权；  
（五）可以转让的注册商标专用权、专利权、著作权等知识产权中的财产权；  
（六）现有的以及将有的应收账款；  
（七）法律、行政法规规定可以出质的其他财产权利。

<a id="art-441"></a>第四百四十一条　以汇票、本票、支票、债券、存款单、仓单、提单出质的，质权自权利凭证交付质权人时设立；没有权利凭证的，质权自办理出质登记时设立。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 441** Where a bill of exchange, promissory note, cheque, bond, certificate of deposit, warehouse receipt, or bill of lading is pledged, the pledge right shall be established at the time when the document of title is delivered to the pledgee; if there is no document of title, the pledge right shall be established at the time when the pledge registration is made. Where any law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-442"></a>第四百四十二条　汇票、本票、支票、债券、存款单、仓单、提单的兑现日期或者提货日期先于主债权到期的，质权人可以兑现或者提货，并与出质人协议将兑现的价款或者提取的货物提前清偿债务或者提存。

**Article 442** Where the date for cashing or taking delivery of a bill of exchange, promissory note, cheque, bond, certificate of deposit, warehouse receipt, or bill of lading falls before the due date of the principal claim, the pledgee may cash or take delivery of the goods, and shall reach an agreement with the pledgor to apply the proceeds of cashing or the goods taken delivery of to pay off the debt ahead of schedule or to deposit them.

<a id="art-443"></a>第四百四十三条　以基金份额、股权出质的，质权自办理出质登记时设立。  

**Article 443** Where fund units or stock rights are pledged, the pledge right shall be established at the time when the pledge registration is made.
After fund units or stock rights are pledged, they shall not be transferred, unless otherwise agreed by the pledgor and the pledgee through consultation. The proceeds obtained by the pledgor from the transfer of fund units or stock rights shall be used to pay off the debt to the pledgee ahead of schedule or be deposited.
基金份额、股权出质后，不得转让，但是出质人与质权人协商同意的除外。出质人转让基金份额、股权所得的价款，应当向质权人提前清偿债务或者提存。

<a id="art-444"></a>第四百四十四条　以注册商标专用权、专利权、著作权等知识产权中的财产权出质的，质权自办理出质登记时设立。  

**Article 444** Where property rights in intellectual property, such as the right to exclusive use of a registered trademark, patent right, or copyright, are pledged, the pledge right shall be established at the time when the pledge registration is made.
After the property rights in intellectual property are pledged, the pledgor shall not transfer or license others to use them, unless otherwise agreed by the pledgor and the pledgee through consultation. The proceeds obtained by the pledgor from the transfer of or licensing of others to use the pledged property rights in intellectual property shall be used to pay off the debt to the pledgee ahead of schedule or be deposited.
知识产权中的财产权出质后，出质人不得转让或者许可他人使用，但是出质人与质权人协商同意的除外。出质人转让或者许可他人使用出质的知识产权中的财产权所得的价款，应当向质权人提前清偿债务或者提存。

<a id="art-445"></a>第四百四十五条　以应收账款出质的，质权自办理出质登记时设立。  

**Article 445** Where an accounts receivable is pledged, the pledge right shall be established at the time when the pledge registration is made.
After an accounts receivable is pledged, it shall not be transferred, unless otherwise agreed by the pledgor and the pledgee through consultation. The proceeds obtained by the pledgor from the transfer of the accounts receivable shall be used to pay off the debt to the pledgee ahead of schedule or be deposited.
应收账款出质后，不得转让，但是出质人与质权人协商同意的除外。出质人转让应收账款所得的价款，应当向质权人提前清偿债务或者提存。

<a id="art-446"></a>第四百四十六条　权利质权除适用本节规定外，适用本章第一节的有关规定。

**Article 446** In addition to the provisions of this section, the relevant provisions of Section 1 of this Chapter shall apply to the pledge of rights.

### 第十九章　留置权

<a id="art-447"></a>第四百四十七条　债务人不履行到期债务，债权人可以留置已经合法占有的债务人的动产，并有权就该动产优先受偿。  

**Article 447** Where a debtor fails to perform his due obligation, the creditor may retain the debtor's movables which are already in the creditor's lawful possession, and shall have the right to be paid in priority from such movables.
The creditor mentioned in the preceding paragraph is the lienholder, and the movables in possession are the retained property.
前款规定的债权人为留置权人，占有的动产为留置财产。

<a id="art-448"></a>第四百四十八条　债权人留置的动产，应当与债权属于同一法律关系，但是企业之间留置的除外。

**Article 448** The movables retained by the creditor shall be in the same legal relationship as the claim, unless the retention is between enterprises.

<a id="art-449"></a>第四百四十九条　法律规定或者当事人约定不得留置的动产，不得留置。

**Article 149** Any movables which, as provided by law or agreed by the parties, shall not be retained, shall not be retained.

<a id="art-450"></a>第四百五十条　留置财产为可分物的，留置财产的价值应当相当于债务的金额。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-38" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第38条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 450** Where the retained property is a divisible thing, the value of the retained property shall be equivalent to the amount of the debt.

<a id="art-451"></a>第四百五十一条　留置权人负有妥善保管留置财产的义务；因保管不善致使留置财产毁损、灭失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 451** The lienholder shall be obligated to properly keep the retained property; if the retained property is damaged or lost due to improper custody, the lienholder shall bear the liability for compensation.

<a id="art-452"></a>第四百五十二条　留置权人有权收取留置财产的孳息。  

**Article 452** The lienholder shall have the right to collect the fruits of the retained property.
The fruits as mentioned in the preceding paragraph shall be used first to offset the expenses for collecting the fruits.
前款规定的孳息应当先充抵收取孳息的费用。

<a id="art-453"></a>第四百五十三条　留置权人与债务人应当约定留置财产后的债务履行期限；没有约定或者约定不明确的，留置权人应当给债务人六十日以上履行债务的期限，但是鲜活易腐等不易保管的动产除外。债务人逾期未履行的，留置权人可以与债务人协议以留置财产折价，也可以就拍卖、变卖留置财产所得的价款优先受偿。  

**Article 453** The lienholder and the debtor shall agree on the time limit for performance of the debt after retention; if there is no such agreement or the agreement is unclear, the lienholder shall give the debtor a time limit of not less than sixty days for performing the debt, except for perishable or otherwise difficult-to-preserve movables. If the debtor fails to perform within the time limit, the lienholder may reach an agreement with the debtor to set off the retained property against the debt, and may also be paid in priority from the proceeds obtained from the auction or sale of the retained property.
If the retained property is set off against the debt or sold, the price shall be determined by reference to the market price.
留置财产折价或者变卖的，应当参照市场价格。

<a id="art-454"></a>第四百五十四条　债务人可以请求留置权人在债务履行期限届满后行使留置权；留置权人不行使的，债务人可以请求人民法院拍卖、变卖留置财产。

**Article 454** The debtor may request the lienholder to exercise the lien after the time limit for performance of the debt has expired; if the lienholder fails to do so, the debtor may request the People's Court to auction or sell the retained property.

<a id="art-455"></a>第四百五十五条　留置财产折价或者拍卖、变卖后，其价款超过债权数额的部分归债务人所有，不足部分由债务人清偿。

**Article 455** After the retained property is set off against the debt or auctioned or sold, any sum of the proceeds in excess of the amount of the claim shall belong to the debtor, and any deficiency shall be paid by the debtor.

<a id="art-456"></a>第四百五十六条　同一动产上已经设立抵押权或者质权，该动产又被留置的，留置权人优先受偿。

**Article 456** Where a mortgage or pledge right has already been established on the same movables, and the movables are later retained, the lienholder has the priority to be paid.

<a id="art-457"></a>第四百五十七条　留置权人对留置财产丧失占有或者留置权人接受债务人另行提供担保的，留置权消灭。  

**Article 457** If the lienholder loses possession of the retained property or the lienholder accepts alternative security provided by the debtor, the lien shall be extinguished.
Part V: Possession
第五分编　占　　有

### 第二十章　占有

<a id="art-458"></a>第四百五十八条　基于合同关系等产生的占有，有关不动产或者动产的使用、收益、违约责任等，按照合同约定；合同没有约定或者约定不明确的，依照有关法律规定。

**Article 458** For possession arising from a contractual relationship or the like, matters such as the use of, proceeds from, and liability for breach of contract in respect of the immovable or movable shall be governed by the contract; if the contract has no such provision or the provision is unclear, they shall be governed by the relevant laws.

<a id="art-459"></a>第四百五十九条　占有人因使用占有的不动产或者动产，致使该不动产或者动产受到损害的，恶意占有人应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 459** If the possessor causes damage to the immovable or movable in possession during its use, a possessor in bad faith shall bear the liability for compensation.

<a id="art-460"></a>第四百六十条　不动产或者动产被占有人占有的，权利人可以请求返还原物及其孳息；但是，应当支付善意占有人因维护该不动产或者动产支出的必要费用。

**Article 460** Where an immovable or movable is possessed by another person, the right holder may demand the return of the original thing and its fruits; provided that the right holder shall pay the necessary expenses incurred by a possessor in good faith for maintaining the immovable or movable.

<a id="art-461"></a>第四百六十一条　占有的不动产或者动产毁损、灭失，该不动产或者动产的权利人请求赔偿的，占有人应当将因毁损、灭失取得的保险金、赔偿金或者补偿金等返还给权利人；权利人的损害未得到足够弥补的，恶意占有人还应当赔偿损失。

**Article 461** Where the immovable or movable property in possession is damaged or lost, and the right holder of such immovable or movable property requests compensation, the possessor shall return the insurance proceeds, damages, or compensation, etc. obtained due to such damage or loss to the right holder; if the damage to the right holder is not sufficiently compensated, the malicious possessor shall also compensate for the loss.

<a id="art-462"></a>第四百六十二条　占有的不动产或者动产被侵占的，占有人有权请求返还原物；对妨害占有的行为，占有人有权请求排除妨害或者消除危险；因侵占或者妨害造成损害的，占有人有权依法请求损害赔偿。  

**Article 462** Where the immovable or movable property in possession is encroached upon, the possessor has the right to request the return of the original property; where the possession is obstructed, the possessor has the right to request the removal of the obstruction or the elimination of the danger; where damage is caused by encroachment or obstruction, the possessor has the right to request damages according to law.
Where the possessor's right to request the return of the original property is not exercised within one year from the date of the encroachment, such right is extinguished.
占有人返还原物的请求权，自侵占发生之日起一年内未行使的，该请求权消灭。

## 第三编　合同

### 第一章　一般规定

<a id="art-463"></a>第四百六十三条　本编调整因合同产生的民事关系。

**Article 463** This Book regulates civil relations arising from contracts.

<a id="art-464"></a>第四百六十四条　合同是民事主体之间设立、变更、终止民事法律关系的协议。  

**Article 464** A contract is an agreement between civil subjects to establish, modify, or terminate a civil legal relationship.
Agreements concerning personal status relations such as marriage, adoption, and guardianship shall be governed by the legal provisions relating to such personal status relations; in the absence of such provisions, the provisions of this Book may be applied by reference based on their nature.
婚姻、收养、监护等有关身份关系的协议，适用有关该身份关系的法律规定；没有规定的，可以根据其性质参照适用本编规定。

<a id="art-465"></a>第四百六十五条　依法成立的合同，受法律保护。  

**Article 465** A contract legally formed is protected by law.
A contract legally formed is legally binding only on the parties thereto, unless otherwise provided by law.
依法成立的合同，仅对当事人具有法律约束力，但是法律另有规定的除外。

<a id="art-466"></a>第四百六十六条　当事人对合同条款的理解有争议的，应当依据[本法第一百四十二条](#art-142)第一款的规定，确定争议条款的含义。  

**Article 466** Where the parties dispute the understanding of a contract term, the meaning of the disputed term shall be determined in accordance with the provisions of Paragraph 1 of Article 142 of this Law.
Where a contract is concluded in two or more languages and it is agreed that all versions are equally authentic, the words and phrases used in each version are presumed to have the same meaning. Where the words and phrases used in the different versions are inconsistent, interpretation shall be made in light of the relevant terms, nature, and purpose of the contract, as well as the principle of good faith.
合同文本采用两种以上文字订立并约定具有同等效力的，对各文本使用的词句推定具有相同含义。各文本使用的词句不一致的，应当根据合同的相关条款、性质、目的以及诚信原则等予以解释。

<a id="art-467"></a>第四百六十七条　本法或者其他法律没有明文规定的合同，适用本编通则的规定，并可以参照适用本编或者其他法律最相类似合同的规定。  

**Article 467** For contracts not explicitly provided for in this Law or other laws, the provisions of the General Principles of this Book shall apply, and the provisions of this Book or other laws on the most similar contract may be applied by reference.
Contracts for Sino-foreign equity joint ventures, Sino-foreign contractual joint ventures, and Sino-foreign cooperative exploration and exploitation of natural resources that are performed within the territory of the People's Republic of China shall be governed by the laws of the People's Republic of China.
在中华人民共和国境内履行的中外合资经营企业合同、中外合作经营企业合同、中外合作勘探开发自然资源合同，适用中华人民共和国法律。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理买卖合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释.md#art-32" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理买卖合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释》第32条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-468"></a>第四百六十八条　非因合同产生的债权债务关系，适用有关该债权债务关系的法律规定；没有规定的，适用本编通则的有关规定，但是根据其性质不能适用的除外。

**Article 468** For a relationship of creditor's rights and debts not arising from a contract, the legal provisions relating to such relationship of creditor's rights and debts shall apply; in the absence of such provisions, the relevant provisions of the General Principles of this Book shall apply, unless they cannot be applied due to the nature of the relationship.

### 第二章　合同的订立

<a id="art-469"></a>第四百六十九条　当事人订立合同，可以采用书面形式、口头形式或者其他形式。  

**Article 469** The parties may conclude a contract in writing, orally, or in other forms.
A writing means any form that can tangibly represent the content contained therein, such as a contract instrument, letter, telegram, telex, or facsimile.
A data message that can tangibly represent the content contained therein and be accessed for reference at any time, such as through electronic data interchange or email, is deemed to be a writing.
书面形式是合同书、信件、电报、电传、传真等可以有形地表现所载内容的形式。  
以电子数据交换、电子邮件等方式能够有形地表现所载内容，并可以随时调取查用的数据电文，视为书面形式。

<a id="art-470"></a>第四百七十条　合同的内容由当事人约定，一般包括下列条款：  

**Article 470** The content of a contract shall be agreed upon by the parties and generally includes the following clauses:
(1) Names or titles and domiciles of the parties;
(2) Object;
(3) Quantity;
(4) Quality;
(5) Price or remuneration;
(6) Time, place, and method of performance;
(7) Liability for breach of contract; and
(8) Methods for dispute resolution.
The parties may conclude a contract by reference to model texts for various types of contracts.
（一）当事人的姓名或者名称和住所；  
（二）标的；  
（三）数量；  
（四）质量；  
（五）价款或者报酬；  
（六）履行期限、地点和方式；  
（七）违约责任；  
（八）解决争议的方法。  
当事人可以参照各类合同的示范文本订立合同。

<a id="art-471"></a>第四百七十一条　当事人订立合同，可以采取要约、承诺方式或者其他方式。

**Article 471** The parties may conclude a contract through an offer, acceptance, or other methods.

<a id="art-472"></a>第四百七十二条　要约是希望与他人订立合同的意思表示，该意思表示应当符合下列条件：  

**Article 472** An offer is a manifestation of intention to conclude a contract with another person, which shall satisfy the following conditions:
(1) Its content is specific and definite; and
(2) It indicates that the offeror will be bound by the manifestation of intention upon acceptance by the offeree.
（一）内容具体确定；  
（二）表明经受要约人承诺，要约人即受该意思表示约束。

<a id="art-473"></a>第四百七十三条　要约邀请是希望他人向自己发出要约的表示。拍卖公告、招标公告、招股说明书、债券募集办法、基金招募说明书、商业广告和宣传、寄送的价目表等为要约邀请。  

**Article 473** An invitation to offer is a manifestation of intention to expect another person to make an offer to oneself. Auction announcements, tender announcements, prospectuses, bond offering documentation, fund prospectuses, commercial advertisements and promotions, and price lists sent are invitations to offer.
Where the content of a commercial advertisement or promotion meets the conditions of an offer, it constitutes an offer.
商业广告和宣传的内容符合要约条件的，构成要约。

<a id="art-474"></a>第四百七十四条　要约生效的时间适用[本法第一百三十七条](#art-137)的规定。

**Article 474** The time when an offer becomes effective shall be governed by the provisions of Article 137 of this Law.

<a id="art-475"></a>第四百七十五条　要约可以撤回。要约的撤回适用[本法第一百四十一条](#art-141)的规定。

**Article 475** An offer may be withdrawn. The withdrawal of an offer shall be governed by the provisions of Article 141 of this Law.

<a id="art-476"></a>第四百七十六条　要约可以撤销，但是有下列情形之一的除外：  

**Article 476** An offer may be revoked, except under any of the following circumstances:
(1) The offeror has expressly indicated that the offer is irrevocable by specifying a time for acceptance or otherwise; or
(2) The offeree has reason to believe that the offer is irrevocable and has made reasonable preparations for performing the contract.
（一）要约人以确定承诺期限或者其他形式明示要约不可撤销；  
（二）受要约人有理由认为要约是不可撤销的，并已经为履行合同做了合理准备工作。

<a id="art-477"></a>第四百七十七条　撤销要约的意思表示以对话方式作出的，该意思表示的内容应当在受要约人作出承诺之前为受要约人所知道；撤销要约的意思表示以非对话方式作出的，应当在受要约人作出承诺之前到达受要约人。

**Article 477** Where the manifestation of intention to revoke an offer is made in a dialogue form, the content of such manifestation of intention shall come to the knowledge of the offeree before the offeree dispatches an acceptance; where the manifestation of intention to revoke an offer is made in a non-dialogue form, it shall reach the offeree before the offeree dispatches an acceptance.

<a id="art-478"></a>第四百七十八条　有下列情形之一的，要约失效：  

**Article 478** An offer ceases to be effective under any of the following circumstances:
(1) The offer is rejected;
(2) The offer is lawfully revoked;
(3) The time limit for acceptance expires and the offeree has not made an acceptance; or
(4) The offeree materially alters the content of the offer.
（一）要约被拒绝；  
（二）要约被依法撤销；  
（三）承诺期限届满，受要约人未作出承诺；  
（四）受要约人对要约的内容作出实质性变更。

<a id="art-479"></a>第四百七十九条　承诺是受要约人同意要约的意思表示。

**Article 479** An acceptance is a manifestation of intention by the offeree to assent to the offer.

<a id="art-480"></a>第四百八十条　承诺应当以通知的方式作出；但是，根据交易习惯或者要约表明可以通过行为作出承诺的除外。

**Article 480** An acceptance shall be made by way of a notification, unless it may be made by conduct in accordance with trade customs or as indicated by the offer.

<a id="art-481"></a>第四百八十一条　承诺应当在要约确定的期限内到达要约人。  

**Article 481** An acceptance shall reach the offeror within the time limit fixed by the offer. Where no time limit for acceptance is fixed by the offer, the acceptance shall reach the offeror in accordance with the following provisions: (1) where the offer is made orally, the acceptance shall be made immediately; (2) where the offer is made in a non-oral manner, the acceptance shall reach the offeror within a reasonable period of time.
要约没有确定承诺期限的，承诺应当依照下列规定到达：  
（一）要约以对话方式作出的，应当即时作出承诺；  
（二）要约以非对话方式作出的，承诺应当在合理期限内到达。

<a id="art-482"></a>第四百八十二条　要约以信件或者电报作出的，承诺期限自信件载明的日期或者电报交发之日开始计算。信件未载明日期的，自投寄该信件的邮戳日期开始计算。要约以电话、传真、电子邮件等快速通讯方式作出的，承诺期限自要约到达受要约人时开始计算。

**Article 482** Where an offer is made by letter or telegram, the time limit for acceptance shall be calculated from the date stated in the letter or the date of dispatch of the telegram. Where no date is stated in the letter, it shall be calculated from the postmark date of the letter. Where an offer is made by means of instantaneous communication such as telephone, facsimile, or email, the time limit for acceptance shall be calculated from the time the offer reaches the offeree.

<a id="art-483"></a>第四百八十三条　承诺生效时合同成立，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 483** A contract is formed when the acceptance becomes effective, except where otherwise provided by law or otherwise agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-484"></a>第四百八十四条　以通知方式作出的承诺，生效的时间适用[本法第一百三十七条](#art-137)的规定。  

**Article 484** Where an acceptance is made by notice, the time when it becomes effective shall be governed by the provisions of Article 137 of this Law. Where notice of acceptance is not required, the acceptance becomes effective when an act of acceptance is performed in accordance with trade usage or the requirements of the offer.
承诺不需要通知的，根据交易习惯或者要约的要求作出承诺的行为时生效。

<a id="art-485"></a>第四百八十五条　承诺可以撤回。承诺的撤回适用[本法第一百四十一条](#art-141)的规定。

**Article 485** An acceptance may be withdrawn. The withdrawal of an acceptance shall be governed by the provisions of Article 141 of this Law.

<a id="art-486"></a>第四百八十六条　受要约人超过承诺期限发出承诺，或者在承诺期限内发出承诺，按照通常情形不能及时到达要约人的，为新要约；但是，要约人及时通知受要约人该承诺有效的除外。

**Article 486** Where the offeree makes an acceptance after the time limit for acceptance has expired, or makes an acceptance within the time limit but, under normal circumstances, it cannot reach the offeror in time, such acceptance shall constitute a new offer, unless the offeror promptly notifies the offeree that the acceptance is effective.

<a id="art-487"></a>第四百八十七条　受要约人在承诺期限内发出承诺，按照通常情形能够及时到达要约人，但是因其他原因致使承诺到达要约人时超过承诺期限的，除要约人及时通知受要约人因承诺超过期限不接受该承诺外，该承诺有效。

**Article 487** Where the offeree makes an acceptance within the time limit for acceptance and, under normal circumstances, it could have reached the offeror in time, but due to other reasons, the acceptance reaches the offeror after the time limit has expired, the acceptance shall be effective unless the offeror promptly notifies the offeree that the acceptance is not accepted because it exceeds the time limit.

<a id="art-488"></a>第四百八十八条　承诺的内容应当与要约的内容一致。受要约人对要约的内容作出实质性变更的，为新要约。有关合同标的、数量、质量、价款或者报酬、履行期限、履行地点和方式、违约责任和解决争议方法等的变更，是对要约内容的实质性变更。

**Article 488** The content of an acceptance shall be consistent with the content of the offer. If the offeree materially alters the content of the offer, it shall constitute a new offer. Changes relating to the subject matter, quantity, quality, price or remuneration, time of performance, place and method of performance, liability for breach of contract, or method of dispute resolution, etc., are material alterations to the content of the offer.

<a id="art-489"></a>第四百八十九条　承诺对要约的内容作出非实质性变更的，除要约人及时表示反对或者要约表明承诺不得对要约的内容作出任何变更外，该承诺有效，合同的内容以承诺的内容为准。

**Article 489** Where an acceptance makes non-material alterations to the content of the offer, such acceptance shall be effective, and the content of the contract shall be based on the content of the acceptance, unless the offeror promptly objects or the offer states that the acceptance shall not make any alteration to the content of the offer.

<a id="art-490"></a>第四百九十条　当事人采用合同书形式订立合同的，自当事人均签名、盖章或者按指印时合同成立。在签名、盖章或者按指印之前，当事人一方已经履行主要义务，对方接受时，该合同成立。  

**Article 490** Where the parties enter into a contract in written form, the contract is formed when all parties have signed, affixed their seals, or made their fingerprints. If, before signing, affixing a seal, or making a fingerprint, one party has already performed the principal obligations and the other party accepts such performance, the contract is formed. Where laws or administrative regulations provide, or the parties have agreed, that the contract shall be concluded in written form, and the parties fail to do so, but one party has already performed the principal obligations and the other party accepts such performance, the contract is formed.
法律、行政法规规定或者当事人约定合同应当采用书面形式订立，当事人未采用书面形式但是一方已经履行主要义务，对方接受时，该合同成立。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/知识产权/最高人民法院关于审理著作权民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-22" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理著作权民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第22条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-491"></a>第四百九十一条　当事人采用信件、数据电文等形式订立合同要求签订确认书的，签订确认书时合同成立。  

**Article 491** Where the parties enter into a contract in the form of letters, electronic data messages, etc., and require the execution of a confirmation letter, the contract is formed when the confirmation letter is executed. Where one party publishes information on goods or services via the internet or other information networks and such information meets the conditions for an offer, the contract is formed when the other party selects such goods or services and successfully submits the order, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.
当事人一方通过互联网等信息网络发布的商品或者服务信息符合要约条件的，对方选择该商品或者服务并提交订单成功时合同成立，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

<a id="art-492"></a>第四百九十二条　承诺生效的地点为合同成立的地点。  

**Article 492** The place where the acceptance becomes effective shall be the place where the contract is formed. Where a contract is concluded in the form of electronic data messages, the principal place of business of the recipient shall be the place where the contract is formed; in the absence of a principal place of business, the place of domicile of the recipient shall be the place where the contract is formed. If the parties have otherwise agreed, such agreement shall prevail.
采用数据电文形式订立合同的，收件人的主营业地为合同成立的地点；没有主营业地的，其住所地为合同成立的地点。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。

<a id="art-493"></a>第四百九十三条　当事人采用合同书形式订立合同的，最后签名、盖章或者按指印的地点为合同成立的地点，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 493** Where the parties enter into a contract in written form, the place where the last party signs, affixes its seal, or makes its fingerprint shall be the place where the contract is formed, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-494"></a>第四百九十四条　国家根据抢险救灾、疫情防控或者其他需要下达国家订货任务、指令性任务的，有关民事主体之间应当依照有关法律、行政法规规定的权利和义务订立合同。  

**Article 494** Where the State issues a national procurement order or mandatory task based on the needs for emergency rescue, disaster relief, epidemic prevention and control, or other needs, the relevant civil subjects shall conclude a contract in accordance with the rights and obligations prescribed by the relevant laws and administrative regulations. A party that is obligated under laws or administrative regulations to make an offer shall promptly make a reasonable offer. A party that is obligated under laws or administrative regulations to make an acceptance shall not refuse a reasonable request by the other party to conclude a contract.
依照法律、行政法规的规定负有发出要约义务的当事人，应当及时发出合理的要约。  
依照法律、行政法规的规定负有作出承诺义务的当事人，不得拒绝对方合理的订立合同要求。

<a id="art-495"></a>第四百九十五条　当事人约定在将来一定期限内订立合同的认购书、订购书、预订书等，构成预约合同。  

**Article 495** A letter of intent, subscription letter, order letter, booking letter, etc., whereby the parties agree to enter into a contract within a certain period in the future, shall constitute a preliminary contract. Where one party fails to perform its obligation to conclude the contract as agreed in the preliminary contract, the other party may request it to bear the liability for breach of the preliminary contract.
当事人一方不履行预约合同约定的订立合同义务的，对方可以请求其承担预约合同的违约责任。

<a id="art-496"></a>第四百九十六条　格式条款是当事人为了重复使用而预先拟定，并在订立合同时未与对方协商的条款。  

**Article 496** Standard terms are clauses that are prepared in advance by a party for repeated use and are not negotiated with the other party at the time of conclusion of the contract. Where standard terms are used in concluding a contract, the party providing the standard terms shall determine the rights and obligations of the parties in accordance with the principle of fairness, and shall, in a reasonable manner, draw the other party's attention to the terms that involve the exemption or limitation of its liability and other terms that are of significant interest to the other party, and shall, upon the other party's request, explain such terms. If the party providing the standard terms fails to perform its obligation of drawing attention or explanation, so that the other party fails to notice or understand the terms that are of significant interest to it, the other party may claim that such terms are not part of the contract.
采用格式条款订立合同的，提供格式条款的一方应当遵循公平原则确定当事人之间的权利和义务，并采取合理的方式提示对方注意免除或者减轻其责任等与对方有重大利害关系的条款，按照对方的要求，对该条款予以说明。提供格式条款的一方未履行提示或者说明义务，致使对方没有注意或者理解与其有重大利害关系的条款的，对方可以主张该条款不成为合同的内容。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第10条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》时间效力的若干规定.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》时间效力的若干规定》第9条引用">[3]</a></sup>

<a id="art-497"></a>第四百九十七条　有下列情形之一的，该格式条款无效：  

**Article 497** A standard term shall be void under any of the following circumstances: (1) it falls under the circumstances of invalidity provided for in Section 3 of Chapter 6 of Part I of this Law and in Article 506 of this Law; (2) the party providing the standard terms unreasonably exempts or reduces its own liability, increases the liability of the other party, or limits the other party's principal rights; (3) the party providing the standard terms excludes the other party's principal rights.
（一）具有本法第一编第六章第三节和[本法第五百零六条](#art-506)规定的无效情形；  
（二）提供格式条款一方不合理地免除或者减轻其责任、加重对方责任、限制对方主要权利；  
（三）提供格式条款一方排除对方主要权利。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理使用人脸识别技术处理个人信息相关民事案件适用法律若干问题的规定.md#art-11" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理使用人脸识别技术处理个人信息相关民事案件适用法律若干问题的规定》第11条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理预付式消费民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理预付式消费民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第9条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-498"></a>第四百九十八条　对格式条款的理解发生争议的，应当按照通常理解予以解释。对格式条款有两种以上解释的，应当作出不利于提供格式条款一方的解释。格式条款和非格式条款不一致的，应当采用非格式条款。

**Article 498** Where there is a dispute over the understanding of a standard term, it shall be interpreted in accordance with the common understanding. Where a standard term is subject to two or more interpretations, it shall be interpreted in a manner unfavorable to the party providing the standard terms. Where a standard term is inconsistent with a non-standard term, the non-standard term shall prevail.

<a id="art-499"></a>第四百九十九条　悬赏人以公开方式声明对完成特定行为的人支付报酬的，完成该行为的人可以请求其支付。

**Article 499** Where a reward offeror, by a public statement, promises to pay a reward to a person who performs a specific act, the person who has performed such act may request the reward.

<a id="art-500"></a>第五百条　当事人在订立合同过程中有下列情形之一，造成对方损失的，应当承担赔偿责任：  

**Article 500** A party that, during the process of concluding a contract, causes loss to the other party under any of the following circumstances shall be liable for damages: (1) negotiating in bad faith under the pretext of concluding a contract; (2) intentionally concealing material facts related to the conclusion of the contract or providing false information; (3) committing other acts contrary to the principle of good faith.
（一）假借订立合同，恶意进行磋商；  
（二）故意隐瞒与订立合同有关的重要事实或者提供虚假情况；  
（三）有其他违背诚信原则的行为。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/证券与期货/最高人民法院关于审理期货纠纷案件若干问题的规定.md#art-16" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理期货纠纷案件若干问题的规定》第16条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-501"></a>第五百零一条　当事人在订立合同过程中知悉的商业秘密或者其他应当保密的信息，无论合同是否成立，不得泄露或者不正当地使用；泄露、不正当地使用该商业秘密或者信息，造成对方损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

### 第三章　合同的效力

<a id="art-502"></a>第五百零二条　依法成立的合同，自成立时生效，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。  
依照法律、行政法规的规定，合同应当办理批准等手续的，依照其规定。未办理批准等手续影响合同生效的，不影响合同中履行报批等义务条款以及相关条款的效力。应当办理申请批准等手续的当事人未履行义务的，对方可以请求其承担违反该义务的责任。  
依照法律、行政法规的规定，合同的变更、转让、解除等情形应当办理批准等手续的，适用前款规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-14" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第14条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-503"></a>第五百零三条　无权代理人以被代理人的名义订立合同，被代理人已经开始履行合同义务或者接受相对人履行的，视为对合同的追认。

<a id="art-504"></a>第五百零四条　法人的法定代表人或者非法人组织的负责人超越权限订立的合同，除相对人知道或者应当知道其超越权限外，该代表行为有效，订立的合同对法人或者非法人组织发生效力。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-20" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第20条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-22" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第22条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-505"></a>第五百零五条　当事人超越经营范围订立的合同的效力，应当依照本法第一编第六章第三节和本编的有关规定确定，不得仅以超越经营范围确认合同无效。

<a id="art-506"></a>第五百零六条　合同中的下列免责条款无效：  
（一）造成对方人身损害的；  
（二）因故意或者重大过失造成对方财产损失的。

<a id="art-507"></a>第五百零七条　合同不生效、无效、被撤销或者终止的，不影响合同中有关解决争议方法的条款的效力。

<a id="art-508"></a>第五百零八条　本编对合同的效力没有规定的，适用本法第一编第六章的有关规定。

### 第四章　合同的履行

<a id="art-509"></a>第五百零九条　当事人应当按照约定全面履行自己的义务。  
当事人应当遵循诚信原则，根据合同的性质、目的和交易习惯履行通知、协助、保密等义务。  
当事人在履行合同过程中，应当避免浪费资源、污染环境和破坏生态。

<a id="art-510"></a>第五百一十条　合同生效后，当事人就质量、价款或者报酬、履行地点等内容没有约定或者约定不明确的，可以协议补充；不能达成补充协议的，按照合同相关条款或者交易习惯确定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理买卖合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理买卖合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院关于审理民事案件适用诉讼时效制度若干问题的规定.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理民事案件适用诉讼时效制度若干问题的规定》第4条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理预付式消费民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理预付式消费民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第8条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》合同编通则若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[4]</a></sup>

**Article 510** After the contract takes effect, if the parties have not agreed on the contents such as quality, price or remuneration, or place of performance, or if the agreement is unclear, they may supplement by agreement; if they cannot reach a supplementary agreement, the matter shall be determined in accordance with the relevant clauses of the contract or trade practices.

<a id="art-511"></a>第五百一十一条　当事人就有关合同内容约定不明确，依据前条规定仍不能确定的，适用下列规定：  

**Article 511** Where the parties have not agreed on the relevant contents of the contract clearly and cannot be determined in accordance with the preceding article, the following provisions shall apply:
(1) If quality requirements are unclear, performance shall be made in accordance with mandatory national standards; in the absence of mandatory national standards, in accordance with recommended national standards; in the absence of recommended national standards, in accordance with industry standards; in the absence of national or industry standards, in accordance with usual standards or specific standards consistent with the purpose of the contract.
(2) If price or remuneration is unclear, performance shall be made at the market price at the place of performance at the time of conclusion of the contract; if government-set prices or government-guided prices are to be followed according to law, performance shall be made in accordance with such provisions.
(3) If the place of performance is unclear, for payment of money, performance shall be made at the place where the party receiving the money is located; for delivery of immovable property, at the place where the immovable property is located; for other subject matters, at the place where the party performing the obligation is located.
(4) If the time limit for performance is unclear, the debtor may perform at any time, and the creditor may demand performance at any time, provided that the other party is given the necessary time for preparation.
(5) If the method of performance is unclear, performance shall be made in a manner conducive to achieving the purpose of the contract.
(6) If the burden of performance expenses is unclear, it shall be borne by the party performing the obligation; expenses increased due to the creditor's reasons shall be borne by the creditor.
（一）质量要求不明确的，按照强制性国家标准履行；没有强制性国家标准的，按照推荐性国家标准履行；没有推荐性国家标准的，按照行业标准履行；没有国家标准、行业标准的，按照通常标准或者符合合同目的的特定标准履行。  
（二）价款或者报酬不明确的，按照订立合同时履行地的市场价格履行；依法应当执行政府定价或者政府指导价的，依照规定履行。  
（三）履行地点不明确，给付货币的，在接受货币一方所在地履行；交付不动产的，在不动产所在地履行；其他标的，在履行义务一方所在地履行。  
（四）履行期限不明确的，债务人可以随时履行，债权人也可以随时请求履行，但是应当给对方必要的准备时间。  
（五）履行方式不明确的，按照有利于实现合同目的的方式履行。  
（六）履行费用的负担不明确的，由履行义务一方负担；因债权人原因增加的履行费用，由债权人负担。

<a id="art-512"></a>第五百一十二条　通过互联网等信息网络订立的电子合同的标的为交付商品并采用快递物流方式交付的，收货人的签收时间为交付时间。电子合同的标的为提供服务的，生成的电子凭证或者实物凭证中载明的时间为提供服务时间；前述凭证没有载明时间或者载明时间与实际提供服务时间不一致的，以实际提供服务的时间为准。  

**Article 512** If the subject matter of an electronic contract concluded via the internet or other information networks is to deliver goods and delivery is by courier logistics, the time of receipt by the consignee shall be the delivery time.
