Where a gift is made subject to obligations, the donee shall perform the obligations in accordance with the agreement.
赠与附义务的，受赠人应当按照约定履行义务。

<a id="art-662"></a>第六百六十二条　赠与的财产有瑕疵的，赠与人不承担责任。附义务的赠与，赠与的财产有瑕疵的，赠与人在附义务的限度内承担与出卖人相同的责任。  

**Article 662** The donor shall not assume liability for any defects in the donated property. Where a gift is made subject to obligations and the donated property is defective, the donor shall, to the extent of the obligations imposed, bear the same liability as a seller.
Where the donor intentionally fails to disclose the defects or warrants that the property is free from defects, thereby causing losses to the donee, the donor shall bear compensation liability.
赠与人故意不告知瑕疵或者保证无瑕疵，造成受赠人损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

<a id="art-663"></a>第六百六十三条　受赠人有下列情形之一的，赠与人可以撤销赠与：  

**Article 663** The donor may revoke the gift under any of the following circumstances:
(1) The donee seriously infringes upon the lawful rights and interests of the donor or any of the donor's close relatives;
(2) The donee fails to perform the obligation to support the donor; or
(3) The donee fails to perform the obligations agreed upon in the gift contract.
The donor's right of revocation shall be exercised within one year from the date when the donor knows or should have known the cause for revocation.
（一）严重侵害赠与人或者赠与人近亲属的合法权益；  
（二）对赠与人有扶养义务而不履行；  
（三）不履行赠与合同约定的义务。  
赠与人的撤销权，自知道或者应当知道撤销事由之日起一年内行使。

<a id="art-664"></a>第六百六十四条　因受赠人的违法行为致使赠与人死亡或者丧失民事行为能力的，赠与人的继承人或者法定代理人可以撤销赠与。  

**Article 664** Where the donor dies or loses his capacity for civil conduct due to the donee's illegal act, the donor's heir or legal representative may revoke the gift.
The right of revocation of the donor's heir or legal representative shall be exercised within six months from the date when the heir or legal representative knows or should have known the cause for revocation.
赠与人的继承人或者法定代理人的撤销权，自知道或者应当知道撤销事由之日起六个月内行使。

<a id="art-665"></a>第六百六十五条　撤销权人撤销赠与的，可以向受赠人请求返还赠与的财产。

**Article 665** Where the person entitled to revocation revokes the gift, he may claim the return of the donated property from the donee.

<a id="art-666"></a>第六百六十六条　赠与人的经济状况显著恶化，严重影响其生产经营或者家庭生活的，可以不再履行赠与义务。

**Article 666** Where the donor's financial situation has deteriorated significantly, seriously affecting its production and business operations or family life, the donor may no longer perform the obligation to make the gift.

### 第十二章　借款合同

<a id="art-667"></a>第六百六十七条　借款合同是借款人向贷款人借款，到期返还借款并支付利息的合同。

**Article 667** A loan contract is a contract whereby the borrower borrows a sum of money from the lender and repays the borrowed money with interest at the time of maturity.

<a id="art-668"></a>第六百六十八条　借款合同应当采用书面形式，但是自然人之间借款另有约定的除外。  

**Article 668** A loan contract shall be made in writing, unless otherwise agreed between natural persons.
The content of a loan contract generally includes the type, currency, purpose, amount, interest rate, term, and repayment method of the loan.
借款合同的内容一般包括借款种类、币种、用途、数额、利率、期限和还款方式等条款。

<a id="art-669"></a>第六百六十九条　订立借款合同，借款人应当按照贷款人的要求提供与借款有关的业务活动和财务状况的真实情况。

**Article 669** When concluding a loan contract, the borrower shall, as required by the lender, provide truthful information about its business activities and financial status related to the borrowing.

<a id="art-670"></a>第六百七十条　借款的利息不得预先在本金中扣除。利息预先在本金中扣除的，应当按照实际借款数额返还借款并计算利息。

**Article 670** The interest on a loan shall not be deducted from the principal in advance. Where interest is deducted from the principal in advance, the loan shall be repaid and interest shall be calculated based on the actual amount of borrowed money.

<a id="art-671"></a>第六百七十一条　贷款人未按照约定的日期、数额提供借款，造成借款人损失的，应当赔偿损失。  

**Article 671** Where the lender fails to provide the loan on the agreed date and in the agreed amount, thereby causing losses to the borrower, the lender shall compensate the losses.
Where the borrower fails to collect the loan on the agreed date and in the agreed amount, the borrower shall pay interest based on the agreed date and amount.
借款人未按照约定的日期、数额收取借款的，应当按照约定的日期、数额支付利息。

<a id="art-672"></a>第六百七十二条　贷款人按照约定可以检查、监督借款的使用情况。借款人应当按照约定向贷款人定期提供有关财务会计报表或者其他资料。

**Article 672** The lender may, in accordance with the agreement, inspect and supervise the use of the loan. The borrower shall, as agreed, regularly provide the lender with relevant financial and accounting reports or other materials.

<a id="art-673"></a>第六百七十三条　借款人未按照约定的借款用途使用借款的，贷款人可以停止发放借款、提前收回借款或者解除合同。

**Article 673** Where the borrower fails to use the loan for the agreed purpose, the lender may stop disbursing the loan, recall the loan before the due date, or rescind the contract.

<a id="art-674"></a>第六百七十四条　借款人应当按照约定的期限支付利息。对支付利息的期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定，借款期间不满一年的，应当在返还借款时一并支付；借款期间一年以上的，应当在每届满一年时支付，剩余期间不满一年的，应当在返还借款时一并支付。

**Article 674** The borrower shall pay interest within the agreed time limit. Where the time limit for payment of interest is not agreed or is not clearly agreed, and cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, if the loan term is less than one year, the interest shall be paid together with the repayment of the loan; if the loan term is one year or more, the interest shall be paid at the end of each year, and if the remaining period is less than one year, the interest shall be paid together with the repayment of the loan.

<a id="art-675"></a>第六百七十五条　借款人应当按照约定的期限返还借款。对借款期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，借款人可以随时返还；贷款人可以催告借款人在合理期限内返还。

**Article 675** The borrower shall repay the loan within the agreed time limit. Where the loan term is not agreed or is not clearly agreed, and cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the borrower may repay the loan at any time; and the lender may demand that the borrower repay the loan within a reasonable time.

<a id="art-676"></a>第六百七十六条　借款人未按照约定的期限返还借款的，应当按照约定或者国家有关规定支付逾期利息。

**Article 676** Where the borrower fails to repay the loan within the agreed time limit, the borrower shall pay overdue interest in accordance with the agreement or the relevant provisions of the state.

<a id="art-677"></a>第六百七十七条　借款人提前返还借款的，除当事人另有约定外，应当按照实际借款的期间计算利息。

**Article 677** Where the borrower repays the loan before the due date, unless otherwise agreed by the parties, interest shall be calculated based on the actual loan period.

<a id="art-678"></a>第六百七十八条　借款人可以在还款期限届满前向贷款人申请展期；贷款人同意的，可以展期。

**Article 678** The borrower may apply to the lender for an extension of the loan term before the repayment date; if the lender approves, an extension may be granted.

<a id="art-679"></a>第六百七十九条　自然人之间的借款合同，自贷款人提供借款时成立。

**Article 679** A loan contract between natural persons shall be formed when the lender provides the loan.

<a id="art-680"></a>第六百八十条　禁止高利放贷，借款的利率不得违反国家有关规定。  

**Article 680** Usurious lending is prohibited, and the interest rate on loans shall not violate the relevant provisions of the state.
Where the payment of interest is not agreed upon in the loan contract, it is deemed that no interest is payable.
Where the agreement on the payment of interest in a loan contract is not clear and the parties cannot reach a supplementary agreement, the interest shall be determined based on factors such as local or the parties' transaction practices, transaction customs, and market interest rates. For a loan between natural persons, it is deemed that no interest is payable.
借款合同对支付利息没有约定的，视为没有利息。  
借款合同对支付利息约定不明确，当事人不能达成补充协议的，按照当地或者当事人的交易方式、交易习惯、市场利率等因素确定利息；自然人之间借款的，视为没有利息。

### 第十三章　保证合同

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-681"></a>第六百八十一条　保证合同是为保障债权的实现，保证人和债权人约定，当债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的情形时，保证人履行债务或者承担责任的合同。

**Article 681** A suretyship contract is a contract whereby the surety and the creditor agree that, when the debtor fails to perform its due obligation or where a circumstance agreed upon by the parties occurs, the surety shall perform the obligation or bear liability.

<a id="art-682"></a>第六百八十二条　保证合同是主债权债务合同的从合同。主债权债务合同无效的，保证合同无效，但是法律另有规定的除外。  

**Article 682** A suretyship contract is an ancillary contract to the principal creditor-debtor contract. Where the principal creditor-debtor contract is void, the suretyship contract is void, unless otherwise provided by law. 
After the suretyship contract is confirmed to be void, the debtor, the surety, or the creditor that is at fault shall bear civil liability correspondingly according to its fault.
保证合同被确认无效后，债务人、保证人、债权人有过错的，应当根据其过错各自承担相应的民事责任。

<a id="art-683"></a>第六百八十三条　机关法人不得为保证人，但是经国务院批准为使用外国政府或者国际经济组织贷款进行转贷的除外。  

**Article 683** A government organ as a legal person may not act as a surety, except where it, upon approval of the State Council, acts as a surety for relending loans borrowed from a foreign government or an international economic organization. 
A non-profit legal person or an unincorporated organization established for public welfare purposes may not act as a surety.
以公益为目的的非营利法人、非法人组织不得为保证人。

<a id="art-684"></a>第六百八十四条　保证合同的内容一般包括被保证的主债权的种类、数额，债务人履行债务的期限，保证的方式、范围和期间等条款。

**Article 684** The contents of a suretyship contract generally include clauses such as the type and amount of the principal claim secured, the time limit for the debtor to perform the obligation, the method, scope, and period of the suretyship.

<a id="art-685"></a>第六百八十五条　保证合同可以是单独订立的书面合同，也可以是主债权债务合同中的保证条款。  

**Article 685** A suretyship contract may be a stand-alone written contract or a suretyship clause in the principal creditor-debtor contract. 
Where a third party makes a suretyship offer to the creditor unilaterally in written form, and the creditor receives the offer without raising any objection, the suretyship contract is established.
第三人单方以书面形式向债权人作出保证，债权人接收且未提出异议的，保证合同成立。

<a id="art-686"></a>第六百八十六条　保证的方式包括一般保证和连带责任保证。  

**Article 686** The methods of suretyship include general suretyship and suretyship with joint and several liability. 
Where the parties have not agreed upon or have ambiguously agreed upon the method of suretyship in the suretyship contract, the surety shall bear suretyship liability in accordance with general suretyship.
当事人在保证合同中对保证方式没有约定或者约定不明确的，按照一般保证承担保证责任。

<a id="art-687"></a>第六百八十七条　当事人在保证合同中约定，债务人不能履行债务时，由保证人承担保证责任的，为一般保证。  

**Article 687** Where the parties agree in the suretyship contract that the surety shall bear suretyship liability when the debtor is unable to perform the obligation, this is a general suretyship. 
The surety in a general suretyship has the right to refuse to bear suretyship liability vis-à-vis the creditor before the dispute on the principal contract is adjudicated or arbitrated and the debtor's property is still insufficient to perform the obligation after compulsory enforcement according to law, except in any of the following circumstances: 
(1) the debtor's whereabouts are unknown and there is no property available for enforcement; 
(2) the People's Court has accepted a bankruptcy case of the debtor; 
(3) the creditor has evidence proving that the debtor's property is insufficient to perform all the obligations or the debtor has lost the capacity to perform the obligation; or 
(4) the surety has waived the right provided in this paragraph in writing.
一般保证的保证人在主合同纠纷未经审判或者仲裁，并就债务人财产依法强制执行仍不能履行债务前，有权拒绝向债权人承担保证责任，但是有下列情形之一的除外：  
（一）债务人下落不明，且无财产可供执行；  
（二）人民法院已经受理债务人破产案件；  
（三）债权人有证据证明债务人的财产不足以履行全部债务或者丧失履行债务能力；  
（四）保证人书面表示放弃本款规定的权利。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-26" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第26条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-28" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第28条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-688"></a>第六百八十八条　当事人在保证合同中约定保证人和债务人对债务承担连带责任的，为连带责任保证。  

**Article 688** Where the parties agree in the suretyship contract that the surety and the debtor shall bear joint and several liability for the obligation, this is a suretyship with joint and several liability. 
Where the debtor in a suretyship with joint and several liability fails to perform its due obligation or where a circumstance agreed upon by the parties occurs, the creditor may request the debtor to perform the obligation, or may request the surety to bear suretyship liability within its suretyship scope.
连带责任保证的债务人不履行到期债务或者发生当事人约定的情形时，债权人可以请求债务人履行债务，也可以请求保证人在其保证范围内承担保证责任。

<a id="art-689"></a>第六百八十九条　保证人可以要求债务人提供反担保。

**Article 689** The surety may require the debtor to provide counter-suretyship.

<a id="art-690"></a>第六百九十条　保证人与债权人可以协商订立最高额保证的合同，约定在最高债权额限度内就一定期间连续发生的债权提供保证。  

**Article 690** The surety and the creditor may negotiate to conclude a contract of suretyship with a maximum amount, agreeing to provide suretyship for claims arising continuously within a certain period up to a maximum amount. 
Unless the provisions of this Chapter apply, the relevant provisions on the maximum amount mortgage in Part II of this Law shall apply mutatis mutandis to a suretyship with a maximum amount.
最高额保证除适用本章规定外，参照适用本法第二编最高额抵押权的有关规定。

#### 第二节　保证责任

<a id="art-691"></a>第六百九十一条　保证的范围包括主债权及其利息、违约金、损害赔偿金和实现债权的费用。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。

**Article 691** The scope of suretyship includes the principal claim and its interest, liquidated damages, damages, and the expenses incurred to realize the claim. Where the parties have otherwise agreed, such agreement shall prevail.

<a id="art-692"></a>第六百九十二条　保证期间是确定保证人承担保证责任的期间，不发生中止、中断和延长。  

**Article 692** The suretyship period is the period during which the surety's suretyship liability is determined, and it shall not be suspended, interrupted, or extended. 
The creditor and the surety may agree upon the suretyship period; however, if the agreed suretyship period expires before or on the same date as the performance period of the principal obligation, it shall be deemed that no suretyship period has been agreed upon. Where no suretyship period has been agreed upon or the agreement is ambiguous, the suretyship period shall be six months from the date on which the performance period of the principal obligation expires. 
Where the creditor and the debtor have not agreed upon or have ambiguously agreed upon the performance period of the principal obligation, the suretyship period shall be calculated from the date on which the grace period for the creditor to request the debtor to perform the obligation expires.
债权人与保证人可以约定保证期间，但是约定的保证期间早于主债务履行期限或者与主债务履行期限同时届满的，视为没有约定；没有约定或者约定不明确的，保证期间为主债务履行期限届满之日起六个月。  
债权人与债务人对主债务履行期限没有约定或者约定不明确的，保证期间自债权人请求债务人履行债务的宽限期届满之日起计算。

<a id="art-693"></a>第六百九十三条　一般保证的债权人未在保证期间对债务人提起诉讼或者申请仲裁的，保证人不再承担保证责任。  

**Article 693** Where the creditor in a general suretyship fails to file a lawsuit or apply for arbitration against the debtor within the suretyship period, the surety shall no longer bear suretyship liability. 
Where the creditor in a suretyship with joint and several liability fails to request the surety to bear suretyship liability within the suretyship period, the surety shall no longer bear suretyship liability.
连带责任保证的债权人未在保证期间请求保证人承担保证责任的，保证人不再承担保证责任。

<a id="art-694"></a>第六百九十四条　一般保证的债权人在保证期间届满前对债务人提起诉讼或者申请仲裁的，从保证人拒绝承担保证责任的权利消灭之日起，开始计算保证债务的诉讼时效。  

**Article 694** Where the creditor in a general suretyship files a lawsuit or applies for arbitration against the debtor before the expiration of the suretyship period, the statute of limitations for the suretyship obligation shall begin to run from the date on which the surety's right to refuse to bear suretyship liability is extinguished. 
Where the creditor in a suretyship with joint and several liability requests the surety to bear suretyship liability before the expiration of the suretyship period, the statute of limitations for the suretyship obligation shall begin to run from the date on which the creditor makes the request to the surety to bear suretyship liability.
连带责任保证的债权人在保证期间届满前请求保证人承担保证责任的，从债权人请求保证人承担保证责任之日起，开始计算保证债务的诉讼时效。

<a id="art-695"></a>第六百九十五条　债权人和债务人未经保证人书面同意，协商变更主债权债务合同内容，减轻债务的，保证人仍对变更后的债务承担保证责任；加重债务的，保证人对加重的部分不承担保证责任。  

**Article 695** Where the creditor and the debtor negotiate to modify the contents of the principal creditor-debtor contract without the surety's written consent, if the obligation is reduced, the surety shall still bear suretyship liability for the modified obligation; if the obligation is increased, the surety shall not bear suretyship liability for the increased part. 
Where the creditor and the debtor modify the performance period of the principal creditor-debtor contract without the surety's written consent, the suretyship period shall not be affected.
债权人和债务人变更主债权债务合同的履行期限，未经保证人书面同意的，保证期间不受影响。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-20" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第20条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-696"></a>第六百九十六条　债权人转让全部或者部分债权，未通知保证人的，该转让对保证人不发生效力。  

**Article 696** Where the creditor assigns all or part of the claim without notifying the surety, such assignment shall not be effective against the surety. 
Where the surety and the creditor have agreed that the claim shall not be assigned, and the creditor assigns the claim without the surety's written consent, the surety shall no longer bear suretyship liability to the assignee.
保证人与债权人约定禁止债权转让，债权人未经保证人书面同意转让债权的，保证人对受让人不再承担保证责任。

<a id="art-697"></a>第六百九十七条　债权人未经保证人书面同意，允许债务人转移全部或者部分债务，保证人对未经其同意转移的债务不再承担保证责任，但是债权人和保证人另有约定的除外。  

**Article 697** Where the creditor permits the debtor to transfer all or part of the obligation without the surety's written consent, the surety shall no longer bear suretyship liability for the obligation transferred without its consent, unless otherwise agreed between the creditor and the surety. 
Where a third party joins the obligation, the surety's suretyship liability shall not be affected.
第三人加入债务的，保证人的保证责任不受影响。

<a id="art-698"></a>第六百九十八条　一般保证的保证人在主债务履行期限届满后，向债权人提供债务人可供执行财产的真实情况，债权人放弃或者怠于行使权利致使该财产不能被执行的，保证人在其提供可供执行财产的价值范围内不再承担保证责任。

**Article 698** Where, after the performance period of the principal obligation has expired, the surety in a general suretyship provides the creditor with true information about the debtor's property available for enforcement, and the creditor abandons or neglects to exercise its rights, resulting in such property not being enforced, the surety shall no longer bear suretyship liability to the extent of the value of the property available for enforcement provided by the surety.

<a id="art-699"></a>第六百九十九条　同一债务有两个以上保证人的，保证人应当按照保证合同约定的保证份额，承担保证责任；没有约定保证份额的，债权人可以请求任何一个保证人在其保证范围内承担保证责任。

**Article 699** Where there are two or more sureties for the same obligation, the sureties shall bear suretyship liability in accordance with the respective suretyship share agreed upon in the suretyship contract. If no suretyship share has been agreed upon, the creditor may request any of the sureties to bear suretyship liability within its suretyship scope.

<a id="art-700"></a>第七百条　保证人承担保证责任后，除当事人另有约定外，有权在其承担保证责任的范围内向债务人追偿，享有债权人对债务人的权利，但是不得损害债权人的利益。

**Article 700** After the surety has borne suretyship liability, except otherwise agreed by the parties, it has the right to seek recourse against the debtor within the scope of the suretyship liability assumed, and acquires the creditor's rights against the debtor, provided that it shall not harm the creditor's interests.

<a id="art-701"></a>第七百零一条　保证人可以主张债务人对债权人的抗辩。债务人放弃抗辩的，保证人仍有权向债权人主张抗辩。

<a id="art-702"></a>第七百零二条　债务人对债权人享有抵销权或者撤销权的，保证人可以在相应范围内拒绝承担保证责任。

### 第十四章　租赁合同

<a id="art-703"></a>第七百零三条　租赁合同是出租人将租赁物交付承租人使用、收益，承租人支付租金的合同。

<a id="art-704"></a>第七百零四条　租赁合同的内容一般包括租赁物的名称、数量、用途、租赁期限、租金及其支付期限和方式、租赁物维修等条款。

<a id="art-705"></a>第七百零五条　租赁期限不得超过二十年。超过二十年的，超过部分无效。  
租赁期限届满，当事人可以续订租赁合同；但是，约定的租赁期限自续订之日起不得超过二十年。

<a id="art-706"></a>第七百零六条　当事人未依照法律、行政法规规定办理租赁合同登记备案手续的，不影响合同的效力。

<a id="art-707"></a>第七百零七条　租赁期限六个月以上的，应当采用书面形式。当事人未采用书面形式，无法确定租赁期限的，视为不定期租赁。

<a id="art-708"></a>第七百零八条　出租人应当按照约定将租赁物交付承租人，并在租赁期限内保持租赁物符合约定的用途。

<a id="art-709"></a>第七百零九条　承租人应当按照约定的方法使用租赁物。对租赁物的使用方法没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，应当根据租赁物的性质使用。

<a id="art-710"></a>第七百一十条　承租人按照约定的方法或者根据租赁物的性质使用租赁物，致使租赁物受到损耗的，不承担赔偿责任。

**Article 710** Where the lessee uses the leased property in the agreed manner or according to its nature, resulting in wear and tear to the leased property, the lessee shall not bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-711"></a>第七百一十一条　承租人未按照约定的方法或者未根据租赁物的性质使用租赁物，致使租赁物受到损失的，出租人可以解除合同并请求赔偿损失。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理城镇房屋租赁合同纠纷案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理城镇房屋租赁合同纠纷案件具体应用法律若干问题的解释》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 711** Where the lessee fails to use the leased property in the agreed manner or according to its nature, resulting in damage to the leased property, the lessor may dissolve the contract and claim damages.

<a id="art-712"></a>第七百一十二条　出租人应当履行租赁物的维修义务，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 712** The lessor shall perform the obligation of maintaining the leased property, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-713"></a>第七百一十三条　承租人在租赁物需要维修时可以请求出租人在合理期限内维修。出租人未履行维修义务的，承租人可以自行维修，维修费用由出租人负担。因维修租赁物影响承租人使用的，应当相应减少租金或者延长租期。  

**Article 713** Where the leased property needs maintenance, the lessee may request the lessor to carry out the maintenance within a reasonable period. Where the lessor fails to perform the maintenance obligation, the lessee may carry out the maintenance on its own, and the maintenance costs shall be borne by the lessor. If the maintenance of the leased property affects the lessee's use, the rent shall be reduced accordingly or the lease term shall be extended.
Where the leased property needs maintenance due to the lessee's fault, the lessor shall not be obligated to perform the maintenance obligation as provided in the preceding paragraph.
因承租人的过错致使租赁物需要维修的，出租人不承担前款规定的维修义务。

<a id="art-714"></a>第七百一十四条　承租人应当妥善保管租赁物，因保管不善造成租赁物毁损、灭失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 714** The lessee shall take good care of the leased property. Where the leased property is damaged or lost due to improper care, the lessee shall bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-715"></a>第七百一十五条　承租人经出租人同意，可以对租赁物进行改善或者增设他物。  

**Article 715** With the lessor's consent, the lessee may make improvements to or add ancillary items to the leased property.
Where the lessee makes improvements to or adds ancillary items to the leased property without the lessor's consent, the lessor may request the lessee to restore the leased property to its original condition or pay compensation for the losses.
承租人未经出租人同意，对租赁物进行改善或者增设他物的，出租人可以请求承租人恢复原状或者赔偿损失。

<a id="art-716"></a>第七百一十六条　承租人经出租人同意，可以将租赁物转租给第三人。承租人转租的，承租人与出租人之间的租赁合同继续有效；第三人造成租赁物损失的，承租人应当赔偿损失。  

**Article 716** With the lessor's consent, the lessee may sublease the leased property to a third party. Where the lessee subleases the leased property, the lease contract between the lessee and the lessor remains valid; where a third party causes damage to the leased property, the lessee shall compensate for the losses.
Where the lessee subleases the leased property without the lessor's consent, the lessor may dissolve the contract.
承租人未经出租人同意转租的，出租人可以解除合同。

<a id="art-717"></a>第七百一十七条　承租人经出租人同意将租赁物转租给第三人，转租期限超过承租人剩余租赁期限的，超过部分的约定对出租人不具有法律约束力，但是出租人与承租人另有约定的除外。

**Article 717** Where the lessee, with the lessor's consent, subleases the leased property to a third party, and the sublease term exceeds the remaining term of the lease, the agreement on the exceeding part shall have no legal binding force on the lessor, unless the lessor and the lessee agree otherwise.

<a id="art-718"></a>第七百一十八条　出租人知道或者应当知道承租人转租，但是在六个月内未提出异议的，视为出租人同意转租。

**Article 718** Where the lessor knows or should have known that the lessee has subleased the leased property but fails to raise an objection within six months, it shall be deemed that the lessor has consented to the sublease.

<a id="art-719"></a>第七百一十九条　承租人拖欠租金的，次承租人可以代承租人支付其欠付的租金和违约金，但是转租合同对出租人不具有法律约束力的除外。  

**Article 719** Where the lessee is in arrears with the rent, the sublessee may pay the overdue rent and liquidated damages on behalf of the lessee, unless the sublease contract has no legal binding force on the lessor.
The rent and liquidated damages paid by the sublessee on behalf of the lessee may be set off against the rent payable by the sublessee to the lessee; any amount exceeding the rent payable by the sublessee may be recovered from the lessee.
次承租人代为支付的租金和违约金，可以充抵次承租人应当向承租人支付的租金；超出其应付的租金数额的，可以向承租人追偿。

<a id="art-720"></a>第七百二十条　在租赁期限内因占有、使用租赁物获得的收益，归承租人所有，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 720** The proceeds obtained from possession or use of the leased property during the lease term shall belong to the lessee, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-721"></a>第七百二十一条　承租人应当按照约定的期限支付租金。对支付租金的期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定，租赁期限不满一年的，应当在租赁期限届满时支付；租赁期限一年以上的，应当在每届满一年时支付，剩余期限不满一年的，应当在租赁期限届满时支付。

**Article 721** The lessee shall pay the rent within the agreed period. Where the period for payment of rent is not agreed or the agreement is unclear, and cannot be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 510 of this Law, if the lease term is less than one year, the rent shall be paid upon the expiration of the lease term; if the lease term is one year or more, the rent shall be paid at the end of each year, and if the remaining term is less than one year, the rent shall be paid upon the expiration of the lease term.

<a id="art-722"></a>第七百二十二条　承租人无正当理由未支付或者迟延支付租金的，出租人可以请求承租人在合理期限内支付；承租人逾期不支付的，出租人可以解除合同。

**Article 722** Where the lessee fails to pay or delays in paying the rent without justifiable cause, the lessor may request the lessee to pay the rent within a reasonable period; if the lessee fails to pay the rent within the extended period, the lessor may dissolve the contract.

<a id="art-723"></a>第七百二十三条　因第三人主张权利，致使承租人不能对租赁物使用、收益的，承租人可以请求减少租金或者不支付租金。  

**Article 723** Where a third party claims rights, thereby causing the lessee to be unable to use or benefit from the leased property, the lessee may request a reduction of or exemption from the rent.
Where a third party claims rights, the lessee shall promptly notify the lessor.
第三人主张权利的，承租人应当及时通知出租人。

<a id="art-724"></a>第七百二十四条　有下列情形之一，非因承租人原因致使租赁物无法使用的，承租人可以解除合同：  

**Article 724** Under any of the following circumstances, where the leased property cannot be used for reasons not attributable to the lessee, the lessee may dissolve the contract:
(1) the leased property is lawfully sealed up or seized by a judicial organ or an administrative organ;
(2) the ownership of the leased property is disputed;
(3) the leased property violates the compulsory provisions of laws or administrative regulations regarding conditions of use.
（一）租赁物被司法机关或者行政机关依法查封、扣押；  
（二）租赁物权属有争议；  
（三）租赁物具有违反法律、行政法规关于使用条件的强制性规定情形。

<a id="art-725"></a>第七百二十五条　租赁物在承租人按照租赁合同占有期限内发生所有权变动的，不影响租赁合同的效力。

**Article 725** Where the ownership of the leased property changes during the period when the lessee possesses it in accordance with the lease contract, the validity of the lease contract shall not be affected.

<a id="art-726"></a>第七百二十六条　出租人出卖租赁房屋的，应当在出卖之前的合理期限内通知承租人，承租人享有以同等条件优先购买的权利；但是，房屋按份共有人行使优先购买权或者出租人将房屋出卖给近亲属的除外。  

**Article 726** Where the lessor sells the leased house, it shall notify the lessee within a reasonable period prior to the sale, and the lessee has the right of first refusal to purchase the house on the same conditions; provided that this does not apply if the co-owners of the house by shares exercise their right of first refusal or the lessor sells the house to a close relative.
After the lessor performs the obligation of notification, if the lessee fails to clearly indicate its intention to purchase within fifteen days, it shall be deemed that the lessee has waived the right of first refusal.
出租人履行通知义务后，承租人在十五日内未明确表示购买的，视为承租人放弃优先购买权。

<a id="art-727"></a>第七百二十七条　出租人委托拍卖人拍卖租赁房屋的，应当在拍卖五日前通知承租人。承租人未参加拍卖的，视为放弃优先购买权。

**Article 727** Where the lessor entrusts an auctioneer to auction the leased house, it shall notify the lessee five days prior to the auction. If the lessee does not participate in the auction, it shall be deemed that the lessee has waived the right of first refusal.

<a id="art-728"></a>第七百二十八条　出租人未通知承租人或者有其他妨害承租人行使优先购买权情形的，承租人可以请求出租人承担赔偿责任。但是，出租人与第三人订立的房屋买卖合同的效力不受影响。

**Article 728** Where the lessor fails to notify the lessee or otherwise impedes the lessee from exercising the right of first refusal, the lessee may request the lessor to bear compensation liability. However, the validity of the house sale contract concluded between the lessor and a third party shall not be affected.

<a id="art-729"></a>第七百二十九条　因不可归责于承租人的事由，致使租赁物部分或者全部毁损、灭失的，承租人可以请求减少租金或者不支付租金；因租赁物部分或者全部毁损、灭失，致使不能实现合同目的的，承租人可以解除合同。

**Article 729** Where the leased property is partially or entirely damaged or lost due to reasons not attributable to the lessee, the lessee may request a reduction of or exemption from the rent; where the contract purpose cannot be achieved due to the partial or entire damage or loss of the leased property, the lessee may dissolve the contract.

<a id="art-730"></a>第七百三十条　当事人对租赁期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，视为不定期租赁；当事人可以随时解除合同，但是应当在合理期限之前通知对方。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理涉及农村土地承包纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释.md#art-16" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理涉及农村土地承包纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释》第16条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 730** Where the parties have not agreed on the lease term or the agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 510 of this Law, the lease shall be deemed to be a lease for an indefinite term; the parties may dissolve the contract at any time, provided that they shall notify the other party within a reasonable period in advance.

<a id="art-731"></a>第七百三十一条　租赁物危及承租人的安全或者健康的，即使承租人订立合同时明知该租赁物质量不合格，承租人仍然可以随时解除合同。

**Article 731** Where the leased property endangers the safety or health of the lessee, the lessee may dissolve the contract at any time even if the lessee knew at the time of concluding the contract that the leased property was substandard.

<a id="art-732"></a>第七百三十二条　承租人在房屋租赁期限内死亡的，与其生前共同居住的人或者共同经营人可以按照原租赁合同租赁该房屋。

**Article 732** Where the lessee dies during the term of the house lease, the persons who lived together with the lessee during its lifetime or its joint operators may lease the house in accordance with the original lease contract.

<a id="art-733"></a>第七百三十三条　租赁期限届满，承租人应当返还租赁物。返还的租赁物应当符合按照约定或者根据租赁物的性质使用后的状态。

**Article 733** Upon expiration of the lease term, the lessee shall return the leased property. The returned leased property shall be in a condition consistent with that resulting from its use in accordance with the agreement or the nature of the leased property.

<a id="art-734"></a>第七百三十四条　租赁期限届满，承租人继续使用租赁物，出租人没有提出异议的，原租赁合同继续有效，但是租赁期限为不定期。  

**Article 734** Where the lessee continues to use the leased property after the expiration of the lease term and the lessor raises no objection, the original lease contract shall remain valid, but the lease term shall be deemed indefinite.
Upon expiration of the lease term, the lessee of the house has the right of first refusal to lease the house on the same conditions.
租赁期限届满，房屋承租人享有以同等条件优先承租的权利。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》时间效力的若干规定.md#art-21" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》时间效力的若干规定》第21条引用">[1]</a></sup>

### 第十五章　融资租赁合同

<a id="art-735"></a>第七百三十五条　融资租赁合同是出租人根据承租人对出卖人、租赁物的选择，向出卖人购买租赁物，提供给承租人使用，承租人支付租金的合同。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理融资租赁合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理融资租赁合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 735** A financial leasing contract is a contract whereby the lessor, based on the lessee's selection of the seller and the leased property, purchases the leased property from the seller, provides it to the lessee for use, and the lessee pays the rent.

<a id="art-736"></a>第七百三十六条　融资租赁合同的内容一般包括租赁物的名称、数量、规格、技术性能、检验方法，租赁期限，租金构成及其支付期限和方式、币种，租赁期限届满租赁物的归属等条款。  

**Article 736** The contents of a financial leasing contract generally include clauses on the name, quantity, specifications, technical performance, and inspection method of the leased property, the lease term, the composition of the rent and the term and method and currency of payment, and the ownership of the leased property upon expiration of the lease term.
A financial leasing contract shall be in written form.
融资租赁合同应当采用书面形式。

<a id="art-737"></a>第七百三十七条　当事人以虚构租赁物方式订立的融资租赁合同无效。

**Article 737** Where the parties conclude a financial leasing contract by fabricating the leased property, the contract is void.

<a id="art-738"></a>第七百三十八条　依照法律、行政法规的规定，对于租赁物的经营使用应当取得行政许可的，出租人未取得行政许可不影响融资租赁合同的效力。

**Article 738** Where an administrative license is required for the operation and use of the leased property in accordance with the provisions of laws or administrative regulations, the lessor's failure to obtain the administrative license shall not affect the validity of the financial leasing contract.

<a id="art-739"></a>第七百三十九条　出租人根据承租人对出卖人、租赁物的选择订立的买卖合同，出卖人应当按照约定向承租人交付标的物，承租人享有与受领标的物有关的买受人的权利。

**Article 739** Where the lessor concludes a sales contract based on the lessee's selection of the seller and the leased property, the seller shall deliver the subject matter to the lessee in accordance with the agreement, and the lessee enjoys the buyer's rights related to taking delivery of the subject matter.

<a id="art-740"></a>第七百四十条　出卖人违反向承租人交付标的物的义务，有下列情形之一的，承租人可以拒绝受领出卖人向其交付的标的物：  

**Article 740** Where the seller violates the obligation to deliver the subject matter to the lessee, under any of the following circumstances, the lessee may refuse to take delivery of the subject matter delivered by the seller:
(1) the subject matter materially fails to conform to the agreement;
(2) the subject matter is not delivered in accordance with the agreement, and is still not delivered within a reasonable period after being urged by the lessee or lessor.
Where the lessee refuses to take delivery of the subject matter, it shall promptly notify the lessor.
（一）标的物严重不符合约定；  
（二）未按照约定交付标的物，经承租人或者出租人催告后在合理期限内仍未交付。  
承租人拒绝受领标的物的，应当及时通知出租人。

<a id="art-741"></a>第七百四十一条　出租人、出卖人、承租人可以约定，出卖人不履行买卖合同义务的，由承租人行使索赔的权利。承租人行使索赔权利的，出租人应当协助。

**Article 741** The lessor, seller, and lessee may agree that, if the seller fails to perform its obligations under the sales contract, the lessee shall exercise the right to claim compensation. Where the lessee exercises the right to claim compensation, the lessor shall provide assistance.

<a id="art-742"></a>第七百四十二条　承租人对出卖人行使索赔权利，不影响其履行支付租金的义务。但是，承租人依赖出租人的技能确定租赁物或者出租人干预选择租赁物的，承租人可以请求减免相应租金。

**Article 742** The lessee's exercise of the right to claim compensation against the seller shall not affect its obligation to pay the rent. However, if the lessee determined the leased object relying on the lessor's expertise or the lessor interfered with the selection of the leased object, the lessee may request a reduction or exemption of the corresponding rent.

<a id="art-743"></a>第七百四十三条　出租人有下列情形之一，致使承租人对出卖人行使索赔权利失败的，承租人有权请求出租人承担相应的责任：  

**Article 743** Where the lessor falls under any of the following circumstances, thereby causing the lessee to fail in exercising the right to claim compensation against the seller, the lessee shall have the right to request the lessor to assume the corresponding liability:
(1) The lessor knew of a quality defect in the leased object but failed to inform the lessee; or
(2) The lessor failed to provide necessary assistance in a timely manner when the lessee exercised the right to claim compensation.
Where the lessor is dilatory in exercising a right to claim compensation against the seller that only the lessor can exercise, thereby causing losses to the lessee, the lessee shall have the right to request the lessor to assume liability for compensation.
（一）明知租赁物有质量瑕疵而不告知承租人；  
（二）承租人行使索赔权利时，未及时提供必要协助。  
出租人怠于行使只能由其对出卖人行使的索赔权利，造成承租人损失的，承租人有权请求出租人承担赔偿责任。

<a id="art-744"></a>第七百四十四条　出租人根据承租人对出卖人、租赁物的选择订立的买卖合同，未经承租人同意，出租人不得变更与承租人有关的合同内容。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理融资租赁合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理融资租赁合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释》第8条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 744** Where a sales contract is concluded by the lessor in accordance with the lessee's selection of the seller and the leased object, the lessor shall not modify the contents of the contract that are related to the lessee without the lessee's consent.

<a id="art-745"></a>第七百四十五条　出租人对租赁物享有的所有权，未经登记，不得对抗善意第三人。

**Article 745** The lessor's ownership of the leased object shall not be set up against a bona fide third party if it is not registered.

<a id="art-746"></a>第七百四十六条　融资租赁合同的租金，除当事人另有约定外，应当根据购买租赁物的大部分或者全部成本以及出租人的合理利润确定。

**Article 746** Unless otherwise agreed by the parties, the rent under a financial leasing contract shall be determined based on most or all of the cost of purchasing the leased object and the lessor's reasonable profit.

<a id="art-747"></a>第七百四十七条　租赁物不符合约定或者不符合使用目的的，出租人不承担责任。但是，承租人依赖出租人的技能确定租赁物或者出租人干预选择租赁物的除外。

**Article 747** The lessor shall not be liable if the leased object does not conform to the agreement or the intended purpose, unless the lessee determined the leased object relying on the lessor's expertise or the lessor interfered with the selection of the leased object.

<a id="art-748"></a>第七百四十八条　出租人应当保证承租人对租赁物的占有和使用。  

**Article 748** The lessor shall guarantee the lessee's possession and use of the leased object.
The lessee shall have the right to request the lessor to compensate for losses in any of the following circumstances:
(1) The lessor takes back the leased object without just cause;
(2) The lessor hinders or interferes with the lessee's possession and use of the leased object without just cause;
(3) A third party claims rights over the leased object due to the lessor's reasons; or
(4) Other circumstances that improperly affect the lessee's possession and use of the leased object.
出租人有下列情形之一的，承租人有权请求其赔偿损失：  
（一）无正当理由收回租赁物；  
（二）无正当理由妨碍、干扰承租人对租赁物的占有和使用；  
（三）因出租人的原因致使第三人对租赁物主张权利；  
（四）不当影响承租人对租赁物占有和使用的其他情形。

<a id="art-749"></a>第七百四十九条　承租人占有租赁物期间，租赁物造成第三人人身损害或者财产损失的，出租人不承担责任。

**Article 749** During the period when the lessee possesses the leased object, the lessor shall not be liable for personal injury or property damage caused to a third party by the leased object.

<a id="art-750"></a>第七百五十条　承租人应当妥善保管、使用租赁物。  

**Article 750** The lessee shall properly keep and use the leased object.
The lessee shall perform the obligation of maintaining the leased object during the period of possession.
承租人应当履行占有租赁物期间的维修义务。

<a id="art-751"></a>第七百五十一条　承租人占有租赁物期间，租赁物毁损、灭失的，出租人有权请求承租人继续支付租金，但是法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 751** Where the leased object is damaged or destroyed during the period when the lessee is in possession thereof, the lessor shall have the right to request the lessee to continue paying the rent, unless otherwise provided by law or agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-752"></a>第七百五十二条　承租人应当按照约定支付租金。承租人经催告后在合理期限内仍不支付租金的，出租人可以请求支付全部租金；也可以解除合同，收回租赁物。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理融资租赁合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理融资租赁合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释》第10条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 752** The lessee shall pay the rent in accordance with the agreement. If the lessee still fails to pay the rent within a reasonable period after receiving a demand, the lessor may request the payment of the entire rent, or may rescind the contract and take back the leased object.

<a id="art-753"></a>第七百五十三条　承租人未经出租人同意，将租赁物转让、抵押、质押、投资入股或者以其他方式处分的，出租人可以解除融资租赁合同。

**Article 753** Where the lessee transfers, mortgages, pledges, makes a capital contribution with, or otherwise disposes of the leased object without the lessor's consent, the lessor may rescind the financial leasing contract.

<a id="art-754"></a>第七百五十四条　有下列情形之一的，出租人或者承租人可以解除融资租赁合同：  

**Article 754** The lessor or the lessee may rescind a financial leasing contract in any of the following circumstances:
(1) The sales contract concluded between the lessor and the seller is rescinded, confirmed as void, or revoked, and no new sales contract can be concluded;
(2) The leased object is damaged or destroyed for reasons not attributable to the parties, and it cannot be repaired or a substitute cannot be determined; or
(3) The purpose of the financial leasing contract cannot be achieved due to reasons attributable to the seller.
（一）出租人与出卖人订立的买卖合同解除、被确认无效或者被撤销，且未能重新订立买卖合同；  
（二）租赁物因不可归责于当事人的原因毁损、灭失，且不能修复或者确定替代物；  
（三）因出卖人的原因致使融资租赁合同的目的不能实现。

<a id="art-755"></a>第七百五十五条　融资租赁合同因买卖合同解除、被确认无效或者被撤销而解除，出卖人、租赁物系由承租人选择的，出租人有权请求承租人赔偿相应损失；但是，因出租人原因致使买卖合同解除、被确认无效或者被撤销的除外。  

**Article 755** Where a financial leasing contract is rescinded because the sales contract is rescinded, confirmed as void, or revoked, and the seller and the leased object were selected by the lessee, the lessor shall have the right to request the lessee to compensate for the corresponding losses, unless the sales contract was rescinded, confirmed as void, or revoked due to reasons attributable to the lessor.
Where the lessor's losses have already been compensated at the time the sales contract was rescinded, confirmed as void, or revoked, the lessee shall no longer bear the corresponding compensation liability.
出租人的损失已经在买卖合同解除、被确认无效或者被撤销时获得赔偿的，承租人不再承担相应的赔偿责任。

<a id="art-756"></a>第七百五十六条　融资租赁合同因租赁物交付承租人后意外毁损、灭失等不可归责于当事人的原因解除的，出租人可以请求承租人按照租赁物折旧情况给予补偿。

**Article 756** Where a financial leasing contract is rescinded due to reasons not attributable to the parties, such as accidental damage or destruction of the leased object after delivery to the lessee, the lessor may request the lessee to provide compensation based on the depreciation of the leased object.

<a id="art-757"></a>第七百五十七条　出租人和承租人可以约定租赁期限届满租赁物的归属；对租赁物的归属没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，租赁物的所有权归出租人。

**Article 757** The lessor and the lessee may agree on the ownership of the leased object upon expiration of the lease term. If there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, and the ownership cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the ownership of the leased object shall belong to the lessor.

<a id="art-758"></a>第七百五十八条　当事人约定租赁期限届满租赁物归承租人所有，承租人已经支付大部分租金，但是无力支付剩余租金，出租人因此解除合同收回租赁物，收回的租赁物的价值超过承租人欠付的租金以及其他费用的，承租人可以请求相应返还。  

**Article 758** Where the parties agree that the leased object shall belong to the lessee upon expiration of the lease term, and the lessee has paid most of the rent but is unable to pay the remaining rent, and the lessor rescinds the contract and takes back the leased object, if the value of the repossessed leased object exceeds the rent and other fees owed by the lessee, the lessee may request a corresponding refund.
Where the parties agree that the leased object shall belong to the lessor upon expiration of the lease term, and the lessee is unable to return the leased object because it has been damaged, destroyed, or has become attached to or commingled with another object, the lessor shall have the right to request the lessee to provide reasonable compensation.
当事人约定租赁期限届满租赁物归出租人所有，因租赁物毁损、灭失或者附合、混合于他物致使承租人不能返还的，出租人有权请求承租人给予合理补偿。

<a id="art-759"></a>第七百五十九条　当事人约定租赁期限届满，承租人仅需向出租人支付象征性价款的，视为约定的租金义务履行完毕后租赁物的所有权归承租人。

**Article 759** Where the parties agree that, upon expiration of the lease term, the lessee only needs to pay a symbolic price to the lessor, the ownership of the leased object shall be deemed to belong to the lessee upon the fulfillment of the agreed rent obligation.

<a id="art-760"></a>第七百六十条　融资租赁合同无效，当事人就该情形下租赁物的归属有约定的，按照其约定；没有约定或者约定不明确的，租赁物应当返还出租人。但是，因承租人原因致使合同无效，出租人不请求返还或者返还后会显著降低租赁物效用的，租赁物的所有权归承租人，由承租人给予出租人合理补偿。

**Article 760** Where a financial leasing contract is invalid, the ownership of the leased object under such circumstance shall follow any agreement between the parties. In the absence of an agreement or if the agreement is unclear, the leased object shall be returned to the lessor. However, if the contract is invalid due to reasons attributable to the lessee, and the lessor does not request the return or the return would significantly reduce the utility of the leased object, the ownership of the leased object shall belong to the lessee, and the lessee shall provide reasonable compensation to the lessor.

### 第十六章　保理合同

<a id="art-761"></a>第七百六十一条　保理合同是应收账款债权人将现有的或者将有的应收账款转让给保理人，保理人提供资金融通、应收账款管理或者催收、应收账款债务人付款担保等服务的合同。

**Article 761** A factoring contract is a contract under which an accounts receivable creditor assigns its existing or future accounts receivable to a factor, and the factor provides services such as financing, management or collection of accounts receivable, or guarantee of payment by the accounts receivable debtor.

<a id="art-762"></a>第七百六十二条　保理合同的内容一般包括业务类型、服务范围、服务期限、基础交易合同情况、应收账款信息、保理融资款或者服务报酬及其支付方式等条款。  

**Article 762** The content of a factoring contract generally includes clauses such as the type of business, scope of services, term of services, status of the underlying transaction contract, information on the accounts receivable, the factoring financing funds or service remuneration and payment method thereof.
A factoring contract shall be in writing.
保理合同应当采用书面形式。

<a id="art-763"></a>第七百六十三条　应收账款债权人与债务人虚构应收账款作为转让标的，与保理人订立保理合同的，应收账款债务人不得以应收账款不存在为由对抗保理人，但是保理人明知虚构的除外。

**Article 763** Where an accounts receivable creditor and its debtor fabricate accounts receivable as the subject of assignment and enter into a factoring contract with a factor, the accounts receivable debtor shall not assert against the factor that the accounts receivable do not exist, unless the factor knew of such fabrication.

<a id="art-764"></a>第七百六十四条　保理人向应收账款债务人发出应收账款转让通知的，应当表明保理人身份并附有必要凭证。

**Article 764** Where a factor sends a notice of assignment of accounts receivable to the accounts receivable debtor, it shall identify itself as a factor and attach the necessary supporting documents.

<a id="art-765"></a>第七百六十五条　应收账款债务人接到应收账款转让通知后，应收账款债权人与债务人无正当理由协商变更或者终止基础交易合同，对保理人产生不利影响的，对保理人不发生效力。

**Article 765** After the accounts receivable debtor receives the notice of assignment of accounts receivable, if the accounts receivable creditor and the debtor negotiate to modify or terminate the underlying transaction contract without justifiable cause, thereby adversely affecting the factor, such modification or termination shall not be binding on the factor.

<a id="art-766"></a>第七百六十六条　当事人约定有追索权保理的，保理人可以向应收账款债权人主张返还保理融资款本息或者回购应收账款债权，也可以向应收账款债务人主张应收账款债权。保理人向应收账款债务人主张应收账款债权，在扣除保理融资款本息和相关费用后有剩余的，剩余部分应当返还给应收账款债权人。

**Article 766** Where the parties agree on factoring with recourse, the factor may claim repayment of the principal and interest of the factoring financing or repurchase of the accounts receivable claim from the accounts receivable creditor, and may also claim the accounts receivable claim from the accounts receivable debtor. If, after the factor claims the accounts receivable claim from the accounts receivable debtor and deducts the principal and interest of the factoring financing and related expenses, there is a surplus, the surplus shall be returned to the accounts receivable creditor.

<a id="art-767"></a>第七百六十七条　当事人约定无追索权保理的，保理人应当向应收账款债务人主张应收账款债权，保理人取得超过保理融资款本息和相关费用的部分，无需向应收账款债权人返还。

**Article 767** Where the parties agree on factoring without recourse, the factor shall claim the accounts receivable claim from the accounts receivable debtor, and the factor is not required to return to the accounts receivable creditor any amount it receives in excess of the principal and interest of the factoring financing and related expenses.

<a id="art-768"></a>第七百六十八条　应收账款债权人就同一应收账款订立多个保理合同，致使多个保理人主张权利的，已经登记的先于未登记的取得应收账款；均已经登记的，按照登记时间的先后顺序取得应收账款；均未登记的，由最先到达应收账款债务人的转让通知中载明的保理人取得应收账款；既未登记也未通知的，按照保理融资款或者服务报酬的比例取得应收账款。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释.md#art-66" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》有关担保制度的解释》第66条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 768** Where an accounts receivable creditor enters into multiple factoring contracts for the same accounts receivable, causing multiple factors to claim rights, a factor that has registered shall have priority over an unregistered factor in obtaining the accounts receivable; where all factors have registered, they shall obtain the accounts receivable in the order of registration time; where none have registered, the factor named in the notice of assignment that first reaches the accounts receivable debtor shall obtain the accounts receivable; where neither registration nor notice has occurred, the factors shall obtain the accounts receivable in proportion to the factoring financing funds or service remuneration.

<a id="art-769"></a>第七百六十九条　本章没有规定的，适用本编第六章债权转让的有关规定。

**Article 769** Matters not covered in this Chapter shall be governed by the relevant provisions in Chapter 6 of this Part on the assignment of claims.

### 第十七章　承揽合同

<a id="art-770"></a>第七百七十条　承揽合同是承揽人按照定作人的要求完成工作，交付工作成果，定作人支付报酬的合同。  

**Article 770** A work contract is a contract under which a contractor completes work in accordance with the requirements of the client and delivers the work product, and the client pays remuneration.
Work includes processing, manufacturing, repairing, reproducing, testing, inspecting, and similar activities.
承揽包括加工、定作、修理、复制、测试、检验等工作。

<a id="art-771"></a>第七百七十一条　承揽合同的内容一般包括承揽的标的、数量、质量、报酬，承揽方式，材料的提供，履行期限，验收标准和方法等条款。

**Article 771** The content of a work contract generally includes clauses such as the subject matter, quantity, quality, remuneration of the work, the method of performance, the provision of materials, the time limit for performance, and the standards and methods for acceptance inspection.

<a id="art-772"></a>第七百七十二条　承揽人应当以自己的设备、技术和劳力，完成主要工作，但是当事人另有约定的除外。  

**Article 772** A contractor shall complete the principal work with its own equipment, technology, and labor, unless the parties agree otherwise.
Where the contractor entrusts the principal work it has contracted to a third party, it shall be liable to the client for the work product completed by that third party; if the client does not consent to such entrustment, the client may also dissolve the contract.
承揽人将其承揽的主要工作交由第三人完成的，应当就该第三人完成的工作成果向定作人负责；未经定作人同意的，定作人也可以解除合同。

<a id="art-773"></a>第七百七十三条　承揽人可以将其承揽的辅助工作交由第三人完成。承揽人将其承揽的辅助工作交由第三人完成的，应当就该第三人完成的工作成果向定作人负责。

**Article 773** A contractor may entrust the ancillary work it has contracted to a third party. Where the contractor entrusts the ancillary work it has contracted to a third party, it shall be liable to the client for the work product completed by that third party.

<a id="art-774"></a>第七百七十四条　承揽人提供材料的，应当按照约定选用材料，并接受定作人检验。

**Article 774** Where the contractor provides materials, it shall select and use the materials in accordance with the agreement, and shall submit to inspection by the client.

<a id="art-775"></a>第七百七十五条　定作人提供材料的，应当按照约定提供材料。承揽人对定作人提供的材料应当及时检验，发现不符合约定时，应当及时通知定作人更换、补齐或者采取其他补救措施。  

**Article 775** Where the client provides materials, it shall provide them in accordance with the agreement. The contractor shall promptly inspect the materials provided by the client, and if it finds them non-conforming with the agreement, it shall promptly notify the client to replace, supplement, or take other remedial measures.
The contractor shall not replace, without authorization, the materials provided by the client, nor shall it replace parts that do not require repair.
承揽人不得擅自更换定作人提供的材料，不得更换不需要修理的零部件。

<a id="art-776"></a>第七百七十六条　承揽人发现定作人提供的图纸或者技术要求不合理的，应当及时通知定作人。因定作人怠于答复等原因造成承揽人损失的，应当赔偿损失。

**Article 776** Where the contractor discovers that the drawings or technical requirements provided by the client are unreasonable, it shall promptly notify the client. If the client's failure to respond or other reasons cause losses to the contractor, the client shall compensate for such losses.

<a id="art-777"></a>第七百七十七条　定作人中途变更承揽工作的要求，造成承揽人损失的，应当赔偿损失。

**Article 777** Where the client changes the requirements for the work during its performance, causing losses to the contractor, the client shall compensate for such losses.

<a id="art-778"></a>第七百七十八条　承揽工作需要定作人协助的，定作人有协助的义务。定作人不履行协助义务致使承揽工作不能完成的，承揽人可以催告定作人在合理期限内履行义务，并可以顺延履行期限；定作人逾期不履行的，承揽人可以解除合同。

**Article 778** Where the work requires the client's assistance, the client has a duty to assist. If the client fails to perform its duty of assistance, causing the work to be impossible to complete, the contractor may demand that the client perform its duty within a reasonable time limit, and may extend the time limit for performance; if the client fails to perform within the extended period, the contractor may dissolve the contract.

<a id="art-779"></a>第七百七十九条　承揽人在工作期间，应当接受定作人必要的监督检验。定作人不得因监督检验妨碍承揽人的正常工作。

**Article 779** During the performance of the work, the contractor shall accept the necessary supervision and inspection by the client. The client shall not hinder the contractor's normal work due to such supervision and inspection.

<a id="art-780"></a>第七百八十条　承揽人完成工作的，应当向定作人交付工作成果，并提交必要的技术资料和有关质量证明。定作人应当验收该工作成果。

**Article 780** Upon completion of the work, the contractor shall deliver the work product to the client and submit the necessary technical documents and relevant quality certificates. The client shall accept and inspect the work product.

<a id="art-781"></a>第七百八十一条　承揽人交付的工作成果不符合质量要求的，定作人可以合理选择请求承揽人承担修理、重作、减少报酬、赔偿损失等违约责任。

**Article 781** Where the work product delivered by the contractor does not meet the quality requirements, the client may reasonably choose to request the contractor to bear liability for breach of contract such as repair, rework, reduction of remuneration, and compensation for losses.

<a id="art-782"></a>第七百八十二条　定作人应当按照约定的期限支付报酬。对支付报酬的期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，定作人应当在承揽人交付工作成果时支付；工作成果部分交付的，定作人应当相应支付。

**Article 782** The client shall pay the remuneration within the agreed period. Where there is no agreement or the agreement on the period for payment of remuneration is unclear, and cannot be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 510 of this Law, the client shall pay the remuneration upon delivery of the work product by the contractor; where the work product is delivered in part, the client shall make proportionate payment.

<a id="art-783"></a>第七百八十三条　定作人未向承揽人支付报酬或者材料费等价款的，承揽人对完成的工作成果享有留置权或者有权拒绝交付，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 783** Where the client fails to pay the contractor the remuneration or material costs or other equivalent amounts, the contractor shall have a lien on the completed work product or the right to refuse delivery, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-784"></a>第七百八十四条　承揽人应当妥善保管定作人提供的材料以及完成的工作成果，因保管不善造成毁损、灭失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 784** The contractor shall properly keep the materials provided by the client and the completed work product, and shall bear liability for compensation for any damage or loss caused by improper keeping.

<a id="art-785"></a>第七百八十五条　承揽人应当按照定作人的要求保守秘密，未经定作人许可，不得留存复制品或者技术资料。

**Article 785** The contractor shall keep the secrets according to the requirements of the client, and shall not retain copies or technical materials without the permission of the client.

<a id="art-786"></a>第七百八十六条　共同承揽人对定作人承担连带责任，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 786** Co-contractors shall bear joint and several liability to the client, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-787"></a>第七百八十七条　定作人在承揽人完成工作前可以随时解除合同，造成承揽人损失的，应当赔偿损失。

**Article 787** The client may rescind the contract at any time before the contractor completes the work, but shall compensate the contractor for any losses caused thereby.

### 第十八章　建设工程合同

<a id="art-788"></a>第七百八十八条　建设工程合同是承包人进行工程建设，发包人支付价款的合同。  

**Article 788** A construction project contract is a contract whereby the contractor undertakes the construction of a project and the contractee pays the price.
Construction project contracts include contracts for survey, design, and construction of projects.
建设工程合同包括工程勘察、设计、施工合同。

<a id="art-789"></a>第七百八十九条　建设工程合同应当采用书面形式。

**Article 789** A construction project contract shall be in writing.

<a id="art-790"></a>第七百九十条　建设工程的招标投标活动，应当依照有关法律的规定公开、公平、公正进行。

**Article 790** The tendering and bidding activities for construction projects shall be conducted openly, fairly, and impartially in accordance with the provisions of relevant laws.

<a id="art-791"></a>第七百九十一条　发包人可以与总承包人订立建设工程合同，也可以分别与勘察人、设计人、施工人订立勘察、设计、施工承包合同。发包人不得将应当由一个承包人完成的建设工程支解成若干部分发包给数个承包人。  

**Article 791** The contractee may enter into a construction project contract with a general contractor, or may enter into separate contracts for survey, design, and construction with the surveyor, designer, and constructor. The contractee shall not divide a construction project that should be completed by one contractor into several parts and contract them out to several contractors.
With the consent of the contractee, the general contractor or the survey, design, or construction contractor may subcontract part of its contracted work to a third party. The third party shall bear joint and several liability to the contractee with the general contractor or the survey, design, or construction contractor for the work product it completes. The contractor shall not subcontract the entire construction project it has contracted to a third party, nor shall it divide the entire construction project it has contracted and subcontract it in the name of subcontracting.
Contractors are prohibited from subcontracting projects to entities that do not possess corresponding qualifications. Subcontracting units are prohibited from re-subcontracting the projects they have contracted. The construction of the main structure of a construction project must be completed by the contractor itself.
总承包人或者勘察、设计、施工承包人经发包人同意，可以将自己承包的部分工作交由第三人完成。第三人就其完成的工作成果与总承包人或者勘察、设计、施工承包人向发包人承担连带责任。承包人不得将其承包的全部建设工程转包给第三人或者将其承包的全部建设工程支解以后以分包的名义分别转包给第三人。  
禁止承包人将工程分包给不具备相应资质条件的单位。禁止分包单位将其承包的工程再分包。建设工程主体结构的施工必须由承包人自行完成。

<a id="art-792"></a>第七百九十二条　国家重大建设工程合同，应当按照国家规定的程序和国家批准的投资计划、可行性研究报告等文件订立。

**Article 792** Contracts for major national construction projects shall be entered into in accordance with the procedures prescribed by the State and documents such as the investment plan approved by the State and the feasibility study report.

<a id="art-793"></a>第七百九十三条　建设工程施工合同无效，但是建设工程经验收合格的，可以参照合同关于工程价款的约定折价补偿承包人。  

**Article 793** Where a construction project construction contract is void, but the construction project has passed acceptance inspection, the contractor may be compensated by a discounted amount with reference to the agreement on project price in the contract.
Where a construction project construction contract is void and the construction project has failed the acceptance inspection, the matter shall be handled as follows:
(1) if the construction project passes the acceptance inspection after repair, the contractee may request the contractor to bear the repair costs;
(2) if the construction project fails the acceptance inspection after repair, the contractor shall have no right to request compensation by a discounted amount with reference to the agreement on project price in the contract.
Where the contractee is at fault for losses caused by the construction project failing to meet the standards, it shall bear corresponding liability.
建设工程施工合同无效，且建设工程经验收不合格的，按照以下情形处理：  
（一）修复后的建设工程经验收合格的，发包人可以请求承包人承担修复费用；  
（二）修复后的建设工程经验收不合格的，承包人无权请求参照合同关于工程价款的约定折价补偿。  
发包人对因建设工程不合格造成的损失有过错的，应当承担相应的责任。

<a id="art-794"></a>第七百九十四条　勘察、设计合同的内容一般包括提交有关基础资料和概预算等文件的期限、质量要求、费用以及其他协作条件等条款。

**Article 794** The content of a survey or design contract generally includes clauses such as the time limit for submitting relevant basic data and documents such as budgetary estimates and final accounts, quality requirements, fees, and other conditions for cooperation.

<a id="art-795"></a>第七百九十五条　施工合同的内容一般包括工程范围、建设工期、中间交工工程的开工和竣工时间、工程质量、工程造价、技术资料交付时间、材料和设备供应责任、拨款和结算、竣工验收、质量保修范围和质量保证期、相互协作等条款。

**Article 795** The content of a construction contract generally includes clauses such as the scope of the project, construction period, commencement and completion times of intermediate handover works, project quality, project cost, time for delivery of technical data, responsibility for supply of materials and equipment, disbursement and settlement, completion acceptance, scope and period of quality warranty, and mutual cooperation.

<a id="art-796"></a>第七百九十六条　建设工程实行监理的，发包人应当与监理人采用书面形式订立委托监理合同。发包人与监理人的权利和义务以及法律责任，应当依照本编委托合同以及其他有关法律、行政法规的规定。

**Article 796** Where supervision is implemented for a construction project, the contractee shall enter into a commission supervision contract in writing with the supervisor. The rights, obligations, and legal liabilities of the contractee and the supervisor shall be governed by the provisions of this Part on commission contracts and other relevant laws and administrative regulations.

<a id="art-797"></a>第七百九十七条　发包人在不妨碍承包人正常作业的情况下，可以随时对作业进度、质量进行检查。

**Article 797** The contractee may, without hindering the normal operation of the contractor, inspect the progress and quality of the work at any time.

<a id="art-798"></a>第七百九十八条　隐蔽工程在隐蔽以前，承包人应当通知发包人检查。发包人没有及时检查的，承包人可以顺延工程日期，并有权请求赔偿停工、窝工等损失。

**Article 798** Before a concealed project is covered, the contractor shall notify the contractee for inspection. If the contractee fails to conduct the inspection in a timely manner, the contractor may extend the project period and has the right to request compensation for losses such as work stoppage and idle labor.

<a id="art-799"></a>第七百九十九条　建设工程竣工后，发包人应当根据施工图纸及说明书、国家颁发的施工验收规范和质量检验标准及时进行验收。验收合格的，发包人应当按照约定支付价款，并接收该建设工程。  

**Article 799** After the completion of a construction project, the contractee shall promptly conduct acceptance inspection in accordance with the construction drawings and specifications, the construction acceptance standards issued by the State, and the quality inspection standards. If the project passes the acceptance inspection, the contractee shall pay the price in accordance with the agreement and take delivery of the construction project.
A completed construction project may be delivered for use only after it passes the acceptance inspection; it shall not be delivered for use if no acceptance inspection has been conducted or the inspection has been failed.
建设工程竣工经验收合格后，方可交付使用；未经验收或者验收不合格的，不得交付使用。

<a id="art-800"></a>第八百条　勘察、设计的质量不符合要求或者未按照期限提交勘察、设计文件拖延工期，造成发包人损失的，勘察人、设计人应当继续完善勘察、设计，减收或者免收勘察、设计费并赔偿损失。

**Article 800** Where the quality of the survey or design fails to meet the requirements, or the survey or design documents are not submitted within the time limit, delaying the project schedule and causing losses to the contractee, the surveyor or designer shall continue to perfect the survey or design, reduce or waive the survey or design fees, and compensate for the losses.

<a id="art-801"></a>第八百零一条　因施工人的原因致使建设工程质量不符合约定的，发包人有权请求施工人在合理期限内无偿修理或者返工、改建。经过修理或者返工、改建后，造成逾期交付的，施工人应当承担违约责任。

<a id="art-802"></a>第八百零二条　因承包人的原因致使建设工程在合理使用期限内造成人身损害和财产损失的，承包人应当承担赔偿责任。

<a id="art-803"></a>第八百零三条　发包人未按照约定的时间和要求提供原材料、设备、场地、资金、技术资料的，承包人可以顺延工程日期，并有权请求赔偿停工、窝工等损失。

<a id="art-804"></a>第八百零四条　因发包人的原因致使工程中途停建、缓建的，发包人应当采取措施弥补或者减少损失，赔偿承包人因此造成的停工、窝工、倒运、机械设备调迁、材料和构件积压等损失和实际费用。

<a id="art-805"></a>第八百零五条　因发包人变更计划，提供的资料不准确，或者未按照期限提供必需的勘察、设计工作条件而造成勘察、设计的返工、停工或者修改设计，发包人应当按照勘察人、设计人实际消耗的工作量增付费用。

<a id="art-806"></a>第八百零六条　承包人将建设工程转包、违法分包的，发包人可以解除合同。  
发包人提供的主要建筑材料、建筑构配件和设备不符合强制性标准或者不履行协助义务，致使承包人无法施工，经催告后在合理期限内仍未履行相应义务的，承包人可以解除合同。  
合同解除后，已经完成的建设工程质量合格的，发包人应当按照约定支付相应的工程价款；已经完成的建设工程质量不合格的，参照[本法第七百九十三条](#art-793)的规定处理。

<a id="art-807"></a>第八百零七条　发包人未按照约定支付价款的，承包人可以催告发包人在合理期限内支付价款。发包人逾期不支付的，除根据建设工程的性质不宜折价、拍卖外，承包人可以与发包人协议将该工程折价，也可以请求人民法院将该工程依法拍卖。建设工程的价款就该工程折价或者拍卖的价款优先受偿。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）.md#art-35" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）》第35条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）.md#art-36" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理建设工程施工合同纠纷案件适用法律问题的解释（一）》第36条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院关于审理执行异议之诉案件适用法律问题的解释.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理执行异议之诉案件适用法律问题的解释》第17条引用">[3]</a></sup>

<a id="art-808"></a>第八百零八条　本章没有规定的，适用承揽合同的有关规定。

### 第十九章　运输合同

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-809"></a>第八百零九条　运输合同是承运人将旅客或者货物从起运地点运输到约定地点，旅客、托运人或者收货人支付票款或者运输费用的合同。

<a id="art-810"></a>第八百一十条　从事公共运输的承运人不得拒绝旅客、托运人通常、合理的运输要求。

**Article 810** A carrier engaged in public transport shall not refuse the normal and reasonable transport requests of passengers or consignors.

<a id="art-811"></a>第八百一十一条　承运人应当在约定期限或者合理期限内将旅客、货物安全运输到约定地点。

**Article 811** The carrier shall transport the passenger or cargo safely to the agreed destination within the agreed period or a reasonable period.

<a id="art-812"></a>第八百一十二条　承运人应当按照约定的或者通常的运输路线将旅客、货物运输到约定地点。

**Article 812** The carrier shall transport the passenger or cargo to the agreed destination via the agreed or customary transport route.

<a id="art-813"></a>第八百一十三条　旅客、托运人或者收货人应当支付票款或者运输费用。承运人未按照约定路线或者通常路线运输增加票款或者运输费用的，旅客、托运人或者收货人可以拒绝支付增加部分的票款或者运输费用。

**Article 813** The passenger, consignor, or consignee shall pay the fare or freight charges. If, due to the carrier's failure to transport via the agreed or customary route, the fare or freight charges are increased, the passenger, consignor, or consignee may refuse to pay the increased portion.

#### 第二节　客运合同

<a id="art-814"></a>第八百一十四条　客运合同自承运人向旅客出具客票时成立，但是当事人另有约定或者另有交易习惯的除外。

**Article 814** A passenger transport contract is formed when the carrier issues a ticket to the passenger, unless the parties have agreed otherwise or there is a customary practice to the contrary.

<a id="art-815"></a>第八百一十五条　旅客应当按照有效客票记载的时间、班次和座位号乘坐。旅客无票乘坐、超程乘坐、越级乘坐或者持不符合减价条件的优惠客票乘坐的，应当补交票款，承运人可以按照规定加收票款；旅客不支付票款的，承运人可以拒绝运输。  

**Article 815** A passenger shall travel in accordance with the time, flight number, and seat number specified on the valid ticket. If a passenger travels without a ticket, exceeds the destination, upgrades without authorization, or uses a discounted ticket for which they do not qualify, they shall make up the fare, and the carrier may charge an additional fare in accordance with the regulations; if the passenger fails to pay the fare, the carrier may refuse transport.
If a passenger under a real-name passenger transport contract loses their ticket, they may request the carrier to report the loss and reissue the ticket, and the carrier shall not charge the fare again or any other unreasonable fees.
实名制客运合同的旅客丢失客票的，可以请求承运人挂失补办，承运人不得再次收取票款和其他不合理费用。

<a id="art-816"></a>第八百一十六条　旅客因自己的原因不能按照客票记载的时间乘坐的，应当在约定的期限内办理退票或者变更手续；逾期办理的，承运人可以不退票款，并不再承担运输义务。

**Article 816** If a passenger, for their own reasons, cannot travel at the time specified on the ticket, they shall go through the procedures for refund or change within the agreed period; if they fail to do so within the period, the carrier may refuse to refund the fare and shall no longer be obliged to transport them.

<a id="art-817"></a>第八百一十七条　旅客随身携带行李应当符合约定的限量和品类要求；超过限量或者违反品类要求携带行李的，应当办理托运手续。

**Article 817** The luggage a passenger carries on their person shall comply with the agreed limits and category requirements; if the luggage exceeds the limits or violates the category requirements, the passenger shall go through the consignment procedures.

<a id="art-818"></a>第八百一十八条　旅客不得随身携带或者在行李中夹带易燃、易爆、有毒、有腐蚀性、有放射性以及可能危及运输工具上人身和财产安全的危险物品或者违禁物品。  

**Article 818** A passenger shall not carry on their person or conceal in their luggage any dangerous or prohibited articles that are flammable, explosive, toxic, corrosive, radioactive, or likely to endanger the safety of persons or property on the means of transport.
If a passenger violates the preceding paragraph, the carrier may unload, destroy, or deliver the dangerous or prohibited articles to the relevant authorities. If the passenger insists on carrying or concealing such dangerous or prohibited articles, the carrier shall refuse transport.
旅客违反前款规定的，承运人可以将危险物品或者违禁物品卸下、销毁或者送交有关部门。旅客坚持携带或者夹带危险物品或者违禁物品的，承运人应当拒绝运输。

<a id="art-819"></a>第八百一十九条　承运人应当严格履行安全运输义务，及时告知旅客安全运输应当注意的事项。旅客对承运人为安全运输所作的合理安排应当积极协助和配合。

**Article 819** The carrier shall strictly perform its obligation of safe transport and promptly inform passengers of matters requiring attention for safe transport. Passengers shall actively assist and cooperate with the carrier's reasonable arrangements for safe transport.

<a id="art-820"></a>第八百二十条　承运人应当按照有效客票记载的时间、班次和座位号运输旅客。承运人迟延运输或者有其他不能正常运输情形的，应当及时告知和提醒旅客，采取必要的安置措施，并根据旅客的要求安排改乘其他班次或者退票；由此造成旅客损失的，承运人应当承担赔偿责任，但是不可归责于承运人的除外。

**Article 820** The carrier shall transport passengers in accordance with the time, flight number, and seat number specified on the valid ticket. If the carrier delays transport or is otherwise unable to provide normal transport, it shall promptly notify and remind passengers, take necessary accommodation measures, and arrange for passengers to change to another flight or provide a refund at their request; if this causes losses to passengers, the carrier shall bear compensation liability, unless the cause is not attributable to the carrier.

<a id="art-821"></a>第八百二十一条　承运人擅自降低服务标准的，应当根据旅客的请求退票或者减收票款；提高服务标准的，不得加收票款。

**Article 821** Where a carrier arbitrarily lowers its service standards, it shall, at the request of the passenger, refund the ticket or reduce the fare; where it raises the service standards, it shall not charge an additional fare.

<a id="art-822"></a>第八百二十二条　承运人在运输过程中，应当尽力救助患有急病、分娩、遇险的旅客。

**Article 822** The carrier shall, during the course of transport, use its best endeavors to rescue passengers who are suffering from acute diseases, are in childbirth, or are in danger.

<a id="art-823"></a>第八百二十三条　承运人应当对运输过程中旅客的伤亡承担赔偿责任；但是，伤亡是旅客自身健康原因造成的或者承运人证明伤亡是旅客故意、重大过失造成的除外。  

**Article 823** The carrier shall bear compensation liability for the personal injury or death of a passenger during the course of transport, except where the injury or death is caused by the passenger's own health condition, or the carrier proves that the injury or death is caused by the passenger's intentional conduct or gross negligence.
The provisions of the preceding paragraph apply to passengers who, in accordance with the applicable provisions, are exempt from a ticket, hold a preferential ticket, or ride without a ticket with the carrier's permission.
前款规定适用于按照规定免票、持优待票或者经承运人许可搭乘的无票旅客。

<a id="art-824"></a>第八百二十四条　在运输过程中旅客随身携带物品毁损、灭失，承运人有过错的，应当承担赔偿责任。  

**Article 824** Where the hand luggage carried by a passenger is damaged or lost during the course of transport, the carrier shall bear compensation liability if it is at fault.
Where the checked luggage of a passenger is damaged or lost, the relevant provisions on the carriage of goods apply.
旅客托运的行李毁损、灭失的，适用货物运输的有关规定。

#### 第三节　货运合同

<a id="art-825"></a>第八百二十五条　托运人办理货物运输，应当向承运人准确表明收货人的姓名、名称或者凭指示的收货人，货物的名称、性质、重量、数量，收货地点等有关货物运输的必要情况。  

**Article 825** When handling the carriage of goods, the consignor shall accurately inform the carrier of the name or designation of the consignee or the consignee to be notified, the name, nature, weight, and quantity of the goods, the place of receipt, and other necessary information relating to the carriage of goods.
Where the carrier suffers a loss due to the consignor's false declaration or omission of material information, the consignor shall bear compensation liability.
因托运人申报不实或者遗漏重要情况，造成承运人损失的，托运人应当承担赔偿责任。

<a id="art-826"></a>第八百二十六条　货物运输需要办理审批、检验等手续的，托运人应当将办理完有关手续的文件提交承运人。

**Article 826** Where the carriage of goods requires formalities such as examination and approval or inspection, the consignor shall submit to the carrier the documents evidencing completion of such formalities.

<a id="art-827"></a>第八百二十七条　托运人应当按照约定的方式包装货物。对包装方式没有约定或者约定不明确的，适用[本法第六百一十九条](#art-619)的规定。  

**Article 827** The consignor shall package the goods in the agreed manner. Where there is no agreement on the packaging manner or the agreement is unclear, the provisions of Article 619 of this Law apply.
Where the consignor violates the provisions of the preceding paragraph, the carrier may refuse to transport the goods.
托运人违反前款规定的，承运人可以拒绝运输。

<a id="art-828"></a>第八百二十八条　托运人托运易燃、易爆、有毒、有腐蚀性、有放射性等危险物品的，应当按照国家有关危险物品运输的规定对危险物品妥善包装，做出危险物品标志和标签，并将有关危险物品的名称、性质和防范措施的书面材料提交承运人。  

**Article 828** Where the consignor consigns dangerous goods such as flammable, explosive, toxic, corrosive, or radioactive articles, it shall, in accordance with the relevant state provisions on the transport of dangerous goods, package the dangerous goods properly, affix warning signs and labels for dangerous goods, and submit to the carrier written documents concerning the name, nature, and precautionary measures of the dangerous goods.
Where the consignor violates the provisions of the preceding paragraph, the carrier may refuse to transport the goods or may take corresponding measures to avoid the occurrence of loss, and the expenses incurred thereby shall be borne by the consignor.
托运人违反前款规定的，承运人可以拒绝运输，也可以采取相应措施以避免损失的发生，因此产生的费用由托运人负担。

<a id="art-829"></a>第八百二十九条　在承运人将货物交付收货人之前，托运人可以要求承运人中止运输、返还货物、变更到达地或者将货物交给其他收货人，但是应当赔偿承运人因此受到的损失。

**Article 829** Before the carrier delivers the goods to the consignee, the consignor may request the carrier to discontinue the transport, return the goods, change the place of destination, or deliver the goods to another consignee, provided that it shall compensate the carrier for any loss suffered thereby.

<a id="art-830"></a>第八百三十条　货物运输到达后，承运人知道收货人的，应当及时通知收货人，收货人应当及时提货。收货人逾期提货的，应当向承运人支付保管费等费用。

**Article 830** After the goods have arrived at the place of destination, the carrier that is aware of the consignee shall promptly notify the consignee, and the consignee shall promptly take delivery of the goods. Where the consignee fails to take delivery within the time limit, it shall pay storage fees and other expenses to the carrier.

<a id="art-831"></a>第八百三十一条　收货人提货时应当按照约定的期限检验货物。对检验货物的期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，应当在合理期限内检验货物。收货人在约定的期限或者合理期限内对货物的数量、毁损等未提出异议的，视为承运人已经按照运输单证的记载交付的初步证据。

**Article 831** When taking delivery of the goods, the consignee shall inspect the goods within the agreed time limit. Where there is no agreement on the time limit for inspection or the agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 510 of this Law, the consignee shall inspect the goods within a reasonable time limit. Where the consignee does not raise any objection to the quantity or damage of the goods within the agreed time limit or within a reasonable time limit, it shall be deemed as prima facie evidence that the carrier has delivered the goods as described in the transport documents.

<a id="art-832"></a>第八百三十二条　承运人对运输过程中货物的毁损、灭失承担赔偿责任。但是，承运人证明货物的毁损、灭失是因不可抗力、货物本身的自然性质或者合理损耗以及托运人、收货人的过错造成的，不承担赔偿责任。

**Article 832** The carrier shall bear compensation liability for the damage or loss of goods during the course of transport. However, the carrier shall not bear compensation liability if it proves that the damage or loss of the goods is caused by force majeure, the natural nature of the goods themselves or reasonable wear and tear, or the fault of the consignor or the consignee.

<a id="art-833"></a>第八百三十三条　货物的毁损、灭失的赔偿额，当事人有约定的，按照其约定；没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，按照交付或者应当交付时货物到达地的市场价格计算。法律、行政法规对赔偿额的计算方法和赔偿限额另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 833** The amount of compensation for the damage or loss of the goods shall be calculated in accordance with the agreement between the parties, if any; where there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 510 of this Law, it shall be calculated based on the market price at the place of destination of the goods at the time of delivery or the time when delivery should have been made. Where laws or administrative regulations provide otherwise for the method of calculating the amount of compensation and the limitation of liability, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-834"></a>第八百三十四条　两个以上承运人以同一运输方式联运的，与托运人订立合同的承运人应当对全程运输承担责任；损失发生在某一运输区段的，与托运人订立合同的承运人和该区段的承运人承担连带责任。

**Article 834** Where two or more carriers operate through transport by the same mode of transport, the carrier that concludes the contract with the consignor shall be liable for the entire transport; where the loss occurs in a specific section of transport, the carrier that concludes the contract with the consignor and the carrier for that section shall bear joint and several liability.

<a id="art-835"></a>第八百三十五条　货物在运输过程中因不可抗力灭失，未收取运费的，承运人不得请求支付运费；已经收取运费的，托运人可以请求返还。法律另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 835** Where the goods are lost due to force majeure during the course of transport and the freight has not been collected, the carrier shall not be entitled to request payment of the freight; where the freight has already been collected, the consignor may request its return. Where the law provides otherwise, such provisions shall prevail.

<a id="art-836"></a>第八百三十六条　托运人或者收货人不支付运费、保管费或者其他费用的，承运人对相应的运输货物享有留置权，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 836** Where the consignor or the consignee fails to pay the freight, storage fees, or other expenses, the carrier shall have a lien on the corresponding transported goods, unless the parties have agreed otherwise.

<a id="art-837"></a>第八百三十七条　收货人不明或者收货人无正当理由拒绝受领货物的，承运人依法可以提存货物。

**Article 837** Where the consignee is unknown or the consignee refuses to take delivery of the goods without just cause, the carrier may deposit the goods in accordance with the law.

#### 第四节　多式联运合同

<a id="art-838"></a>第八百三十八条　多式联运经营人负责履行或者组织履行多式联运合同，对全程运输享有承运人的权利，承担承运人的义务。

**Article 838** The multimodal transport operator shall be responsible for performing or organizing the performance of the multimodal transport contract, and shall enjoy the rights and assume the obligations of a carrier for the entire carriage.

<a id="art-839"></a>第八百三十九条　多式联运经营人可以与参加多式联运的各区段承运人就多式联运合同的各区段运输约定相互之间的责任；但是，该约定不影响多式联运经营人对全程运输承担的义务。

**Article 839** The multimodal transport operator may agree with the carriers of the various sections participating in the multimodal transport on their respective responsibilities for the carriage in each section under the multimodal transport contract; however, such agreement shall not affect the obligations of the multimodal transport operator for the entire carriage.

<a id="art-840"></a>第八百四十条　多式联运经营人收到托运人交付的货物时，应当签发多式联运单据。按照托运人的要求，多式联运单据可以是可转让单据，也可以是不可转让单据。

**Article 840** Upon receiving the goods delivered by the consignor, the multimodal transport operator shall issue a multimodal transport document. At the request of the consignor, the multimodal transport document may be a negotiable document or a non-negotiable document.

<a id="art-841"></a>第八百四十一条　因托运人托运货物时的过错造成多式联运经营人损失的，即使托运人已经转让多式联运单据，托运人仍然应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 841** Where losses are caused to the multimodal transport operator due to the fault of the consignor when consigning the goods, the consignor shall still bear the liability for compensation even if the consignor has already transferred the multimodal transport document.

<a id="art-842"></a>第八百四十二条　货物的毁损、灭失发生于多式联运的某一运输区段的，多式联运经营人的赔偿责任和责任限额，适用调整该区段运输方式的有关法律规定；货物毁损、灭失发生的运输区段不能确定的，依照本章规定承担赔偿责任。

**Article 842** Where the damage to or loss of goods occurs during a certain transport section of the multimodal transport, the multimodal transport operator's liability for compensation and the limitation of liability shall be governed by the provisions of the relevant laws that regulate the mode of transport of that section; where the transport section in which such damage or loss occurs cannot be determined, the liability for compensation shall be borne in accordance with the provisions of this Chapter.

### 第二十章　技术合同

#### 第一节　一般规定

<a id="art-843"></a>第八百四十三条　技术合同是当事人就技术开发、转让、许可、咨询或者服务订立的确立相互之间权利和义务的合同。

**Article 843** A technology contract is a contract concluded by the parties to establish their mutual rights and obligations in respect of technology development, transfer, licensing, consulting, or services.

<a id="art-844"></a>第八百四十四条　订立技术合同，应当有利于知识产权的保护和科学技术的进步，促进科学技术成果的研发、转化、应用和推广。

**Article 844** A technology contract shall be concluded in a manner conducive to the protection of intellectual property and the advancement of science and technology, and to the promotion of the research and development, transformation, application, and dissemination of scientific and technological achievements.
