<a id="art-845"></a>第八百四十五条　技术合同的内容一般包括项目的名称，标的的内容、范围和要求，履行的计划、地点和方式，技术信息和资料的保密，技术成果的归属和收益的分配办法，验收标准和方法，名词和术语的解释等条款。  

**Article 845** The contents of a technology contract generally include clauses such as the name of the project, the subject matter, content, scope, and requirements, the plan, place, and method of performance, the confidentiality of technical information and materials, the ownership of technological achievements and the method of distribution of proceeds, the acceptance criteria and methods, and the explanation of terms and terminology.
Technical background materials, feasibility studies and technical evaluation reports, project task statements and plans, technical standards, technical specifications, original designs and process documents, and other technical documentation related to the performance of the contract may, as agreed by the parties, constitute a part of the contract.
Where a technology contract involves a patent, it shall indicate the name of the invention or creation, the patent applicant and patent holder, the date of application, the application number, the patent number, and the term of validity of the patent right.
与履行合同有关的技术背景资料、可行性论证和技术评价报告、项目任务书和计划书、技术标准、技术规范、原始设计和工艺文件，以及其他技术文档，按照当事人的约定可以作为合同的组成部分。  
技术合同涉及专利的，应当注明发明创造的名称、专利申请人和专利权人、申请日期、申请号、专利号以及专利权的有效期限。

<a id="art-846"></a>第八百四十六条　技术合同价款、报酬或者使用费的支付方式由当事人约定，可以采取一次总算、一次总付或者一次总算、分期支付，也可以采取提成支付或者提成支付附加预付入门费的方式。  

**Article 846** The method of payment of the price, remuneration, or royalties under a technology contract shall be agreed upon by the parties, and may be made by way of a lump sum payment, a lump sum payment in installments, a royalty payment, or a royalty payment with an initial upfront fee.
Where a royalty payment is agreed upon, it may be calculated as a certain percentage of the product price, or the increase in output value, profit, or product sales volume generated after the implementation of the patent or use of the technical know-how, or as otherwise agreed. The royalty percentage may be a fixed percentage, a progressively increasing percentage, or a progressively decreasing percentage.
Where a royalty payment is agreed upon, the parties may agree on a method for inspecting the relevant accounting books.
约定提成支付的，可以按照产品价格、实施专利和使用技术秘密后新增的产值、利润或者产品销售额的一定比例提成，也可以按照约定的其他方式计算。提成支付的比例可以采取固定比例、逐年递增比例或者逐年递减比例。  
约定提成支付的，当事人可以约定查阅有关会计账目的办法。

<a id="art-847"></a>第八百四十七条　职务技术成果的使用权、转让权属于法人或者非法人组织的，法人或者非法人组织可以就该项职务技术成果订立技术合同。法人或者非法人组织订立技术合同转让职务技术成果时，职务技术成果的完成人享有以同等条件优先受让的权利。  

**Article 847** Where the right to use and the right to transfer a job-related technological achievement vest in a legal person or an unincorporated organization, such legal person or unincorporated organization may conclude a technology contract regarding such job-related technological achievement. When a legal person or an unincorporated organization concludes a technology contract to transfer a job-related technological achievement, the person who accomplished such job-related technological achievement shall have the right of first refusal under the same conditions.
A job-related technological achievement is a technological achievement accomplished in the performance of a task assigned by a legal person or an unincorporated organization, or mainly by using the material and technical resources of such legal person or unincorporated organization.
职务技术成果是执行法人或者非法人组织的工作任务，或者主要是利用法人或者非法人组织的物质技术条件所完成的技术成果。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第2条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第3条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第4条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第6条引用">[4]</a></sup>

<a id="art-848"></a>第八百四十八条　非职务技术成果的使用权、转让权属于完成技术成果的个人，完成技术成果的个人可以就该项非职务技术成果订立技术合同。

**Article 848** The right to use and the right to transfer a non-job-related technological achievement vest in the individual who accomplished such technological achievement, and such individual may conclude a technology contract regarding such non-job-related technological achievement.

<a id="art-849"></a>第八百四十九条　完成技术成果的个人享有在有关技术成果文件上写明自己是技术成果完成者的权利和取得荣誉证书、奖励的权利。

**Article 849** An individual who accomplishes a technological achievement shall have the right to be named as the person who accomplished it in the relevant technological achievement documents, and the right to obtain honorary certificates and awards.

<a id="art-850"></a>第八百五十条　非法垄断技术或者侵害他人技术成果的技术合同无效。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第10条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-12" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第12条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 850** A technology contract that illegally monopolizes technology or infringes upon another person's technological achievements shall be void.

#### 第二节　技术开发合同

<a id="art-851"></a>第八百五十一条　技术开发合同是当事人之间就新技术、新产品、新工艺、新品种或者新材料及其系统的研究开发所订立的合同。  

**Article 851** A technology development contract is a contract concluded between the parties for the research and development of new technologies, new products, new processes, new varieties, or new materials, and their systems.
Technology development contracts include commissioned development contracts and collaborative development contracts.
A technology development contract shall be in written form.
A contract concluded between the parties for the purpose of implementing the transformation of scientific and technological achievements of practical value shall be governed by reference to the relevant provisions on technology development contracts.
技术开发合同包括委托开发合同和合作开发合同。  
技术开发合同应当采用书面形式。  
当事人之间就具有实用价值的科技成果实施转化订立的合同，参照适用技术开发合同的有关规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第17条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-18" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第18条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-852"></a>第八百五十二条　委托开发合同的委托人应当按照约定支付研究开发经费和报酬，提供技术资料，提出研究开发要求，完成协作事项，接受研究开发成果。

**Article 852** The commissioning party under a commissioned development contract shall, as agreed, pay the research and development funding and remuneration, provide technical materials, specify research and development requirements, complete collaborative matters, and accept the research and development results.

<a id="art-853"></a>第八百五十三条　委托开发合同的研究开发人应当按照约定制定和实施研究开发计划，合理使用研究开发经费，按期完成研究开发工作，交付研究开发成果，提供有关的技术资料和必要的技术指导，帮助委托人掌握研究开发成果。

**Article 853** The research and developer under a commissioned development contract shall, as agreed, formulate and implement a research and development plan, use the research and development funds reasonably, complete the research and development work on time, deliver the research and development results, provide relevant technical materials and necessary technical guidance, and assist the commissioning party in mastering the research and development results.

<a id="art-854"></a>第八百五十四条　委托开发合同的当事人违反约定造成研究开发工作停滞、延误或者失败的，应当承担违约责任。

**Article 854** Where a party to a commissioned development contract violates the agreement, causing the research and development work to be halted, delayed, or unsuccessful, it shall bear liability for breach of contract.

<a id="art-855"></a>第八百五十五条　合作开发合同的当事人应当按照约定进行投资，包括以技术进行投资，分工参与研究开发工作，协作配合研究开发工作。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第19条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 855** The parties to a collaborative development contract shall, as agreed, make investments, including investments in the form of technology, divide the work to participate in the research and development, and collaborate and cooperate in the research and development work.

<a id="art-856"></a>第八百五十六条　合作开发合同的当事人违反约定造成研究开发工作停滞、延误或者失败的，应当承担违约责任。

**Article 856** Where a party to a collaborative development contract violates the agreement, causing the research and development work to be halted, delayed, or unsuccessful, it shall bear liability for breach of contract.

<a id="art-857"></a>第八百五十七条　作为技术开发合同标的的技术已经由他人公开，致使技术开发合同的履行没有意义的，当事人可以解除合同。

**Article 857** Where the technology that is the subject matter of a technology development contract has been disclosed by another party, rendering the performance of the contract meaningless, the parties may rescind the contract.

<a id="art-858"></a>第八百五十八条　技术开发合同履行过程中，因出现无法克服的技术困难，致使研究开发失败或者部分失败的，该风险由当事人约定；没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，风险由当事人合理分担。  

**Article 858** Where, during the performance of a technology development contract, the research and development fails or partially fails due to insurmountable technical difficulties, the risk shall be borne as agreed by the parties; if there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined pursuant to Article 510 of this Law, the risk shall be reasonably apportioned among the parties.
Where one party discovers a circumstance as specified in the preceding paragraph that may cause the research and development to fail or partially fail, it shall promptly notify the other party and take appropriate measures to reduce the loss; if it fails to promptly notify and take appropriate measures, causing the loss to expand, it shall bear liability for the expanded loss.
当事人一方发现前款规定的可能致使研究开发失败或者部分失败的情形时，应当及时通知另一方并采取适当措施减少损失；没有及时通知并采取适当措施，致使损失扩大的，应当就扩大的损失承担责任。

<a id="art-859"></a>第八百五十九条　委托开发完成的发明创造，除法律另有规定或者当事人另有约定外，申请专利的权利属于研究开发人。研究开发人取得专利权的，委托人可以依法实施该专利。  

**Article 859** Unless otherwise provided by law or agreed by the parties, the right to apply for a patent for an invention or creation accomplished through commissioned development shall belong to the research and developer. Where the research and developer obtains the patent right, the commissioning party may exploit the patent in accordance with the law.
Where the research and developer transfers the right to apply for a patent, the commissioning party shall have the right of first refusal under the same conditions.
研究开发人转让专利申请权的，委托人享有以同等条件优先受让的权利。

<a id="art-860"></a>第八百六十条　合作开发完成的发明创造，申请专利的权利属于合作开发的当事人共有；当事人一方转让其共有的专利申请权的，其他各方享有以同等条件优先受让的权利。但是，当事人另有约定的除外。  

**Article 860** The right to apply for a patent for an invention or creation accomplished through collaborative development shall be jointly owned by the parties to the collaborative development; where one party transfers its share of the joint right to apply for a patent, the other parties shall have the right of first refusal under the same conditions, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.
Where one party to the collaborative development renounces its share of the joint right to apply for a patent, the other party or parties may file the application separately or jointly, unless otherwise agreed by the parties. Where the applicant obtains the patent right, the party that renounced the right to apply for the patent may exploit the patent free of charge.
Where one party to the collaborative development does not agree to file a patent application, the other party or parties may not file the patent application.
合作开发的当事人一方声明放弃其共有的专利申请权的，除当事人另有约定外，可以由另一方单独申请或者由其他各方共同申请。申请人取得专利权的，放弃专利申请权的一方可以免费实施该专利。  
合作开发的当事人一方不同意申请专利的，另一方或者其他各方不得申请专利。

<a id="art-861"></a>第八百六十一条　委托开发或者合作开发完成的技术秘密成果的使用权、转让权以及收益的分配办法，由当事人约定；没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，在没有相同技术方案被授予专利权前，当事人均有使用和转让的权利。但是，委托开发的研究开发人不得在向委托人交付研究开发成果之前，将研究开发成果转让给第三人。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-20" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第20条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 861** : The right to use, the right to transfer, and the method for distributing the benefits of technical secret achievements developed through commissioned development or cooperative development shall be agreed upon by the parties. If there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, and it still cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, each party shall have the right to use and transfer the achievements before the same technical solution is granted a patent right. However, the developer in commissioned development may not transfer the research and development achievements to a third party before delivering them to the commissioning party.

#### 第三节　技术转让合同和技术许可合同

<a id="art-862"></a>第八百六十二条　技术转让合同是合法拥有技术的权利人，将现有特定的专利、专利申请、技术秘密的相关权利让与他人所订立的合同。  

**Article 862** : A technology transfer contract is a contract under which the right holder lawfully owning the technology assigns to another party the relevant rights to an existing specific patent, patent application, or technical secret. A technology license contract is a contract under which the right holder lawfully owning the technology licenses another party to exploit or use the relevant rights to an existing specific patent or technical secret. The stipulations in a technology transfer contract or a technology license contract concerning the provision of specialized equipment or raw materials for exploiting the technology, or the provision of relevant technical consultation or technical services, shall be part of the contract.
技术许可合同是合法拥有技术的权利人，将现有特定的专利、技术秘密的相关权利许可他人实施、使用所订立的合同。  
技术转让合同和技术许可合同中关于提供实施技术的专用设备、原材料或者提供有关的技术咨询、技术服务的约定，属于合同的组成部分。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-22" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第22条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-863"></a>第八百六十三条　技术转让合同包括专利权转让、专利申请权转让、技术秘密转让等合同。  

**Article 863** : Technology transfer contracts include contracts for the transfer of patent rights, the transfer of patent application rights, and the transfer of technical secrets. Technology license contracts include contracts for patent exploitation licenses and technical secret use licenses. Technology transfer contracts and technology license contracts shall be made in writing.
技术许可合同包括专利实施许可、技术秘密使用许可等合同。  
技术转让合同和技术许可合同应当采用书面形式。

<a id="art-864"></a>第八百六十四条　技术转让合同和技术许可合同可以约定实施专利或者使用技术秘密的范围，但是不得限制技术竞争和技术发展。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-28" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第28条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 864** : Technology transfer contracts and technology license contracts may specify the scope of exploiting the patent or using the technical secret, but they shall not restrict technological competition or technological development.

<a id="art-865"></a>第八百六十五条　专利实施许可合同仅在该专利权的存续期限内有效。专利权有效期限届满或者专利权被宣告无效的，专利权人不得就该专利与他人订立专利实施许可合同。

**Article 865** : A patent exploitation license contract shall be valid only within the duration of the patent right. If the term of validity of the patent right expires or the patent right is declared invalid, the patentee may not enter into a patent exploitation license contract with another party regarding that patent.

<a id="art-866"></a>第八百六十六条　专利实施许可合同的许可人应当按照约定许可被许可人实施专利，交付实施专利有关的技术资料，提供必要的技术指导。

**Article 866** : The licensor under a patent exploitation license contract shall, in accordance with the agreement, license the licensee to exploit the patent, deliver the technical materials related to the exploitation of the patent, and provide necessary technical guidance.

<a id="art-867"></a>第八百六十七条　专利实施许可合同的被许可人应当按照约定实施专利，不得许可约定以外的第三人实施该专利，并按照约定支付使用费。

**Article 867** : The licensee under a patent exploitation license contract shall exploit the patent in accordance with the agreement, shall not license a third party other than those agreed upon to exploit the patent, and shall pay the royalties as agreed.

<a id="art-868"></a>第八百六十八条　技术秘密转让合同的让与人和技术秘密使用许可合同的许可人应当按照约定提供技术资料，进行技术指导，保证技术的实用性、可靠性，承担保密义务。  

**Article 868** : The transferor under a technical secret transfer contract and the licensor under a technical secret use license contract shall, in accordance with the agreement, provide technical materials, provide technical guidance, ensure the practicality and reliability of the technology, and assume the obligation of confidentiality. The confidentiality obligation specified in the preceding paragraph shall not restrict the licensor from applying for a patent, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.
前款规定的保密义务，不限制许可人申请专利，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

<a id="art-869"></a>第八百六十九条　技术秘密转让合同的受让人和技术秘密使用许可合同的被许可人应当按照约定使用技术，支付转让费、使用费，承担保密义务。

**Article 869** : The transferee under a technical secret transfer contract and the licensee under a technical secret use license contract shall use the technology in accordance with the agreement, pay the transfer fee or royalties, and assume the obligation of confidentiality.

<a id="art-870"></a>第八百七十条　技术转让合同的让与人和技术许可合同的许可人应当保证自己是所提供的技术的合法拥有者，并保证所提供的技术完整、无误、有效，能够达到约定的目标。

**Article 870** : The transferor under a technology transfer contract and the licensor under a technology license contract shall guarantee that they are the lawful owner of the technology provided and that the technology provided is complete, error-free, effective, and capable of achieving the agreed objectives.

<a id="art-871"></a>第八百七十一条　技术转让合同的受让人和技术许可合同的被许可人应当按照约定的范围和期限，对让与人、许可人提供的技术中尚未公开的秘密部分，承担保密义务。

**Article 871** : The transferee under a technology transfer contract and the licensee under a technology license contract shall, within the agreed scope and period, assume the obligation of confidentiality with respect to the undisclosed secret part of the technology provided by the transferor or licensor.

<a id="art-872"></a>第八百七十二条　许可人未按照约定许可技术的，应当返还部分或者全部使用费，并应当承担违约责任；实施专利或者使用技术秘密超越约定的范围的，违反约定擅自许可第三人实施该项专利或者使用该项技术秘密的，应当停止违约行为，承担违约责任；违反约定的保密义务的，应当承担违约责任。  

**Article 872** : If the licensor fails to license the technology in accordance with the agreement, it shall refund part or all of the royalties and shall bear liability for breach of contract. If the licensor exploits the patent or uses the technical secret beyond the agreed scope, or, in violation of the agreement, licenses a third party to exploit the patent or use the technical secret without authorization, it shall cease the breach and bear liability for breach of contract. If the licensor violates the agreed confidentiality obligation, it shall bear liability for breach of contract. The liability for breach of contract of the transferor shall be determined with reference to the provisions of the preceding paragraph.
让与人承担违约责任，参照适用前款规定。

<a id="art-873"></a>第八百七十三条　被许可人未按照约定支付使用费的，应当补交使用费并按照约定支付违约金；不补交使用费或者支付违约金的，应当停止实施专利或者使用技术秘密，交还技术资料，承担违约责任；实施专利或者使用技术秘密超越约定的范围的，未经许可人同意擅自许可第三人实施该专利或者使用该技术秘密的，应当停止违约行为，承担违约责任；违反约定的保密义务的，应当承担违约责任。  

**Article 873** : If the licensee fails to pay the royalties in accordance with the agreement, it shall pay the arrears of royalties and pay liquidated damages as agreed; if it fails to pay the arrears of royalties or liquidated damages, it shall cease exploiting the patent or using the technical secret, return the technical materials, and bear liability for breach of contract. If the licensee exploits the patent or uses the technical secret beyond the agreed scope, or, without the licensor's consent, licenses a third party to exploit the patent or use the technical secret without authorization, it shall cease the breach and bear liability for breach of contract. If the licensee violates the agreed confidentiality obligation, it shall bear liability for breach of contract. The liability for breach of contract of the transferee shall be determined with reference to the provisions of the preceding paragraph.
受让人承担违约责任，参照适用前款规定。

<a id="art-874"></a>第八百七十四条　受让人或者被许可人按照约定实施专利、使用技术秘密侵害他人合法权益的，由让与人或者许可人承担责任，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 874** : If the transferee or licensee infringes upon another person's lawful rights and interests by exploiting the patent or using the technical secret in accordance with the agreement, the transferor or licensor shall bear the liability, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.

<a id="art-875"></a>第八百七十五条　当事人可以按照互利的原则，在合同中约定实施专利、使用技术秘密后续改进的技术成果的分享办法；没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，一方后续改进的技术成果，其他各方无权分享。

**Article 875** : The parties may, in accordance with the principle of mutual benefit, agree in the contract on the method for sharing the technical achievements arising from the subsequent improvement of the exploited patent or the used technical secret. If there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, and it still cannot be determined in accordance with Article 510 of this Law, the other parties shall have no right to share the technical achievements subsequently improved by one party.

<a id="art-876"></a>第八百七十六条　集成电路布图设计专有权、植物新品种权、计算机软件著作权等其他知识产权的转让和许可，参照适用本节的有关规定。

**Article 876** : The transfer and licensing of other intellectual property rights, such as the exclusive right of layout design of integrated circuits, the right of new plant varieties, and the copyright of computer software, shall be subject to the relevant provisions of this Section by reference.

<a id="art-877"></a>第八百七十七条　法律、行政法规对技术进出口合同或者专利、专利申请合同另有规定的，依照其规定。

**Article 877** : Where laws or administrative regulations provide otherwise on technology import and export contracts or contracts for patents or patent applications, such provisions shall prevail.

#### 第四节　技术咨询合同和技术服务合同

<a id="art-878"></a>第八百七十八条　技术咨询合同是当事人一方以技术知识为对方就特定技术项目提供可行性论证、技术预测、专题技术调查、分析评价报告等所订立的合同。  

**Article 878** : A technical consultation contract is a contract under which one party, using its technical knowledge, provides the other party with feasibility studies, technical forecasts, special technical investigations, analysis and evaluation reports, and the like, for a specific technical project. A technical service contract is a contract under which one party, using its technical knowledge, solves a specific technical problem for the other party, excluding a work contract and a construction project contract.
技术服务合同是当事人一方以技术知识为对方解决特定技术问题所订立的合同，不包括承揽合同和建设工程合同。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-30" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第30条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-33" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第33条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-879"></a>第八百七十九条　技术咨询合同的委托人应当按照约定阐明咨询的问题，提供技术背景材料及有关技术资料，接受受托人的工作成果，支付报酬。

**Article 879** : The client under a technical consultation contract shall, in accordance with the agreement, clarify the issues for consultation, provide technical background materials and relevant technical data, accept the work results of the受托人, and pay the remuneration.

<a id="art-880"></a>第八百八十条　技术咨询合同的受托人应当按照约定的期限完成咨询报告或者解答问题，提出的咨询报告应当达到约定的要求。

**Article 880** : The受托人 under a technical consultation contract shall complete the consultation report or answer questions within the agreed period, and the consultation report submitted shall meet the agreed requirements.

<a id="art-881"></a>第八百八十一条　技术咨询合同的委托人未按照约定提供必要的资料，影响工作进度和质量，不接受或者逾期接受工作成果的，支付的报酬不得追回，未支付的报酬应当支付。  

**Article 881** Where the client under a technical consulting contract fails to provide necessary information as agreed, thereby affecting the progress and quality of the work, or does not accept or accepts the work results later than the agreed time, the remuneration already paid shall not be recovered, and the unpaid remuneration shall be paid.
Where the受托人 under a technical consulting contract fails to submit the consulting report on time or the submitted consulting report does not comply with the agreement, it shall bear the liability for breach of contract such as reducing or exempting the remuneration.
The losses incurred from the decision made by the client under a technical consulting contract in accordance with the consulting report and opinions that meet the agreed requirements of the受托人 shall be borne by the client, unless otherwise agreed by the parties.
技术咨询合同的受托人未按期提出咨询报告或者提出的咨询报告不符合约定的，应当承担减收或者免收报酬等违约责任。  
技术咨询合同的委托人按照受托人符合约定要求的咨询报告和意见作出决策所造成的损失，由委托人承担，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

<a id="art-882"></a>第八百八十二条　技术服务合同的委托人应当按照约定提供工作条件，完成配合事项，接受工作成果并支付报酬。

**Article 882** The client under a technical service contract shall provide working conditions as agreed, complete the matters of cooperation, accept the work results, and pay the remuneration.

<a id="art-883"></a>第八百八十三条　技术服务合同的受托人应当按照约定完成服务项目，解决技术问题，保证工作质量，并传授解决技术问题的知识。

**Article 883** The受托人 under a technical service contract shall complete the service items as agreed, solve technical problems, guarantee the quality of work, and impart the knowledge for solving technical problems.

<a id="art-884"></a>第八百八十四条　技术服务合同的委托人不履行合同义务或者履行合同义务不符合约定，影响工作进度和质量，不接受或者逾期接受工作成果的，支付的报酬不得追回，未支付的报酬应当支付。  

**Article 884** Where the client under a technical service contract fails to perform its contractual obligations or performs them in a manner not conforming to the agreement, thereby affecting the progress and quality of the work, or does not accept or accepts the work results later than the agreed time, the remuneration already paid shall not be recovered, and the unpaid remuneration shall be paid.
Where the受托人 under a technical service contract fails to complete the service work as agreed, it shall bear the liability for breach of contract such as exemption from remuneration.
技术服务合同的受托人未按照约定完成服务工作的，应当承担免收报酬等违约责任。

<a id="art-885"></a>第八百八十五条　技术咨询合同、技术服务合同履行过程中，受托人利用委托人提供的技术资料和工作条件完成的新的技术成果，属于受托人。委托人利用受托人的工作成果完成的新的技术成果，属于委托人。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。

**Article 885** During the performance of a technical consulting contract or a technical service contract, new technological achievements made by the受托人 using the technical materials and working conditions provided by the client shall belong to the受托人. New technological achievements made by the client using the work results of the受托人 shall belong to the client. If the parties have otherwise agreed, such agreement shall prevail.

<a id="art-886"></a>第八百八十六条　技术咨询合同和技术服务合同对受托人正常开展工作所需费用的负担没有约定或者约定不明确的，由受托人负担。

**Article 886** Where a technical consulting contract or a technical service contract has no agreement or unclear agreement on the burden of expenses necessary for the受托人 to carry out its work normally, such expenses shall be borne by the受托人.

<a id="art-887"></a>第八百八十七条　法律、行政法规对技术中介合同、技术培训合同另有规定的，依照其规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-36" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第36条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/合同与债权/最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-38" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理技术合同纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第38条引用">[2]</a></sup>

**Article 887** Where laws or administrative regulations have otherwise provided for technology intermediary contracts or technology training contracts, such provisions shall prevail.

### 第二十一章　保管合同

<a id="art-888"></a>第八百八十八条　保管合同是保管人保管寄存人交付的保管物，并返还该物的合同。  

**Article 888** A deposit contract is a contract under which the depositary keeps the deposited item delivered by the depositor and returns such item.
Where a depositor engages in activities such as shopping, dining, or accommodation at the depositary's premises and deposits items at a designated place, it shall be deemed as a deposit, unless the parties have otherwise agreed or there is a different trade practice.
寄存人到保管人处从事购物、就餐、住宿等活动，将物品存放在指定场所的，视为保管，但是当事人另有约定或者另有交易习惯的除外。

<a id="art-889"></a>第八百八十九条　寄存人应当按照约定向保管人支付保管费。  

**Article 889** The depositor shall pay the deposit fee to the depositary as agreed.
Where the parties have no agreement or unclear agreement on the deposit fee, and it still cannot be determined in accordance with the provisions of Article 510 of this Law, it shall be deemed as a gratuitous deposit.
当事人对保管费没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，视为无偿保管。

<a id="art-890"></a>第八百九十条　保管合同自保管物交付时成立，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 890** A deposit contract shall be formed upon the delivery of the deposited item, unless the parties have otherwise agreed.

<a id="art-891"></a>第八百九十一条　寄存人向保管人交付保管物的，保管人应当出具保管凭证，但是另有交易习惯的除外。

**Article 891** Where the depositor delivers the deposited item to the depositary, the depositary shall issue a deposit voucher, unless there is a different trade practice.

<a id="art-892"></a>第八百九十二条　保管人应当妥善保管保管物。  

**Article 892** The depositary shall properly keep the deposited item.
The parties may agree on the place or method of deposit. Unless in an emergency or to protect the interests of the depositor, the depositary shall not change the place or method of deposit without authorization.
当事人可以约定保管场所或者方法。除紧急情况或者为维护寄存人利益外，不得擅自改变保管场所或者方法。

<a id="art-893"></a>第八百九十三条　寄存人交付的保管物有瑕疵或者根据保管物的性质需要采取特殊保管措施的，寄存人应当将有关情况告知保管人。寄存人未告知，致使保管物受损失的，保管人不承担赔偿责任；保管人因此受损失的，除保管人知道或者应当知道且未采取补救措施外，寄存人应当承担赔偿责任。

**Article 893** Where the deposited item delivered by the depositor has defects or requires special storage measures due to its nature, the depositor shall inform the depositary of the relevant circumstances. If the depositor fails to inform, causing loss to the deposited item, the depositary shall not be liable for compensation; if the depositary suffers loss as a result, the depositor shall be liable for compensation, unless the depositary knew or should have known and failed to take remedial measures.

<a id="art-894"></a>第八百九十四条　保管人不得将保管物转交第三人保管，但是当事人另有约定的除外。  

**Article 894** The depositary shall not transfer the deposited item to a third party for custody, unless the parties have otherwise agreed.
Where the depositary, in violation of the preceding paragraph, transfers the deposited item to a third party for custody, causing loss to the deposited item, it shall be liable for compensation.
保管人违反前款规定，将保管物转交第三人保管，造成保管物损失的，应当承担赔偿责任。

<a id="art-895"></a>第八百九十五条　保管人不得使用或者许可第三人使用保管物，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 895** The depositary shall not use the deposited item or permit a third party to use it, unless the parties have otherwise agreed.

<a id="art-896"></a>第八百九十六条　第三人对保管物主张权利的，除依法对保管物采取保全或者执行措施外，保管人应当履行向寄存人返还保管物的义务。  

**Article 896** Where a third party claims rights over the deposited item, the depositary shall perform the obligation to return the deposited item to the depositor, except where preservation or enforcement measures are taken against the deposited item in accordance with law.
Where a third party initiates a lawsuit against the depositary or applies for seizure of the deposited item, the depositary shall promptly notify the depositor.
第三人对保管人提起诉讼或者对保管物申请扣押的，保管人应当及时通知寄存人。

<a id="art-897"></a>第八百九十七条　保管期内，因保管人保管不善造成保管物毁损、灭失的，保管人应当承担赔偿责任。但是，无偿保管人证明自己没有故意或者重大过失的，不承担赔偿责任。

**Article 897** During the deposit period, if the deposited item is damaged or lost due to the depositary's failure to keep it properly, the depositary shall be liable for compensation. However, if the gratuitous depositary proves that it has no intention or gross negligence, it shall not be liable for compensation.

<a id="art-898"></a>第八百九十八条　寄存人寄存货币、有价证券或者其他贵重物品的，应当向保管人声明，由保管人验收或者封存；寄存人未声明的，该物品毁损、灭失后，保管人可以按照一般物品予以赔偿。

**Article 898** Where the depositor deposits currency, negotiable securities, or other valuable items, it shall declare them to the depositary, and the depositary shall inspect or seal them; if the depositor fails to declare, after such items are damaged or lost, the depositary may compensate according to the standard for ordinary items.

<a id="art-899"></a>第八百九十九条　寄存人可以随时领取保管物。  

**Article 899** The depositor may retrieve the deposited item at any time.
Where the parties have no agreement or unclear agreement on the deposit period, the depositary may request the depositor to retrieve the deposited item at any time; if the deposit period is agreed upon, the depositary shall not request the depositor to retrieve the deposited item before the expiry of the period, unless there is a special reason.
当事人对保管期限没有约定或者约定不明确的，保管人可以随时请求寄存人领取保管物；约定保管期限的，保管人无特别事由，不得请求寄存人提前领取保管物。

<a id="art-900"></a>第九百条　保管期限届满或者寄存人提前领取保管物的，保管人应当将原物及其孳息归还寄存人。

**Article 900** Upon the expiry of the deposit period or when the depositor retrieves the deposited item in advance, the depositary shall return the original item and its fruits to the depositor.

<a id="art-901"></a>第九百零一条　保管人保管货币的，可以返还相同种类、数量的货币；保管其他可替代物的，可以按照约定返还相同种类、品质、数量的物品。

<a id="art-902"></a>第九百零二条　有偿的保管合同，寄存人应当按照约定的期限向保管人支付保管费。  
当事人对支付期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，应当在领取保管物的同时支付。

<a id="art-903"></a>第九百零三条　寄存人未按照约定支付保管费或者其他费用的，保管人对保管物享有留置权，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

### 第二十二章　仓储合同

<a id="art-904"></a>第九百零四条　仓储合同是保管人储存存货人交付的仓储物，存货人支付仓储费的合同。

<a id="art-905"></a>第九百零五条　仓储合同自保管人和存货人意思表示一致时成立。

<a id="art-906"></a>第九百零六条　储存易燃、易爆、有毒、有腐蚀性、有放射性等危险物品或者易变质物品的，存货人应当说明该物品的性质，提供有关资料。  
存货人违反前款规定的，保管人可以拒收仓储物，也可以采取相应措施以避免损失的发生，因此产生的费用由存货人负担。  
保管人储存易燃、易爆、有毒、有腐蚀性、有放射性等危险物品的，应当具备相应的保管条件。

<a id="art-907"></a>第九百零七条　保管人应当按照约定对入库仓储物进行验收。保管人验收时发现入库仓储物与约定不符合的，应当及时通知存货人。保管人验收后，发生仓储物的品种、数量、质量不符合约定的，保管人应当承担赔偿责任。

<a id="art-908"></a>第九百零八条　存货人交付仓储物的，保管人应当出具仓单、入库单等凭证。

<a id="art-909"></a>第九百零九条　保管人应当在仓单上签名或者盖章。仓单包括下列事项：  
（一）存货人的姓名或者名称和住所；  
（二）仓储物的品种、数量、质量、包装及其件数和标记；  
（三）仓储物的损耗标准；  
（四）储存场所；  
（五）储存期限；  
（六）仓储费；  
（七）仓储物已经办理保险的，其保险金额、期间以及保险人的名称；  
（八）填发人、填发地和填发日期。

<a id="art-910"></a>第九百一十条　仓单是提取仓储物的凭证。存货人或者仓单持有人在仓单上背书并经保管人签名或者盖章的，可以转让提取仓储物的权利。

**Article 910** A warehouse receipt is the document for taking delivery of the stored goods. If the depositor or holder of the warehouse receipt endorses the receipt and the depositary signs or affixes its seal, the right to take delivery of the stored goods may be transferred.

<a id="art-911"></a>第九百一十一条　保管人根据存货人或者仓单持有人的要求，应当同意其检查仓储物或者提取样品。

**Article 911** Upon request of the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt, the depositary shall consent to their inspection of the stored goods or taking of samples.

<a id="art-912"></a>第九百一十二条　保管人发现入库仓储物有变质或者其他损坏的，应当及时通知存货人或者仓单持有人。

**Article 912** If the depositary discovers deterioration or other damage to the stored goods upon entry into the warehouse, it shall promptly notify the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt.

<a id="art-913"></a>第九百一十三条　保管人发现入库仓储物有变质或者其他损坏，危及其他仓储物的安全和正常保管的，应当催告存货人或者仓单持有人作出必要的处置。因情况紧急，保管人可以作出必要的处置；但是，事后应当将该情况及时通知存货人或者仓单持有人。

**Article 913** If the depositary discovers deterioration or other damage to the stored goods upon entry into the warehouse that endangers the safety and proper storage of other goods, it shall urge the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt to take necessary measures. In an emergency, the depositary may take necessary measures; however, it shall promptly notify the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt of such situation afterwards.

<a id="art-914"></a>第九百一十四条　当事人对储存期限没有约定或者约定不明确的，存货人或者仓单持有人可以随时提取仓储物，保管人也可以随时请求存货人或者仓单持有人提取仓储物，但是应当给予必要的准备时间。

**Article 914** If the parties have not agreed on the storage period or the agreement is unclear, the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt may take delivery of the stored goods at any time, and the depositary may also request the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt to take delivery of the stored goods at any time, provided that a reasonable preparation period shall be given.

<a id="art-915"></a>第九百一十五条　储存期限届满，存货人或者仓单持有人应当凭仓单、入库单等提取仓储物。存货人或者仓单持有人逾期提取的，应当加收仓储费；提前提取的，不减收仓储费。

**Article 915** Upon expiration of the storage period, the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt shall take delivery of the stored goods by presenting the warehouse receipt, entry note, or other certificates. If the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt takes delivery beyond the time limit, an additional warehousing fee shall be charged; if delivery is taken before the expiration of the period, the warehousing fee shall not be reduced.

<a id="art-916"></a>第九百一十六条　储存期限届满，存货人或者仓单持有人不提取仓储物的，保管人可以催告其在合理期限内提取；逾期不提取的，保管人可以提存仓储物。

**Article 916** If the depositor or the holder of the warehouse receipt fails to take delivery of the stored goods upon expiration of the storage period, the depositary may urge them to take delivery within a reasonable period; if delivery is not taken after the expiration of such period, the depositary may deposit the stored goods with competent authorities.

<a id="art-917"></a>第九百一十七条　储存期内，因保管不善造成仓储物毁损、灭失的，保管人应当承担赔偿责任。因仓储物本身的自然性质、包装不符合约定或者超过有效储存期造成仓储物变质、损坏的，保管人不承担赔偿责任。

**Article 917** If stored goods are damaged or lost during the storage period due to improper storage by the depositary, the depositary shall bear compensation liability. If deterioration or damage of the stored goods is caused by the natural properties of the goods themselves, improper packaging not conforming to the agreement, or exceeding the effective storage period, the depositary shall not bear compensation liability.

<a id="art-918"></a>第九百一十八条　本章没有规定的，适用保管合同的有关规定。

**Article 918** Matters not provided for in this Chapter shall be governed by the relevant provisions on deposit contracts.

### 第二十三章　委托合同

<a id="art-919"></a>第九百一十九条　委托合同是委托人和受托人约定，由受托人处理委托人事务的合同。

**Article 919** A commission contract is a contract under which the principal and the agent agree that the agent shall handle the affairs of the principal.

<a id="art-920"></a>第九百二十条　委托人可以特别委托受托人处理一项或者数项事务，也可以概括委托受托人处理一切事务。

**Article 920** A principal may specifically authorize the agent to handle one or several matters, or may generally authorize the agent to handle all matters.

<a id="art-921"></a>第九百二十一条　委托人应当预付处理委托事务的费用。受托人为处理委托事务垫付的必要费用，委托人应当偿还该费用并支付利息。

**Article 921** The principal shall prepay the expenses for handling the entrusted matter. For the necessary expenses advanced by the agent in handling the entrusted matter, the principal shall reimburse such expenses and pay interest thereon.

<a id="art-922"></a>第九百二十二条　受托人应当按照委托人的指示处理委托事务。需要变更委托人指示的，应当经委托人同意；因情况紧急，难以和委托人取得联系的，受托人应当妥善处理委托事务，但是事后应当将该情况及时报告委托人。

**Article 922** The agent shall handle the entrusted matter in accordance with the principal's instructions. If a change to the principal's instructions is necessary, the consent of the principal shall be obtained. Where it is difficult to contact the principal due to an emergency, the agent shall properly handle the entrusted matter; provided, however, that the agent shall, after the event, promptly report the situation to the principal.

<a id="art-923"></a>第九百二十三条　受托人应当亲自处理委托事务。经委托人同意，受托人可以转委托。转委托经同意或者追认的，委托人可以就委托事务直接指示转委托的第三人，受托人仅就第三人的选任及其对第三人的指示承担责任。转委托未经同意或者追认的，受托人应当对转委托的第三人的行为承担责任；但是，在紧急情况下受托人为了维护委托人的利益需要转委托第三人的除外。

**Article 923** The agent shall handle the entrusted matter personally. With the consent of the principal, the agent may sub-entrust the matter. Where the sub-entrustment is consented to or ratified, the principal may give instructions directly to the third party on the entrusted matter, and the agent shall be liable only for the selection of the third party and for the instructions given to the third party. Where the sub-entrustment is not consented to or ratified, the agent shall be liable for the acts of the third party; except that, in an emergency, the agent needs to sub-entrust the matter to a third party in order to protect the interests of the principal.

<a id="art-924"></a>第九百二十四条　受托人应当按照委托人的要求，报告委托事务的处理情况。委托合同终止时，受托人应当报告委托事务的结果。

**Article 924** The agent shall, at the request of the principal, report on the progress of the entrusted matter. Upon termination of the agency mandate contract, the agent shall report on the outcome of the entrusted matter.

<a id="art-925"></a>第九百二十五条　受托人以自己的名义，在委托人的授权范围内与第三人订立的合同，第三人在订立合同时知道受托人与委托人之间的代理关系的，该合同直接约束委托人和第三人；但是，有确切证据证明该合同只约束受托人和第三人的除外。

**Article 925** Where the agent enters into a contract with a third party in its own name but within the scope of authority granted by the principal, and the third party knew at the time of entering into the contract of the agency relationship between the agent and the principal, such contract shall directly bind the principal and the third party; except where there is definite evidence proving that the contract binds only the agent and the third party.

<a id="art-926"></a>第九百二十六条　受托人以自己的名义与第三人订立合同时，第三人不知道受托人与委托人之间的代理关系的，受托人因第三人的原因对委托人不履行义务，受托人应当向委托人披露第三人，委托人因此可以行使受托人对第三人的权利。但是，第三人与受托人订立合同时如果知道该委托人就不会订立合同的除外。  

**Article 926** Where, when the agent enters into a contract with a third party in its own name, the third party is unaware of the agency relationship between the agent and the principal, and the agent fails to perform its obligation to the principal due to reasons attributable to the third party, the agent shall disclose the third party to the principal, whereupon the principal may exercise the rights of the agent against the third party. However, this shall not apply if the third party would not have entered into the contract had it known the identity of the principal when contracting with the agent.

Where the agent fails to perform its obligation to the third party due to reasons attributable to the principal, the agent shall disclose the principal to the third party, whereupon the third party may elect to assert its rights against either the agent or the principal as the counterparty; provided that the third party shall not change its elected counterparty.

Where the principal exercises the rights of the agent against the third party, the third party may assert against the principal any defenses it has against the agent. Where the third party elects the principal as its counterparty, the principal may assert against the third party any defenses it has against the agent, as well as any defenses the agent has against the third party.
受托人因委托人的原因对第三人不履行义务，受托人应当向第三人披露委托人，第三人因此可以选择受托人或者委托人作为相对人主张其权利，但是第三人不得变更选定的相对人。  
委托人行使受托人对第三人的权利的，第三人可以向委托人主张其对受托人的抗辩。第三人选定委托人作为其相对人的，委托人可以向第三人主张其对受托人的抗辩以及受托人对第三人的抗辩。

<a id="art-927"></a>第九百二十七条　受托人处理委托事务取得的财产，应当转交给委托人。

**Article 927** The property acquired by the agent in the course of handling the entrusted matter shall be delivered to the principal.

<a id="art-928"></a>第九百二十八条　受托人完成委托事务的，委托人应当按照约定向其支付报酬。  

**Article 928** If the agent completes the entrusted matter, the principal shall pay the agent remuneration as agreed.

Where the agency mandate contract is dissolved or the entrusted matter cannot be completed due to reasons not attributable to the agent, the principal shall pay the agent corresponding remuneration. Where the parties have otherwise agreed, such agreement shall prevail.
因不可归责于受托人的事由，委托合同解除或者委托事务不能完成的，委托人应当向受托人支付相应的报酬。当事人另有约定的，按照其约定。

<a id="art-929"></a>第九百二十九条　有偿的委托合同，因受托人的过错造成委托人损失的，委托人可以请求赔偿损失。无偿的委托合同，因受托人的故意或者重大过失造成委托人损失的，委托人可以请求赔偿损失。  

**Article 929** For a remunerated agency mandate contract, if the agent causes loss to the principal through its fault, the principal may claim compensation for the loss. For a non-remunerated agency mandate contract, if the agent causes loss to the principal through its intentional act or gross negligence, the principal may claim compensation for the loss.

Where the agent causes loss to the principal by acting beyond its authority, it shall compensate for the loss.
受托人超越权限造成委托人损失的，应当赔偿损失。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》侵权责任编的解释（一）.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》侵权责任编的解释（一）》第10条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-930"></a>第九百三十条　受托人处理委托事务时，因不可归责于自己的事由受到损失的，可以向委托人请求赔偿损失。

**Article 930** If the agent suffers loss in the course of handling the entrusted matter due to reasons not attributable to itself, it may claim compensation for the loss from the principal.

<a id="art-931"></a>第九百三十一条　委托人经受托人同意，可以在受托人之外委托第三人处理委托事务。因此造成受托人损失的，受托人可以向委托人请求赔偿损失。

**Article 931** With the consent of the agent, the principal may entrust the entrusted matter to a third party in addition to the agent. If this causes loss to the agent, the agent may claim compensation for the loss from the principal.

<a id="art-932"></a>第九百三十二条　两个以上的受托人共同处理委托事务的，对委托人承担连带责任。

**Article 932** Where two or more agents jointly handle an entrusted matter, they shall bear joint and several liability to the principal.

<a id="art-933"></a>第九百三十三条　委托人或者受托人可以随时解除委托合同。因解除合同造成对方损失的，除不可归责于该当事人的事由外，无偿委托合同的解除方应当赔偿因解除时间不当造成的直接损失，有偿委托合同的解除方应当赔偿对方的直接损失和合同履行后可以获得的利益。

**Article 933** Either the principal or the agent may dissolve the agency mandate contract at any time. Where the dissolution of the contract causes loss to the other party, unless such loss is due to reasons not attributable to the dissolving party, the dissolving party under a non-remunerated agency mandate contract shall compensate for the direct loss caused by the improper timing of the dissolution, while the dissolving party under a remunerated agency mandate contract shall compensate for the other party's direct loss and the benefits that could have been obtained after performance of the contract.

<a id="art-934"></a>第九百三十四条　委托人死亡、终止或者受托人死亡、丧失民事行为能力、终止的，委托合同终止；但是，当事人另有约定或者根据委托事务的性质不宜终止的除外。

**Article 934** An agency mandate contract is terminated upon the death or cessation of existence of the principal, or upon the death, loss of civil capacity, or cessation of existence of the agent; except where the parties have otherwise agreed, or where it is inappropriate to terminate the contract given the nature of the entrusted matter.

<a id="art-935"></a>第九百三十五条　因委托人死亡或者被宣告破产、解散，致使委托合同终止将损害委托人利益的，在委托人的继承人、遗产管理人或者清算人承受委托事务之前，受托人应当继续处理委托事务。

**Article 935** Where termination of an agency mandate contract due to the principal's death or being declared bankrupt or dissolved would harm the interests of the principal, the agent shall continue to handle the entrusted matter until the principal's successor, estate administrator, or liquidator assumes the entrusted matter.

<a id="art-936"></a>第九百三十六条　因受托人死亡、丧失民事行为能力或者被宣告破产、解散，致使委托合同终止的，受托人的继承人、遗产管理人、法定代理人或者清算人应当及时通知委托人。因委托合同终止将损害委托人利益的，在委托人作出善后处理之前，受托人的继承人、遗产管理人、法定代理人或者清算人应当采取必要措施。

**Article 936** Where an agency mandate contract is terminated due to the agent's death, loss of civil capacity, or being declared bankrupt or dissolved, the agent's successor, estate administrator, legal representative, or liquidator shall promptly notify the principal. Where the termination of the contract would harm the interests of the principal, the agent's successor, estate administrator, legal representative, or liquidator shall take necessary measures before the principal undertakes subsequent arrangements.

### 第二十四章　物业服务合同

<a id="art-937"></a>第九百三十七条　物业服务合同是物业服务人在物业服务区域内，为业主提供建筑物及其附属设施的维修养护、环境卫生和相关秩序的管理维护等物业服务，业主支付物业费的合同。  

**Article 937** A property services contract is a contract whereby a property service provider provides property services, including the repair and maintenance of buildings and ancillary facilities, environmental hygiene, and management and maintenance of relevant order, within a property service area, and the owners pay property service fees.

Property service providers include property service enterprises and other managers.
物业服务人包括物业服务企业和其他管理人。

<a id="art-938"></a>第九百三十八条　物业服务合同的内容一般包括服务事项、服务质量、服务费用的标准和收取办法、维修资金的使用、服务用房的管理和使用、服务期限、服务交接等条款。  

**Article 938** The content of a property services contract generally includes terms such as service items, service quality, standards and collection methods for service fees, use of maintenance funds, management and use of service premises, service period, and service handover.

Service commitments publicly made by a property service provider that are favorable to the owners shall be an integral part of the property services contract.

A property services contract shall be in writing.
物业服务人公开作出的有利于业主的服务承诺，为物业服务合同的组成部分。  
物业服务合同应当采用书面形式。

<a id="art-939"></a>第九百三十九条　建设单位依法与物业服务人订立的前期物业服务合同，以及业主委员会与业主大会依法选聘的物业服务人订立的物业服务合同，对业主具有法律约束力。

**Article 939** The preliminary property services contract concluded between a construction unit and a property service provider in accordance with law, and the property services contract concluded between the owners' committee and the property service provider elected by the owners' assembly in accordance with law, shall have legal binding force on the owners.

<a id="art-940"></a>第九百四十条　建设单位依法与物业服务人订立的前期物业服务合同约定的服务期限届满前，业主委员会或者业主与新物业服务人订立的物业服务合同生效的，前期物业服务合同终止。

**Article 940** Where, before the expiry of the service period stipulated in a preliminary property services contract concluded between a construction unit and a property service provider in accordance with law, a property services contract concluded between the owners' committee or the owners and a new property service provider becomes effective, the preliminary property services contract shall be terminated.

<a id="art-941"></a>第九百四十一条　物业服务人将物业服务区域内的部分专项服务事项委托给专业性服务组织或者其他第三人的，应当就该部分专项服务事项向业主负责。  

**Article 941** Where a property service provider entrusts certain specialized service matters within the property service area to a professional service organization or another third party, it shall be liable to the owners for such specialized service matters.
A property service provider may not assign the entire property service it shall provide to a third party, or fragment the entire property service and assign it piecemeal to third parties.
物业服务人不得将其应当提供的全部物业服务转委托给第三人，或者将全部物业服务支解后分别转委托给第三人。

<a id="art-942"></a>第九百四十二条　物业服务人应当按照约定和物业的使用性质，妥善维修、养护、清洁、绿化和经营管理物业服务区域内的业主共有部分，维护物业服务区域内的基本秩序，采取合理措施保护业主的人身、财产安全。  

**Article 942** A property service provider shall, in accordance with the agreement and the nature of the property's use, properly repair, maintain, clean, landscape, and manage the common areas of the owners within the property service area, maintain basic order therein, and take reasonable measures to protect the safety of the owners' persons and property.
With respect to any act within the property service area that violates laws or regulations on public security, environmental protection, fire protection, or the like, the property service provider shall promptly take reasonable measures to stop it, report to the relevant administrative authority, and assist in handling it.
对物业服务区域内违反有关治安、环保、消防等法律法规的行为，物业服务人应当及时采取合理措施制止、向有关行政主管部门报告并协助处理。

<a id="art-943"></a>第九百四十三条　物业服务人应当定期将服务的事项、负责人员、质量要求、收费项目、收费标准、履行情况，以及维修资金使用情况、业主共有部分的经营与收益情况等以合理方式向业主公开并向业主大会、业主委员会报告。

**Article 943** A property service provider shall periodically disclose to the owners in a reasonable manner, and report to the owners' assembly and the owners' committee, the service items, responsible personnel, quality requirements, fee items, fee standards, performance status, as well as the use of maintenance funds, and the operation and income from the common areas of the owners.

<a id="art-944"></a>第九百四十四条　业主应当按照约定向物业服务人支付物业费。物业服务人已经按照约定和有关规定提供服务的，业主不得以未接受或者无需接受相关物业服务为由拒绝支付物业费。  

**Article 944** The owners shall pay property management fees to the property service provider as agreed. Where the property service provider has provided services in accordance with the agreement and relevant provisions, the owners may not refuse to pay the property management fees on the grounds that they have not accepted or do not need the relevant services.
If an owner fails to pay the property management fees within the agreed time limit in violation of the agreement, the property service provider may demand payment within a reasonable time limit by notice; if the owner still fails to pay upon expiry of the reasonable time limit, the property service provider may initiate legal proceedings or apply for arbitration.
A property service provider may not coerce payment of property management fees by suspending power supply, water supply, heat supply, gas supply, or the like.
业主违反约定逾期不支付物业费的，物业服务人可以催告其在合理期限内支付；合理期限届满仍不支付的，物业服务人可以提起诉讼或者申请仲裁。  
物业服务人不得采取停止供电、供水、供热、供燃气等方式催交物业费。

<a id="art-945"></a>第九百四十五条　业主装饰装修房屋的，应当事先告知物业服务人，遵守物业服务人提示的合理注意事项，并配合其进行必要的现场检查。  

**Article 945** Where an owner decorates or renovates the premises, he shall inform the property service provider in advance, comply with the reasonable precautions pointed out by the property service provider, and cooperate with it in conducting necessary on-site inspections.
Where an owner transfers or leases the exclusive part of the property, creates a right of habitation, or changes the use of the common parts in accordance with law, he shall promptly inform the property service provider of the relevant circumstances.
业主转让、出租物业专有部分、设立居住权或者依法改变共有部分用途的，应当及时将相关情况告知物业服务人。

<a id="art-946"></a>第九百四十六条　业主依照法定程序共同决定解聘物业服务人的，可以解除物业服务合同。决定解聘的，应当提前六十日书面通知物业服务人，但是合同对通知期限另有约定的除外。  

**Article 946** Where the owners jointly decide to dismiss the property service provider in accordance with the statutory procedures, they may dissolve the property service contract. Where they decide to dismiss, they shall notify the property service provider in writing sixty days in advance, unless the contract provides otherwise for the notice period.
Where the dissolution of the contract pursuant to the preceding paragraph causes losses to the property service provider, the owners shall compensate for the losses, unless the cause for the dissolution is not attributable to the owners.
依据前款规定解除合同造成物业服务人损失的，除不可归责于业主的事由外，业主应当赔偿损失。

<a id="art-947"></a>第九百四十七条　物业服务期限届满前，业主依法共同决定续聘的，应当与原物业服务人在合同期限届满前续订物业服务合同。  

**Article 947** Before the expiry of the property service term, if the owners jointly decide to re-engage the property service provider in accordance with law, they shall renew the property service contract with the original property service provider before the expiry of the contract term.
Before the expiry of the property service term, if the property service provider disagrees to being re-engaged, it shall notify the owners or the owners' committee in writing ninety days before the expiry of the contract term, unless the contract provides otherwise for the notice period.
物业服务期限届满前，物业服务人不同意续聘的，应当在合同期限届满前九十日书面通知业主或者业主委员会，但是合同对通知期限另有约定的除外。

<a id="art-948"></a>第九百四十八条　物业服务期限届满后，业主没有依法作出续聘或者另聘物业服务人的决定，物业服务人继续提供物业服务的，原物业服务合同继续有效，但是服务期限为不定期。  

**Article 948** After the expiry of the property service term, if the owners fail to make a decision in accordance with law to re-engage the existing property service provider or to engage another one, and the property service provider continues to provide property services, the original property service contract remains valid, but the service term is indefinite.
Either party may dissolve an indefinite-term property service contract at any time, but shall notify the other party in writing sixty days in advance.
当事人可以随时解除不定期物业服务合同，但是应当提前六十日书面通知对方。

<a id="art-949"></a>第九百四十九条　物业服务合同终止的，原物业服务人应当在约定期限或者合理期限内退出物业服务区域，将物业服务用房、相关设施、物业服务所必需的相关资料等交还给业主委员会、决定自行管理的业主或者其指定的人，配合新物业服务人做好交接工作，并如实告知物业的使用和管理状况。  

**Article 949** Upon termination of a property service contract, the original property service provider shall withdraw from the property service area within the agreed or a reasonable time limit, return the property service premises, relevant facilities, and the relevant materials necessary for the property service to the owners' committee, the owners who have decided to self-manage, or the persons designated by them, cooperate with the new property service provider in the handover, and truthfully inform them of the condition of the property's use and management.
If the original property service provider violates the provisions of the preceding paragraph, it may not request the owners to pay property management fees after the termination of the property service contract; if such violation causes losses to the owners, it shall compensate for the losses.
原物业服务人违反前款规定的，不得请求业主支付物业服务合同终止后的物业费；造成业主损失的，应当赔偿损失。

<a id="art-950"></a>第九百五十条　物业服务合同终止后，在业主或者业主大会选聘的新物业服务人或者决定自行管理的业主接管之前，原物业服务人应当继续处理物业服务事项，并可以请求业主支付该期间的物业费。

**Article 950** After the termination of a property service contract, the original property service provider shall continue to handle property service matters until a new property service provider selected by the owners or the owners' assembly, or the owners who have decided to self-manage, takes over, and may request the owners to pay the property management fees for this period.

### 第二十五章　行纪合同

<a id="art-951"></a>第九百五十一条　行纪合同是行纪人以自己的名义为委托人从事贸易活动，委托人支付报酬的合同。

**Article 951** A commission contract is a contract whereby a commission agent, in his own name, engages in trade activities for a principal, and the principal pays remuneration.

<a id="art-952"></a>第九百五十二条　行纪人处理委托事务支出的费用，由行纪人负担，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 952** The expenses incurred by the commission agent in handling the entrusted matter shall be borne by the commission agent, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-953"></a>第九百五十三条　行纪人占有委托物的，应当妥善保管委托物。

**Article 953** Where the commission agent is in possession of the subject matter, he shall keep it in good custody.

<a id="art-954"></a>第九百五十四条　委托物交付给行纪人时有瑕疵或者容易腐烂、变质的，经委托人同意，行纪人可以处分该物；不能与委托人及时取得联系的，行纪人可以合理处分。

**Article 954** If the subject matter delivered to the commission agent is defective or prone to decay or deterioration, the commission agent may dispose of it with the consent of the principal; if the commission agent is unable to contact the principal in a timely manner, he may dispose of it in a reasonable manner.

<a id="art-955"></a>第九百五十五条　行纪人低于委托人指定的价格卖出或者高于委托人指定的价格买入的，应当经委托人同意；未经委托人同意，行纪人补偿其差额的，该买卖对委托人发生效力。  

**Article 955** Where a commission agent sells at a price lower than, or buys at a price higher than, that designated by the principal, he shall obtain the consent of the principal; if the sale or purchase takes place without the consent of the principal but the commission agent makes up the difference, the sale or purchase is binding on the principal.
Where a commission agent sells at a price higher than, or buys at a price lower than, that designated by the principal, he may claim additional remuneration as agreed; if there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, and it cannot be determined under Article 510 of this Law, the benefit belongs to the principal.
If the principal gives specific instructions on the price, the commission agent may not sell or buy in violation of such instructions.
行纪人高于委托人指定的价格卖出或者低于委托人指定的价格买入的，可以按照约定增加报酬；没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，该利益属于委托人。  
委托人对价格有特别指示的，行纪人不得违背该指示卖出或者买入。

<a id="art-956"></a>第九百五十六条　行纪人卖出或者买入具有市场定价的商品，除委托人有相反的意思表示外，行纪人自己可以作为买受人或者出卖人。  

**Article 956** Where a commission agent sells or buys a commodity with a market price, unless the principal expresses an intention to the contrary, the commission agent may himself act as the buyer or seller.
Where the circumstances described in the preceding paragraph exist, the commission agent may still request the principal to pay remuneration.
行纪人有前款规定情形的，仍然可以请求委托人支付报酬。

<a id="art-957"></a>第九百五十七条　行纪人按照约定买入委托物，委托人应当及时受领。经行纪人催告，委托人无正当理由拒绝受领的，行纪人依法可以提存委托物。  

**Article 957** Where a commission agent buys the subject matter as agreed, the principal shall accept it in a timely manner. If, after being urged by the commission agent, the principal refuses to accept it without justifiable cause, the commission agent may deposit the subject matter in accordance with law.
If the subject matter cannot be sold or the principal withdraws the sale, and after being urged by the commission agent, the principal fails to retrieve or dispose of it, the commission agent may deposit the subject matter in accordance with law.
委托物不能卖出或者委托人撤回出卖，经行纪人催告，委托人不取回或者不处分该物的，行纪人依法可以提存委托物。

<a id="art-958"></a>第九百五十八条　行纪人与第三人订立合同的，行纪人对该合同直接享有权利、承担义务。  

**Article 958** Where a commission agent enters into a contract with a third party, the commission agent directly enjoys rights and assumes obligations under that contract.
If the third party's failure to perform its obligations causes damage to the principal, the commission agent shall be liable for compensation, unless otherwise agreed between the commission agent and the principal.
第三人不履行义务致使委托人受到损害的，行纪人应当承担赔偿责任，但是行纪人与委托人另有约定的除外。

<a id="art-959"></a>第九百五十九条　行纪人完成或者部分完成委托事务的，委托人应当向其支付相应的报酬。委托人逾期不支付报酬的，行纪人对委托物享有留置权，但是当事人另有约定的除外。

**Article 959** Where a commission agent completes or partially completes the entrusted matter, the principal shall pay corresponding remuneration to him. If the principal fails to pay the remuneration within the time limit, the commission agent has a lien on the subject matter, unless the parties agree otherwise.

<a id="art-960"></a>第九百六十条　本章没有规定的，参照适用委托合同的有关规定。

**Article 960** Matters not covered in this Chapter shall be governed by reference to the relevant provisions on commission contracts.

### 第二十六章　中介合同

<a id="art-961"></a>第九百六十一条　中介合同是中介人向委托人报告订立合同的机会或者提供订立合同的媒介服务，委托人支付报酬的合同。

<a id="art-962"></a>第九百六十二条　中介人应当就有关订立合同的事项向委托人如实报告。  
中介人故意隐瞒与订立合同有关的重要事实或者提供虚假情况，损害委托人利益的，不得请求支付报酬并应当承担赔偿责任。

<a id="art-963"></a>第九百六十三条　中介人促成合同成立的，委托人应当按照约定支付报酬。对中介人的报酬没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，根据中介人的劳务合理确定。因中介人提供订立合同的媒介服务而促成合同成立的，由该合同的当事人平均负担中介人的报酬。  
中介人促成合同成立的，中介活动的费用，由中介人负担。

<a id="art-964"></a>第九百六十四条　中介人未促成合同成立的，不得请求支付报酬；但是，可以按照约定请求委托人支付从事中介活动支出的必要费用。

<a id="art-965"></a>第九百六十五条　委托人在接受中介人的服务后，利用中介人提供的交易机会或者媒介服务，绕开中介人直接订立合同的，应当向中介人支付报酬。

<a id="art-966"></a>第九百六十六条　本章没有规定的，参照适用委托合同的有关规定。

### 第二十七章　合伙合同

<a id="art-967"></a>第九百六十七条　合伙合同是两个以上合伙人为了共同的事业目的，订立的共享利益、共担风险的协议。

<a id="art-968"></a>第九百六十八条　合伙人应当按照约定的出资方式、数额和缴付期限，履行出资义务。

<a id="art-969"></a>第九百六十九条　合伙人的出资、因合伙事务依法取得的收益和其他财产，属于合伙财产。  
合伙合同终止前，合伙人不得请求分割合伙财产。

<a id="art-970"></a>第九百七十条　合伙人就合伙事务作出决定的，除合伙合同另有约定外，应当经全体合伙人一致同意。  
合伙事务由全体合伙人共同执行。按照合伙合同的约定或者全体合伙人的决定，可以委托一个或者数个合伙人执行合伙事务；其他合伙人不再执行合伙事务，但是有权监督执行情况。  
合伙人分别执行合伙事务的，执行事务合伙人可以对其他合伙人执行的事务提出异议；提出异议后，其他合伙人应当暂停该项事务的执行。

<a id="art-971"></a>第九百七十一条　合伙人不得因执行合伙事务而请求支付报酬，但是合伙合同另有约定的除外。

<a id="art-972"></a>第九百七十二条　合伙的利润分配和亏损分担，按照合伙合同的约定办理；合伙合同没有约定或者约定不明确的，由合伙人协商决定；协商不成的，由合伙人按照实缴出资比例分配、分担；无法确定出资比例的，由合伙人平均分配、分担。

<a id="art-973"></a>第九百七十三条　合伙人对合伙债务承担连带责任。清偿合伙债务超过自己应当承担份额的合伙人，有权向其他合伙人追偿。

<a id="art-974"></a>第九百七十四条　除合伙合同另有约定外，合伙人向合伙人以外的人转让其全部或者部分财产份额的，须经其他合伙人一致同意。

<a id="art-975"></a>第九百七十五条　合伙人的债权人不得代位行使合伙人依照本章规定和合伙合同享有的权利，但是合伙人享有的利益分配请求权除外。

<a id="art-976"></a>第九百七十六条　合伙人对合伙期限没有约定或者约定不明确，依据[本法第五百一十条](#art-510)的规定仍不能确定的，视为不定期合伙。  
合伙期限届满，合伙人继续执行合伙事务，其他合伙人没有提出异议的，原合伙合同继续有效，但是合伙期限为不定期。  
合伙人可以随时解除不定期合伙合同，但是应当在合理期限之前通知其他合伙人。

<a id="art-977"></a>第九百七十七条　合伙人死亡、丧失民事行为能力或者终止的，合伙合同终止；但是，合伙合同另有约定或者根据合伙事务的性质不宜终止的除外。

<a id="art-978"></a>第九百七十八条　合伙合同终止后，合伙财产在支付因终止而产生的费用以及清偿合伙债务后有剩余的，依据[本法第九百七十二条](#art-972)的规定进行分配。  
第三分编　准合同

### 第二十八章　无因管理

<a id="art-979"></a>第九百七十九条　管理人没有法定的或者约定的义务，为避免他人利益受损失而管理他人事务的，可以请求受益人偿还因管理事务而支出的必要费用；管理人因管理事务受到损失的，可以请求受益人给予适当补偿。  
管理事务不符合受益人真实意思的，管理人不享有前款规定的权利；但是，受益人的真实意思违反法律或者违背公序良俗的除外。

<a id="art-980"></a>第九百八十条　管理人管理事务不属于前条规定的情形，但是受益人享有管理利益的，受益人应当在其获得的利益范围内向管理人承担前条第一款规定的义务。

<a id="art-981"></a>第九百八十一条　管理人管理他人事务，应当采取有利于受益人的方法。中断管理对受益人不利的，无正当理由不得中断。

**Article 981** Where a manager manages another person's affairs, he/she shall adopt methods beneficial to the beneficiary. If interrupting the management is detrimental to the beneficiary, the management shall not be interrupted without justifiable reasons.

<a id="art-982"></a>第九百八十二条　管理人管理他人事务，能够通知受益人的，应当及时通知受益人。管理的事务不需要紧急处理的，应当等待受益人的指示。

**Article 982** Where a manager manages another person's affairs and is able to notify the beneficiary, he/she shall notify the beneficiary in a timely manner. If the managed affairs do not require urgent handling, the manager shall await the beneficiary's instructions.

<a id="art-983"></a>第九百八十三条　管理结束后，管理人应当向受益人报告管理事务的情况。管理人管理事务取得的财产，应当及时转交给受益人。

**Article 983** Upon conclusion of the management, the manager shall report to the beneficiary on the status of the managed affairs. Any property obtained by the manager from managing the affairs shall be promptly transferred to the beneficiary.

<a id="art-984"></a>第九百八十四条　管理人管理事务经受益人事后追认的，从管理事务开始时起，适用委托合同的有关规定，但是管理人另有意思表示的除外。

**Article 984** Where the beneficiary subsequently ratifies the management of affairs by the manager, the relevant provisions on the entrustment contract shall apply from the beginning of the management of affairs, unless the manager expresses a contrary intention.

### 第二十九章　不当得利

<a id="art-985"></a>第九百八十五条　得利人没有法律根据取得不当利益的，受损失的人可以请求得利人返还取得的利益，但是有下列情形之一的除外：  

**Article 985** Where an enriched person obtains unjust benefits without legal basis, the aggrieved person may request the enriched person to return the benefits obtained, except under any of the following circumstances:
(1) the performance is made to fulfill a moral obligation;
(2) the payment is made before the debt becomes due;
(3) the debt settlement is made with the knowledge that there is no obligation to perform.
（一）为履行道德义务进行的给付；  
（二）债务到期之前的清偿；  
（三）明知无给付义务而进行的债务清偿。

<a id="art-986"></a>第九百八十六条　得利人不知道且不应当知道取得的利益没有法律根据，取得的利益已经不存在的，不承担返还该利益的义务。

**Article 986** Where a person enriched neither knew nor should have known that the benefits obtained lacked legal basis, and the benefits obtained no longer exist, such person shall not be obliged to return the benefits.

<a id="art-987"></a>第九百八十七条　得利人知道或者应当知道取得的利益没有法律根据的，受损失的人可以请求得利人返还其取得的利益并依法赔偿损失。

**Article 987** Where a person enriched knew or should have known that the benefits obtained lacked legal basis, the aggrieved person may request the enriched person to return the benefits obtained and to compensate for losses according to law.

<a id="art-988"></a>第九百八十八条　得利人已经将取得的利益无偿转让给第三人的，受损失的人可以请求第三人在相应范围内承担返还义务。

**Article 988** Where a person enriched has already gratuitously transferred the obtained benefits to a third party, the aggrieved person may request the third party to assume the obligation to return the benefits within the corresponding scope.

## 第四编　人格权

### 第一章　一般规定

<a id="art-989"></a>第九百八十九条　本编调整因人格权的享有和保护产生的民事关系。

**Article 989** This Book governs civil relations arising from the enjoyment and protection of personality rights.

<a id="art-990"></a>第九百九十条　人格权是民事主体享有的生命权、身体权、健康权、姓名权、名称权、肖像权、名誉权、荣誉权、隐私权等权利。  

**Article 990** Personality rights are the rights enjoyed by civil subjects, including the right to life, the right to personal integrity, the right to health, the right to name, the right to entity name, the right to portrait, the right to reputation, the right to honor, the right to privacy, and other rights.
In addition to the personality rights provided in the preceding paragraph, natural persons enjoy other personality rights and interests arising from personal freedom and personal dignity.
除前款规定的人格权外，自然人享有基于人身自由、人格尊严产生的其他人格权益。

<a id="art-991"></a>第九百九十一条　民事主体的人格权受法律保护，任何组织或者个人不得侵害。

**Article 991** The personality rights of civil subjects shall be protected by law, and no organization or individual may infringe upon them.

<a id="art-992"></a>第九百九十二条　人格权不得放弃、转让或者继承。

**Article 992** Personality rights shall not be waived, transferred, or inherited.

<a id="art-993"></a>第九百九十三条　民事主体可以将自己的姓名、名称、肖像等许可他人使用，但是依照法律规定或者根据其性质不得许可的除外。

**Article 993** A civil subject may license others to use his/her name, entity name, portrait, etc., except where such licensing is prohibited by legal provisions or by the nature of the right.

<a id="art-994"></a>第九百九十四条　死者的姓名、肖像、名誉、荣誉、隐私、遗体等受到侵害的，其配偶、子女、父母有权依法请求行为人承担民事责任；死者没有配偶、子女且父母已经死亡的，其他近亲属有权依法请求行为人承担民事责任。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理使用人脸识别技术处理个人信息相关民事案件适用法律若干问题的规定.md#art-15" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理使用人脸识别技术处理个人信息相关民事案件适用法律若干问题的规定》第15条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 994** Where the name, portrait, reputation, honor, privacy, or remains of a deceased person are infringed upon, his/her spouse, children, and parents shall have the right to request in accordance with law that the tortfeasor bear civil liability; if the deceased has no spouse or children and the parents are deceased, other close relatives shall have the right to request in accordance with law that the tortfeasor bear civil liability.

<a id="art-995"></a>第九百九十五条　人格权受到侵害的，受害人有权依照本法和其他法律的规定请求行为人承担民事责任。受害人的停止侵害、排除妨碍、消除危险、消除影响、恢复名誉、赔礼道歉请求权，不适用诉讼时效的规定。

**Article 995** Where a personality right is infringed upon, the victim shall have the right to request in accordance with the provisions of this Law and other laws that the tortfeasor bear civil liability. The victim's claim for cessation of infringement, removal of obstruction, elimination of danger, elimination of adverse effects, restoration of reputation, and apology shall not be subject to the statute of limitations.

<a id="art-996"></a>第九百九十六条　因当事人一方的违约行为，损害对方人格权并造成严重精神损害，受损害方选择请求其承担违约责任的，不影响受损害方请求精神损害赔偿。

**Article 996** Where a party's breach of contract damages the other party's personality rights and causes serious mental harm, the aggrieved party's choice to request the other party to bear liability for breach of contract shall not affect the aggrieved party's right to claim compensation for mental injury.

<a id="art-997"></a>第九百九十七条　民事主体有证据证明行为人正在实施或者即将实施侵害其人格权的违法行为，不及时制止将使其合法权益受到难以弥补的损害的，有权依法向人民法院申请采取责令行为人停止有关行为的措施。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-12" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第12条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 997** Where a civil subject has evidence that a tortfeasor is committing or is about to commit an illegal act infringing upon his/her personality right, and failure to stop it promptly will cause irreparable damage to his/her lawful rights and interests, he/she shall have the right to apply to the People's Court in accordance with law for an order requiring the tortfeasor to cease the relevant acts.

<a id="art-998"></a>第九百九十八条　认定行为人承担侵害除生命权、身体权和健康权外的人格权的民事责任，应当考虑行为人和受害人的职业、影响范围、过错程度，以及行为的目的、方式、后果等因素。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理使用人脸识别技术处理个人信息相关民事案件适用法律若干问题的规定.md#art-3" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理使用人脸识别技术处理个人信息相关民事案件适用法律若干问题的规定》第3条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 998** Factors such as the occupations of the tortfeasor and the victim, the scope of impact, the degree of fault, and the purpose, manner, and consequences of the act shall be considered in determining the tortfeasor's civil liability for infringing upon personality rights other than the right to life, personal integrity, and health.

<a id="art-999"></a>第九百九十九条　为公共利益实施新闻报道、舆论监督等行为的，可以合理使用民事主体的姓名、名称、肖像、个人信息等；使用不合理侵害民事主体人格权的，应当依法承担民事责任。

**Article 999** Where news reports or public opinion supervision are conducted for the public interest, the name, entity name, portrait, personal information, etc., of a civil subject may be reasonably used; if such use unreasonably infringes upon the personality rights of the civil subject, civil liability shall be borne in accordance with law.

<a id="art-1000"></a>第一千条　行为人因侵害人格权承担消除影响、恢复名誉、赔礼道歉等民事责任的，应当与行为的具体方式和造成的影响范围相当。  

**Article 1000** Where a tortfeasor bears civil liability such as elimination of adverse effects, restoration of reputation, or apology for infringing upon personality rights, such liability shall be commensurate with the specific manner of the act and the scope of its impact.
Where a tortfeasor refuses to bear the civil liability provided in the preceding paragraph, the People's Court may enforce it by means such as publishing an announcement in newspapers or on the internet or publishing the effective judgment, and the expenses incurred thereby shall be borne by the tortfeasor.
行为人拒不承担前款规定的民事责任的，人民法院可以采取在报刊、网络等媒体上发布公告或者公布生效裁判文书等方式执行，产生的费用由行为人负担。

<a id="art-1001"></a>第一千零一条　对自然人因婚姻家庭关系等产生的身份权利的保护，适用本法第一编、第五编和其他法律的相关规定；没有规定的，可以根据其性质参照适用本编人格权保护的有关规定。

### 第二章　生命权、身体权和健康权

<a id="art-1002"></a>第一千零二条　自然人享有生命权。自然人的生命安全和生命尊严受法律保护。任何组织或者个人不得侵害他人的生命权。

<a id="art-1003"></a>第一千零三条　自然人享有身体权。自然人的身体完整和行动自由受法律保护。任何组织或者个人不得侵害他人的身体权。

<a id="art-1004"></a>第一千零四条　自然人享有健康权。自然人的身心健康受法律保护。任何组织或者个人不得侵害他人的健康权。

<a id="art-1005"></a>第一千零五条　自然人的生命权、身体权、健康权受到侵害或者处于其他危难情形的，负有法定救助义务的组织或者个人应当及时施救。

<a id="art-1006"></a>第一千零六条　完全民事行为能力人有权依法自主决定无偿捐献其人体细胞、人体组织、人体器官、遗体。任何组织或者个人不得强迫、欺骗、利诱其捐献。  
完全民事行为能力人依据前款规定同意捐献的，应当采用书面形式，也可以订立遗嘱。  
自然人生前未表示不同意捐献的，该自然人死亡后，其配偶、成年子女、父母可以共同决定捐献，决定捐献应当采用书面形式。

<a id="art-1007"></a>第一千零七条　禁止以任何形式买卖人体细胞、人体组织、人体器官、遗体。  
违反前款规定的买卖行为无效。

<a id="art-1008"></a>第一千零八条　为研制新药、医疗器械或者发展新的预防和治疗方法，需要进行临床试验的，应当依法经相关主管部门批准并经伦理委员会审查同意，向受试者或者受试者的监护人告知试验目的、用途和可能产生的风险等详细情况，并经其书面同意。  
进行临床试验的，不得向受试者收取试验费用。

<a id="art-1009"></a>第一千零九条　从事与人体基因、人体胚胎等有关的医学和科研活动，应当遵守法律、行政法规和国家有关规定，不得危害人体健康，不得违背伦理道德，不得损害公共利益。

<a id="art-1010"></a>第一千零一十条　违背他人意愿，以言语、文字、图像、肢体行为等方式对他人实施性骚扰的，受害人有权依法请求行为人承担民事责任。  
机关、企业、学校等单位应当采取合理的预防、受理投诉、调查处置等措施，防止和制止利用职权、从属关系等实施性骚扰。

<a id="art-1011"></a>第一千零一十一条　以非法拘禁等方式剥夺、限制他人的行动自由，或者非法搜查他人身体的，受害人有权依法请求行为人承担民事责任。

### 第三章　姓名权和名称权

<a id="art-1012"></a>第一千零一十二条　自然人享有姓名权，有权依法决定、使用、变更或者许可他人使用自己的姓名，但是不得违背公序良俗。

<a id="art-1013"></a>第一千零一十三条　法人、非法人组织享有名称权，有权依法决定、使用、变更、转让或者许可他人使用自己的名称。

<a id="art-1014"></a>第一千零一十四条　任何组织或者个人不得以干涉、盗用、假冒等方式侵害他人的姓名权或者名称权。

<a id="art-1015"></a>第一千零一十五条　自然人应当随父姓或者母姓，但是有下列情形之一的，可以在父姓和母姓之外选取姓氏：  
（一）选取其他直系长辈血亲的姓氏；  
（二）因由法定扶养人以外的人扶养而选取扶养人姓氏；  
（三）有不违背公序良俗的其他正当理由。  
少数民族自然人的姓氏可以遵从本民族的文化传统和风俗习惯。

<a id="art-1016"></a>第一千零一十六条　自然人决定、变更姓名，或者法人、非法人组织决定、变更、转让名称的，应当依法向有关机关办理登记手续，但是法律另有规定的除外。  
民事主体变更姓名、名称的，变更前实施的民事法律行为对其具有法律约束力。

<a id="art-1017"></a>第一千零一十七条　具有一定社会知名度，被他人使用足以造成公众混淆的笔名、艺名、网名、译名、字号、姓名和名称的简称等，参照适用姓名权和名称权保护的有关规定。

### 第四章　肖像权

<a id="art-1018"></a>第一千零一十八条　自然人享有肖像权，有权依法制作、使用、公开或者许可他人使用自己的肖像。  
肖像是通过影像、雕塑、绘画等方式在一定载体上所反映的特定自然人可以被识别的外部形象。

<a id="art-1019"></a>第一千零一十九条　任何组织或者个人不得以丑化、污损，或者利用信息技术手段伪造等方式侵害他人的肖像权。未经肖像权人同意，不得制作、使用、公开肖像权人的肖像，但是法律另有规定的除外。  
未经肖像权人同意，肖像作品权利人不得以发表、复制、发行、出租、展览等方式使用或者公开肖像权人的肖像。

<a id="art-1020"></a>第一千零二十条　合理实施下列行为的，可以不经肖像权人同意：  
（一）为个人学习、艺术欣赏、课堂教学或者科学研究，在必要范围内使用肖像权人已经公开的肖像；  
（二）为实施新闻报道，不可避免地制作、使用、公开肖像权人的肖像；  
（三）为依法履行职责，国家机关在必要范围内制作、使用、公开肖像权人的肖像；  
（四）为展示特定公共环境，不可避免地制作、使用、公开肖像权人的肖像；  
（五）为维护公共利益或者肖像权人合法权益，制作、使用、公开肖像权人的肖像的其他行为。

<a id="art-1021"></a>第一千零二十一条　当事人对肖像许可使用合同中关于肖像使用条款的理解有争议的，应当作出有利于肖像权人的解释。

<a id="art-1022"></a>第一千零二十二条　当事人对肖像许可使用期限没有约定或者约定不明确的，任何一方当事人可以随时解除肖像许可使用合同，但是应当在合理期限之前通知对方。  
当事人对肖像许可使用期限有明确约定，肖像权人有正当理由的，可以解除肖像许可使用合同，但是应当在合理期限之前通知对方。因解除合同造成对方损失的，除不可归责于肖像权人的事由外，应当赔偿损失。

<a id="art-1023"></a>第一千零二十三条　对姓名等的许可使用，参照适用肖像许可使用的有关规定。  
对自然人声音的保护，参照适用肖像权保护的有关规定。

### 第五章　名誉权和荣誉权

<a id="art-1024"></a>第一千零二十四条　民事主体享有名誉权。任何组织或者个人不得以侮辱、诽谤等方式侵害他人的名誉权。  
名誉是对民事主体的品德、声望、才能、信用等的社会评价。

<a id="art-1025"></a>第一千零二十五条　行为人为公共利益实施新闻报道、舆论监督等行为，影响他人名誉的，不承担民事责任，但是有下列情形之一的除外：  
（一）捏造、歪曲事实；  
（二）对他人提供的严重失实内容未尽到合理核实义务；  
（三）使用侮辱性言辞等贬损他人名誉。

<a id="art-1026"></a>第一千零二十六条　认定行为人是否尽到前条第二项规定的合理核实义务，应当考虑下列因素：  
（一）内容来源的可信度；  
（二）对明显可能引发争议的内容是否进行了必要的调查；  
（三）内容的时限性；  
（四）内容与公序良俗的关联性；  
（五）受害人名誉受贬损的可能性；  
（六）核实能力和核实成本。

<a id="art-1027"></a>第一千零二十七条　行为人发表的文学、艺术作品以真人真事或者特定人为描述对象，含有侮辱、诽谤内容，侵害他人名誉权的，受害人有权依法请求该行为人承担民事责任。  
行为人发表的文学、艺术作品不以特定人为描述对象，仅其中的情节与该特定人的情况相似的，不承担民事责任。

<a id="art-1028"></a>第一千零二十八条　民事主体有证据证明报刊、网络等媒体报道的内容失实，侵害其名誉权的，有权请求该媒体及时采取更正或者删除等必要措施。

<a id="art-1029"></a>第一千零二十九条　民事主体可以依法查询自己的信用评价；发现信用评价不当的，有权提出异议并请求采取更正、删除等必要措施。信用评价人应当及时核查，经核查属实的，应当及时采取必要措施。

<a id="art-1030"></a>第一千零三十条　民事主体与征信机构等信用信息处理者之间的关系，适用本编有关个人信息保护的规定和其他法律、行政法规的有关规定。

<a id="art-1031"></a>第一千零三十一条　民事主体享有荣誉权。任何组织或者个人不得非法剥夺他人的荣誉称号，不得诋毁、贬损他人的荣誉。  
获得的荣誉称号应当记载而没有记载的，民事主体可以请求记载；获得的荣誉称号记载错误的，民事主体可以请求更正。

### 第六章　隐私权和个人信息保护

<a id="art-1032"></a>第一千零三十二条　自然人享有隐私权。任何组织或者个人不得以刺探、侵扰、泄露、公开等方式侵害他人的隐私权。  
隐私是自然人的私人生活安宁和不愿为他人知晓的私密空间、私密活动、私密信息。

<a id="art-1033"></a>第一千零三十三条　除法律另有规定或者权利人明确同意外，任何组织或者个人不得实施下列行为：  
（一）以电话、短信、即时通讯工具、电子邮件、传单等方式侵扰他人的私人生活安宁；  
（二）进入、拍摄、窥视他人的住宅、宾馆房间等私密空间；  
（三）拍摄、窥视、窃听、公开他人的私密活动；  
（四）拍摄、窥视他人身体的私密部位；  
（五）处理他人的私密信息；  
（六）以其他方式侵害他人的隐私权。

<a id="art-1034"></a>第一千零三十四条　自然人的个人信息受法律保护。  
个人信息是以电子或者其他方式记录的能够单独或者与其他信息结合识别特定自然人的各种信息，包括自然人的姓名、出生日期、身份证件号码、生物识别信息、住址、电话号码、电子邮箱、健康信息、行踪信息等。  
个人信息中的私密信息，适用有关隐私权的规定；没有规定的，适用有关个人信息保护的规定。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理使用人脸识别技术处理个人信息相关民事案件适用法律若干问题的规定.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理使用人脸识别技术处理个人信息相关民事案件适用法律若干问题的规定》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-1035"></a>第一千零三十五条　处理个人信息的，应当遵循合法、正当、必要原则，不得过度处理，并符合下列条件：  
（一）征得该自然人或者其监护人同意，但是法律、行政法规另有规定的除外；  
（二）公开处理信息的规则；  
（三）明示处理信息的目的、方式和范围；  
（四）不违反法律、行政法规的规定和双方的约定。  
个人信息的处理包括个人信息的收集、存储、使用、加工、传输、提供、公开等。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理使用人脸识别技术处理个人信息相关民事案件适用法律若干问题的规定.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理使用人脸识别技术处理个人信息相关民事案件适用法律若干问题的规定》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-1036"></a>第一千零三十六条　处理个人信息，有下列情形之一的，行为人不承担民事责任：  
（一）在该自然人或者其监护人同意的范围内合理实施的行为；  
（二）合理处理该自然人自行公开的或者其他已经合法公开的信息，但是该自然人明确拒绝或者处理该信息侵害其重大利益的除外；  
（三）为维护公共利益或者该自然人合法权益，合理实施的其他行为。

<a id="art-1037"></a>第一千零三十七条　自然人可以依法向信息处理者查阅或者复制其个人信息；发现信息有错误的，有权提出异议并请求及时采取更正等必要措施。  
自然人发现信息处理者违反法律、行政法规的规定或者双方的约定处理其个人信息的，有权请求信息处理者及时删除。

<a id="art-1038"></a>第一千零三十八条　信息处理者不得泄露或者篡改其收集、存储的个人信息；未经自然人同意，不得向他人非法提供其个人信息，但是经过加工无法识别特定个人且不能复原的除外。  
信息处理者应当采取技术措施和其他必要措施，确保其收集、存储的个人信息安全，防止信息泄露、篡改、丢失；发生或者可能发生个人信息泄露、篡改、丢失的，应当及时采取补救措施，按照规定告知自然人并向有关主管部门报告。

<a id="art-1039"></a>第一千零三十九条　国家机关、承担行政职能的法定机构及其工作人员对于履行职责过程中知悉的自然人的隐私和个人信息，应当予以保密，不得泄露或者向他人非法提供。

## 第五编　婚姻家庭

### 第一章　一般规定

<a id="art-1040"></a>第一千零四十条　本编调整因婚姻家庭产生的民事关系。

<a id="art-1041"></a>第一千零四十一条　婚姻家庭受国家保护。  
实行婚姻自由、一夫一妻、男女平等的婚姻制度。  
保护妇女、未成年人、老年人、残疾人的合法权益。

<a id="art-1042"></a>第一千零四十二条　禁止包办、买卖婚姻和其他干涉婚姻自由的行为。禁止借婚姻索取财物。  
禁止重婚。禁止有配偶者与他人同居。  
禁止家庭暴力。禁止家庭成员间的虐待和遗弃。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第1条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-2" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第2条引用">[2]</a></sup>

<a id="art-1043"></a>第一千零四十三条　家庭应当树立优良家风，弘扬家庭美德，重视家庭文明建设。  
夫妻应当互相忠实，互相尊重，互相关爱；家庭成员应当敬老爱幼，互相帮助，维护平等、和睦、文明的婚姻家庭关系。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第4条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-1044"></a>第一千零四十四条　收养应当遵循最有利于被收养人的原则，保障被收养人和收养人的合法权益。  
禁止借收养名义买卖未成年人。

<a id="art-1045"></a>第一千零四十五条　亲属包括配偶、血亲和姻亲。  
配偶、父母、子女、兄弟姐妹、祖父母、外祖父母、孙子女、外孙子女为近亲属。  
配偶、父母、子女和其他共同生活的近亲属为家庭成员。

### 第二章　结婚

<a id="art-1046"></a>第一千零四十六条　结婚应当男女双方完全自愿，禁止任何一方对另一方加以强迫，禁止任何组织或者个人加以干涉。

<a id="art-1047"></a>第一千零四十七条　结婚年龄，男不得早于二十二周岁，女不得早于二十周岁。

<a id="art-1048"></a>第一千零四十八条　直系血亲或者三代以内的旁系血亲禁止结婚。

<a id="art-1049"></a>第一千零四十九条　要求结婚的男女双方应当亲自到婚姻登记机关申请结婚登记。符合本法规定的，予以登记，发给结婚证。完成结婚登记，即确立婚姻关系。未办理结婚登记的，应当补办登记。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-6" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第6条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第7条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-8" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第8条引用">[3]</a></sup>

<a id="art-1050"></a>第一千零五十条　登记结婚后，按照男女双方约定，女方可以成为男方家庭的成员，男方可以成为女方家庭的成员。

<a id="art-1051"></a>第一千零五十一条　有下列情形之一的，婚姻无效：  
（一）重婚；  
（二）有禁止结婚的亲属关系；  
（三）未到法定婚龄。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-9" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第9条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-10" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第10条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-14" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第14条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-15" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第15条引用">[4]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第17条引用">[5]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）.md#art-1" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（二）》第1条引用">[6]</a></sup>

<a id="art-1052"></a>第一千零五十二条　因胁迫结婚的，受胁迫的一方可以向人民法院请求撤销婚姻。  
请求撤销婚姻的，应当自胁迫行为终止之日起一年内提出。  
被非法限制人身自由的当事人请求撤销婚姻的，应当自恢复人身自由之日起一年内提出。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-18" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第18条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》婚姻家庭编的解释（一）》第19条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》时间效力的若干规定.md#art-26" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》时间效力的若干规定》第26条引用">[3]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/婚姻、家庭与继承/婚姻登记条例.md#art-12" title="被《婚姻登记条例》第12条引用">[4]</a></sup>

<a id="art-1053"></a>第一千零五十三条　一方患有重大疾病的，应当在结婚登记前如实告知另一方；不如实告知的，另一方可以向人民法院请求撤销婚姻。  
请求撤销婚姻的，应当自知道或者应当知道撤销事由之日起一年内提出。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/婚姻、家庭与继承/婚姻登记条例.md#art-12" title="被《婚姻登记条例》第12条引用">[1]</a></sup>
