After the tortfeasor pays compensation, it shall have the right to seek recourse against the third party.

<a id="art-1234"></a>第一千二百三十四条　违反国家规定造成生态环境损害，生态环境能够修复的，国家规定的机关或者法律规定的组织有权请求侵权人在合理期限内承担修复责任。侵权人在期限内未修复的，国家规定的机关或者法律规定的组织可以自行或者委托他人进行修复，所需费用由侵权人负担。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理森林资源民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-17" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理森林资源民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第17条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1234** Where damage is caused to the ecological environment in violation of state regulations, if the ecological environment can be remedied, the authority designated by the state or the organization provided for by law shall have the right to request the tortfeasor to assume the remediation liability within a reasonable time limit. If the tortfeasor fails to remedy the ecological environment within the time limit, the said authority or organization may remedy it on its own or entrust others to do so, and the necessary expenses shall be borne by the tortfeasor.

<a id="art-1235"></a>第一千二百三十五条　违反国家规定造成生态环境损害的，国家规定的机关或者法律规定的组织有权请求侵权人赔偿下列损失和费用：  

**Article 1235** Where damage is caused to the ecological environment in violation of state regulations, the authority designated by the state or the organization provided for by law shall have the right to request the tortfeasor to compensate for the following losses and expenses:
(1) Losses resulting from the loss of service function during the period from the occurrence of the ecological environmental damage to its remedy;
(2) Losses caused by permanent damage to the ecological environment function;
(3) Expenses for investigation, identification, and assessment of the ecological environmental damage;
(4) Expenses for pollution removal and ecological environmental remediation; and
(5) Reasonable expenses incurred to prevent the occurrence and spread of the damage.
（一）生态环境受到损害至修复完成期间服务功能丧失导致的损失；  
（二）生态环境功能永久性损害造成的损失；  
（三）生态环境损害调查、鉴定评估等费用；  
（四）清除污染、修复生态环境费用；  
（五）防止损害的发生和扩大所支出的合理费用。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理森林资源民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理森林资源民事纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第19条引用">[1]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理生态环境侵权责任纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-22" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理生态环境侵权责任纠纷案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第22条引用">[2]</a></sup>&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../诉讼与司法程序/民事程序解释/最高人民法院关于生态环境侵权民事诉讼证据的若干规定.md#art-4" title="被《最高人民法院关于生态环境侵权民事诉讼证据的若干规定》第4条引用">[3]</a></sup>

### 第八章　高度危险责任

<a id="art-1236"></a>第一千二百三十六条　从事高度危险作业造成他人损害的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1236** Where damage is caused to another person by engaging in highly hazardous operations, tort liability shall be borne.

<a id="art-1237"></a>第一千二百三十七条　民用核设施或者运入运出核设施的核材料发生核事故造成他人损害的，民用核设施的营运单位应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因战争、武装冲突、暴乱等情形或者受害人故意造成的，不承担责任。

**Article 1237** Where damage is caused to another person by a nuclear accident at a civil nuclear facility or involving nuclear materials transported into or out of such facility, the operator of the civil nuclear facility shall bear tort liability; provided, however, that it shall not bear liability if it can prove that the damage was caused by war, armed conflict, riot, or similar circumstances, or was caused intentionally by the victim.

<a id="art-1238"></a>第一千二百三十八条　民用航空器造成他人损害的，民用航空器的经营者应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因受害人故意造成的，不承担责任。

**Article 1238** Where damage is caused to another person by a civil aircraft, the operator of the civil aircraft shall bear tort liability; provided, however, that it shall not bear liability if it can prove that the damage was caused intentionally by the victim.

<a id="art-1239"></a>第一千二百三十九条　占有或者使用易燃、易爆、剧毒、高放射性、强腐蚀性、高致病性等高度危险物造成他人损害的，占有人或者使用人应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因受害人故意或者不可抗力造成的，不承担责任。被侵权人对损害的发生有重大过失的，可以减轻占有人或者使用人的责任。

**Article 1239** Where damage is caused to another person by the possession or use of highly hazardous substances such as flammable, explosive, highly toxic, highly radioactive, strongly corrosive, or highly pathogenic substances, the possessor or user shall bear tort liability; provided, however, that it shall not bear liability if it can prove that the damage was caused intentionally by the victim or by force majeure. If the aggrieved person is grossly negligent in causing the damage, the liability of the possessor or user may be mitigated.

<a id="art-1240"></a>第一千二百四十条　从事高空、高压、地下挖掘活动或者使用高速轨道运输工具造成他人损害的，经营者应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因受害人故意或者不可抗力造成的，不承担责任。被侵权人对损害的发生有重大过失的，可以减轻经营者的责任。

**Article 1240** Where damage is caused to another person by engaging in operations at highly elevated or high-pressure areas, underground excavation activities, or by using high-speed rail transport, the operator shall bear tort liability; provided, however, that it shall not bear liability if it can prove that the damage was caused intentionally by the victim or by force majeure. If the aggrieved person is grossly negligent in causing the damage, the liability of the operator may be mitigated.

<a id="art-1241"></a>第一千二百四十一条　遗失、抛弃高度危险物造成他人损害的，由所有人承担侵权责任。所有人将高度危险物交由他人管理的，由管理人承担侵权责任；所有人有过错的，与管理人承担连带责任。

**Article 1241** If any loss or harm is caused to any other person due to the loss or abandonment of a highly hazardous substance, the owner shall bear tort liability. If the owner has placed the highly hazardous substance under the management of another person, the manager shall bear tort liability; if the owner is at fault, the owner and the manager shall bear joint and several liability.

<a id="art-1242"></a>第一千二百四十二条　非法占有高度危险物造成他人损害的，由非法占有人承担侵权责任。所有人、管理人不能证明对防止非法占有尽到高度注意义务的，与非法占有人承担连带责任。

**Article 1242** If any loss or harm is caused to any other person due to the illegal possession of a highly hazardous substance, the illegal possessor shall bear tort liability. If the owner or manager is unable to prove that it has exercised a high degree of care to prevent such illegal possession, the owner or manager shall bear joint and several liability with the illegal possessor.

<a id="art-1243"></a>第一千二百四十三条　未经许可进入高度危险活动区域或者高度危险物存放区域受到损害，管理人能够证明已经采取足够安全措施并尽到充分警示义务的，可以减轻或者不承担责任。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/综合与程序批复/最高人民法院关于审理道路交通事故损害赔偿案件适用法律若干问题的解释.md#art-7" title="被《最高人民法院关于审理道路交通事故损害赔偿案件适用法律若干问题的解释》第7条引用">[1]</a></sup>

**Article 1243** If any person suffers harm from entering, without permission, an area where highly hazardous activities are conducted or highly hazardous substances are stored, and the manager is able to prove that it has taken sufficient safety measures and fulfilled its duty to give adequate warnings, the manager's liability may be mitigated or exempted.

<a id="art-1244"></a>第一千二百四十四条　承担高度危险责任，法律规定赔偿限额的，依照其规定，但是行为人有故意或者重大过失的除外。

**Article 1244** Where any person bears liability for ultrahazardous activities, if the law provides for a limit on compensation, such provisions shall apply, except where the actor has acted intentionally or with gross negligence.

### 第九章　饲养动物损害责任

<a id="art-1245"></a>第一千二百四十五条　饲养的动物造成他人损害的，动物饲养人或者管理人应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因被侵权人故意或者重大过失造成的，可以不承担或者减轻责任。

**Article 1245** If any domesticated animal causes harm to any other person, the animal keeper or manager shall bear tort liability; however, if it can be proved that the harm was caused by the intentional act or gross negligence of the infringed person, the liability may be exempted or mitigated.

<a id="art-1246"></a>第一千二百四十六条　违反管理规定，未对动物采取安全措施造成他人损害的，动物饲养人或者管理人应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明损害是因被侵权人故意造成的，可以减轻责任。

**Article 1246** If any person violates management regulations by failing to take safety measures with respect to an animal and thereby causes harm to any other person, the animal keeper or manager shall bear tort liability; however, if it can be proved that the harm was intentionally caused by the infringed person, the liability may be mitigated.

<a id="art-1247"></a>第一千二百四十七条　禁止饲养的烈性犬等危险动物造成他人损害的，动物饲养人或者管理人应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1247** If any dangerous animal, such as a ferocious dog whose keeping is prohibited, causes harm to any other person, the animal keeper or manager shall bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1248"></a>第一千二百四十八条　动物园的动物造成他人损害的，动物园应当承担侵权责任；但是，能够证明尽到管理职责的，不承担侵权责任。

**Article 1248** If any animal in a zoo causes harm to any other person, the zoo shall bear tort liability; however, if it can be proved that it has fulfilled its management duties, it shall not bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1249"></a>第一千二百四十九条　遗弃、逃逸的动物在遗弃、逃逸期间造成他人损害的，由动物原饲养人或者管理人承担侵权责任。

**Article 1249** If any abandoned or escaped animal causes harm to any other person during the period of abandonment or escape, the original keeper or manager of the animal shall bear tort liability.

<a id="art-1250"></a>第一千二百五十条　因第三人的过错致使动物造成他人损害的，被侵权人可以向动物饲养人或者管理人请求赔偿，也可以向第三人请求赔偿。动物饲养人或者管理人赔偿后，有权向第三人追偿。

**Article 1250** If an animal causes harm to any other person due to the fault of a third party, the infringed person may claim compensation from either the animal keeper or manager or from the third party. After the animal keeper or manager has paid compensation, it shall have the right to seek recourse from the third party.

<a id="art-1251"></a>第一千二百五十一条　饲养动物应当遵守法律法规，尊重社会公德，不得妨碍他人生活。

**Article 1251** The keeping of animals shall comply with laws and regulations, respect social ethics, and shall not interfere with the life of others.

### 第十章　建筑物和物件损害责任

<a id="art-1252"></a>第一千二百五十二条　建筑物、构筑物或者其他设施倒塌、塌陷造成他人损害的，由建设单位与施工单位承担连带责任，但是建设单位与施工单位能够证明不存在质量缺陷的除外。建设单位、施工单位赔偿后，有其他责任人的，有权向其他责任人追偿。  

**Article 1252** If a building, structure, or other facility collapses or subsides and causes harm to any other person, the development project owner and the construction unit shall bear joint and several liability, except where the development project owner and the construction unit are able to prove that there is no quality defect. After the development project owner and the construction unit have paid compensation, if there are other persons responsible, they shall have the right to seek recourse from such other persons.
If a building, structure, or other facility collapses or subsides due to reasons attributable to the owner, manager, user, or a third party, thereby causing harm to any other person, the owner, manager, user, or third party shall bear tort liability.
因所有人、管理人、使用人或者第三人的原因，建筑物、构筑物或者其他设施倒塌、塌陷造成他人损害的，由所有人、管理人、使用人或者第三人承担侵权责任。

<a id="art-1253"></a>第一千二百五十三条　建筑物、构筑物或者其他设施及其搁置物、悬挂物发生脱落、坠落造成他人损害，所有人、管理人或者使用人不能证明自己没有过错的，应当承担侵权责任。所有人、管理人或者使用人赔偿后，有其他责任人的，有权向其他责任人追偿。

**Article 1253** If an object that is detached or falls from a building, structure, or other facility, including any object placed or hung thereon, causes harm to any other person, the owner, manager, or user shall bear tort liability unless it can prove that it is not at fault. After the owner, manager, or user has paid compensation, if there are other persons responsible, it shall have the right to seek recourse from such other persons.

<a id="art-1254"></a>第一千二百五十四条　禁止从建筑物中抛掷物品。从建筑物中抛掷物品或者从建筑物上坠落的物品造成他人损害的，由侵权人依法承担侵权责任；经调查难以确定具体侵权人的，除能够证明自己不是侵权人的外，由可能加害的建筑物使用人给予补偿。可能加害的建筑物使用人补偿后，有权向侵权人追偿。  

**Article 1254** The throwing of objects from a building is prohibited. If any object thrown from a building or falling from a building causes harm to any other person, the tortfeasor shall bear tort liability in accordance with law. If, after investigation, it is difficult to identify the specific tortfeasor, the users of the building who are likely to have caused the harm shall provide compensation, unless they can prove that they are not the tortfeasor. After having provided compensation, the users of the building who are likely to have caused the harm shall have the right to seek recourse from the tortfeasor.
Property service enterprises and other building managers shall take necessary security measures to prevent the occurrence of the circumstances described in the preceding paragraph; if they fail to take necessary security measures, they shall bear tort liability for failing to fulfill safety guarantee obligations in accordance with law.
In the event of the circumstances set forth in the first paragraph of this Article, the public security authorities and other relevant authorities shall investigate promptly in accordance with law to ascertain the person responsible.
物业服务企业等建筑物管理人应当采取必要的安全保障措施防止前款规定情形的发生；未采取必要的安全保障措施的，应当依法承担未履行安全保障义务的侵权责任。  
发生本条第一款规定的情形的，公安等机关应当依法及时调查，查清责任人。&thinsp;<sup><a href="../../民事与商事/民法典/最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》时间效力的若干规定.md#art-19" title="被《最高人民法院关于适用《中华人民共和国民法典》时间效力的若干规定》第19条引用">[1]</a></sup>

<a id="art-1255"></a>第一千二百五十五条　堆放物倒塌、滚落或者滑落造成他人损害，堆放人不能证明自己没有过错的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1255** If any piled object collapses, rolls, or slides and causes harm to any other person, the person who piled the object shall bear tort liability unless it can prove that it is not at fault.

<a id="art-1256"></a>第一千二百五十六条　在公共道路上堆放、倾倒、遗撒妨碍通行的物品造成他人损害的，由行为人承担侵权责任。公共道路管理人不能证明已经尽到清理、防护、警示等义务的，应当承担相应的责任。

**Article 1256** If any person stacks, dumps, or spills objects that obstruct traffic on a public road, thereby causing harm to any other person, the person who committed the act shall bear tort liability. If the manager of the public road is unable to prove that it has fulfilled its obligations of cleaning, protection, warning, or the like, it shall bear corresponding liability.

<a id="art-1257"></a>第一千二百五十七条　因林木折断、倾倒或者果实坠落等造成他人损害，林木的所有人或者管理人不能证明自己没有过错的，应当承担侵权责任。

**Article 1257** If any harm is caused to any other person due to the breaking, toppling, or falling of fruits of trees or the like, the owner or manager of the tree shall bear tort liability unless it can prove that it is not at fault.

<a id="art-1258"></a>第一千二百五十八条　在公共场所或者道路上挖掘、修缮安装地下设施等造成他人损害，施工人不能证明已经设置明显标志和采取安全措施的，应当承担侵权责任。  

**Article 1258** If any person excavates, repairs, or installs underground facilities or the like in a public place or on a road, thereby causing harm to any other person, the person carrying out the construction shall bear tort liability unless it can prove that it has set up clear signs and taken safety measures.
If any underground facility such as a manhole cover causes harm to any other person, the manager shall bear tort liability unless it can prove that it has fulfilled its management duties.
Supplementary Provisions
窨井等地下设施造成他人损害，管理人不能证明尽到管理职责的，应当承担侵权责任。  
附　　则

<a id="art-1259"></a>第一千二百五十九条　民法所称的“以上”、“以下”、“以内”、“届满”，包括本数；所称的“不满”、“超过”、“以外”，不包括本数。

**Article 1259** The terms “or above,” “or below,” “within,” and “expiration of” as used in civil law include the number itself; the terms “less than,” “more than,” and “outside,” as used in civil law do not include the number itself.

<a id="art-1260"></a>第一千二百六十条　本法自2021年1月1日起施行。《中华人民共和国婚姻法》、《中华人民共和国继承法》、《中华人民共和国民法通则》、《中华人民共和国收养法》、《中华人民共和国担保法》、《中华人民共和国合同法》、《中华人民共和国物权法》、《中华人民共和国侵权责任法》、《中华人民共和国民法总则》同时废止。

**Article 1260** This Law shall come into force as of January 1, 2021. The Marriage Law of the People's Republic of China, the Law of Succession of the People's Republic of China, the General Principles of the Civil Law of the People's Republic of China, the Adoption Law of the People's Republic of China, the Security Law of the People's Republic of China, the Contract Law of the People's Republic of China, the Property Law of the People's Republic of China, the Tort Liability Law of the People's Republic of China, and the General Provisions of the Civil Law of the People's Republic of China shall be repealed simultaneously.
